1→"""Canonical queue snapshot for phase and visibility decisions."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Any, Iterable
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (
    10→    WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
    11→    WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import path_scoped_issues
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import build_issue_items
    16→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic import (
    17→    build_subjective_items,
    18→    build_triage_stage_items,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic_workflow import (
    21→    build_communicate_score_item,
    22→    build_create_plan_item,
    23→    build_deferred_disposition_item,
    24→    build_import_scores_item,
    25→    build_run_scan_item,
    26→    build_score_checkpoint_item,
    27→)
    28→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    29→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    30→
    31→PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL = "review_initial"
    32→PHASE_EXECUTE = "execute"
    33→PHASE_SCAN = "scan"
    34→PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT = "review_postflight"
    35→PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT = "workflow_postflight"
    36→PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT = "triage_postflight"
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    40→class QueueSnapshot:
    41→    """Canonical queue facts and partitions for one invocation."""
    42→
    43→    phase: str
    44→    all_objective_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    45→    all_initial_review_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    46→    all_postflight_review_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    47→    all_scan_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    48→    all_postflight_workflow_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    49→    all_postflight_triage_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    50→    execution_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    51→    backlog_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    52→    objective_in_scope_count: int
    53→    objective_execution_count: int
    54→    objective_backlog_count: int
    55→    subjective_initial_count: int
    56→    subjective_postflight_count: int
    57→    workflow_postflight_count: int
    58→    triage_pending_count: int
    59→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers: bool
    60→
    61→
    62→def _option_value(options: object | None, name: str, default: Any) -> Any:
    63→    if options is None:
    64→        return default
    65→    return getattr(options, name, default)
    66→
    67→
    68→def _resolved_scan_path(options: object | None, state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    69→    scan_path = _option_value(options, "scan_path", state.get("scan_path"))
    70→    if hasattr(scan_path, "__class__") and scan_path.__class__.__name__ == "_ScanPathFromState":
    71→        return state.get("scan_path")
    72→    return scan_path
    73→
    74→
    75→def _tracked_plan_ids(plan: dict | None) -> set[str]:
    76→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
    77→        return set()
    78→    tracked: set[str] = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    79→    tracked.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    80→    tracked.update(plan.get("overrides", {}).keys())
    81→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    82→        tracked.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    83→        for step in cluster.get("action_steps", []):
    84→            if isinstance(step, dict):
    85→                tracked.update(step.get("issue_refs", []))
    86→    return tracked
    87→
    88→
    89→def _is_fresh_boundary(plan: dict | None) -> bool:
    90→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
    91→        return True
    92→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    93→    if not scores:
    94→        return True
    95→    return isinstance(scores, dict) and bool(scores.get("reset"))
    96→
    97→
    98→def _is_objective_item(item: WorkQueueItem, *, skipped_ids: set[str]) -> bool:
    99→    return (
   100→        item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
   101→        and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   102→        and item.get("id", "") not in skipped_ids
   103→    )
   104→
   105→
   106→def _review_issue_items(items: Iterable[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   107→    return [
   108→        item for item in items
   109→        if item.get("detector", "") in {"review", "concerns", "subjective_review"}
   110→    ]
   111→
   112→
   113→def _subjective_partitions(
   114→    state: StateModel,
   115→    *,
   116→    scoped_issues: dict[str, dict],
   117→    threshold: float,
   118→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   119→    candidates = build_subjective_items(state, scoped_issues, threshold=threshold)
   120→    initial = [item for item in candidates if item.get("initial_review")]
   121→    postflight = [item for item in candidates if not item.get("initial_review")]
   122→    return initial, postflight
   123→
   124→
   125→def _workflow_partitions(
   126→    plan: dict | None,
   127→    state: StateModel,
   128→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   129→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   130→        return [], [], []
   131→    scan_items = [
   132→        item
   133→        for item in (
   134→            build_deferred_disposition_item(plan),
   135→            build_run_scan_item(plan),
   136→        )
   137→        if item is not None
   138→    ]
   139→    postflight_workflow = [
   140→        item
   141→        for item in (
   142→            build_score_checkpoint_item(plan, state),
   143→            build_import_scores_item(plan, state),
   144→            build_communicate_score_item(plan, state),
   145→            build_create_plan_item(plan),
   146→        )
   147→        if item is not None
   148→    ]
   149→    triage_items = build_triage_stage_items(plan, state)
   150→    return scan_items, postflight_workflow, triage_items
   151→
   152→
   153→def _phase_for_snapshot(
   154→    *,
   155→    fresh_boundary: bool,
   156→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   157→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   158→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   159→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   160→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   161→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   162→) -> str:
   163→    if fresh_boundary and initial_review_items:
   164→        return PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL
   165→    if objective_items:
   166→        return PHASE_EXECUTE
   167→    if scan_items:
   168→        return PHASE_SCAN
   169→    if postflight_review_items:
   170→        return PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT
   171→    if postflight_workflow_items:
   172→        return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   173→    if triage_items:
   174→        return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   175→    return PHASE_SCAN
   176→
   177→
   178→def _execution_items_for_phase(
   179→    phase: str,
   180→    *,
   181→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   182→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   183→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   184→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   185→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   186→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   187→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   188→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL:
   189→        return initial_review_items
   190→    if phase == PHASE_EXECUTE:
   191→        return objective_items
   192→    if phase == PHASE_SCAN:
   193→        deferred_items = [
   194→            item for item in scan_items
   195→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID
   196→        ]
   197→        if deferred_items:
   198→            return deferred_items
   199→        return [
   200→            item for item in scan_items
   201→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID
   202→        ]
   203→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   204→        return postflight_review_items
   205→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   206→        return postflight_workflow_items
   207→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   208→        return triage_items
   209→    return []
   210→
   211→
   212→def build_queue_snapshot(
   213→    state: StateModel,
   214→    *,
   215→    options: object | None = None,
   216→    plan: dict | None = None,
   217→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   218→) -> QueueSnapshot:
   219→    """Build the canonical queue snapshot for the current state."""
   220→    context = _option_value(options, "context", None)
   221→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else (
   222→        plan if plan is not None else _option_value(options, "plan", None)
   223→    )
   224→    scan_path = _resolved_scan_path(options, state)
   225→    skipped_ids = set((effective_plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   226→    tracked_ids = _tracked_plan_ids(effective_plan)
   227→    scoped_issues = path_scoped_issues(state.get("issues", {}), scan_path)
   228→    scope = _option_value(options, "scope", None)
   229→    chronic = bool(_option_value(options, "chronic", False))
   230→
   231→    all_issue_items = build_issue_items(
   232→        state,
   233→        scan_path=scan_path,
   234→        status_filter="open",
   235→        scope=scope,
   236→        chronic=chronic,
   237→    )
   238→    objective_items = [
   239→        item for item in all_issue_items
   240→        if _is_objective_item(item, skipped_ids=skipped_ids)
   241→    ]
   242→    review_issue_items = _review_issue_items(all_issue_items)
   243→    initial_review_items, subjective_postflight_items = _subjective_partitions(
   244→        state,
   245→        scoped_issues=scoped_issues,
   246→        threshold=target_strict,
   247→    )
   248→    postflight_review_items = [*subjective_postflight_items, *review_issue_items]
   249→    scan_items, postflight_workflow_items, triage_items = _workflow_partitions(
   250→        effective_plan,
   251→        state,
   252→    )
   253→
   254→    fresh_boundary = _is_fresh_boundary(effective_plan)
   255→    phase = _phase_for_snapshot(
   256→        fresh_boundary=fresh_boundary,
   257→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   258→        objective_items=objective_items,
   259→        scan_items=scan_items,
   260→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   261→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   262→        triage_items=triage_items,
   263→    )
   264→    execution_items = _execution_items_for_phase(
   265→        phase,
   266→        objective_items=objective_items,
   267→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   268→        scan_items=scan_items,
   269→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   270→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   271→        triage_items=triage_items,
   272→    )
   273→
   274→    execution_ids = {item.get("id", "") for item in execution_items}
   275→    backlog_items = [
   276→        item for item in (
   277→            [*objective_items, *initial_review_items, *postflight_review_items, *scan_items, *postflight_workflow_items, *triage_items]
   278→        )
   279→        if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   280→    ]
   281→    objective_backlog_count = sum(
   282→        1 for item in objective_items if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   283→    )
   284→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers = any(
   285→        item.get("id", "") not in tracked_ids for item in objective_items
   286→    )
   287→
   288→    return QueueSnapshot(
   289→        phase=phase,
   290→        all_objective_items=tuple(objective_items),
   291→        all_initial_review_items=tuple(initial_review_items),
   292→        all_postflight_review_items=tuple(postflight_review_items),
   293→        all_scan_items=tuple(scan_items),
   294→        all_postflight_workflow_items=tuple(postflight_workflow_items),
   295→        all_postflight_triage_items=tuple(triage_items),
   296→        execution_items=tuple(execution_items),
   297→        backlog_items=tuple(backlog_items),
   298→        objective_in_scope_count=len(objective_items),
   299→        objective_execution_count=sum(
   300→            1
   301→            for item in execution_items
   302→            if item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
   303→            and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   304→        ),
   305→        objective_backlog_count=objective_backlog_count,
   306→        subjective_initial_count=len(initial_review_items),
   307→        subjective_postflight_count=len(postflight_review_items),
   308→        workflow_postflight_count=len(postflight_workflow_items),
   309→        triage_pending_count=len(triage_items),
   310→        has_unplanned_objective_blockers=has_unplanned_objective_blockers,
   311→    )
   312→
   313→
   314→def coarse_phase_name(phase: str) -> str:
   315→    """Map internal queue phases to the persisted coarse lifecycle value."""
   316→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL or phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   317→        return "review"
   318→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   319→        return "workflow"
   320→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   321→        return "triage"
   322→    return phase
   323→
   324→
   325→__all__ = [
   326→    "PHASE_EXECUTE",
   327→    "PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL",
   328→    "PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT",
   329→    "PHASE_SCAN",
   330→    "PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT",
   331→    "PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT",
   332→    "QueueSnapshot",
   333→    "build_queue_snapshot",
   334→    "coarse_phase_name",
   335→]
   336→
```

1→"""Unified work-queue entrypoints for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import QueueContext
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.finalize import finalize_queue
     7→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.inputs import resolve_queue_inputs
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.models import (
    10→    QueueBuildOptions,
    11→    QueueVisibility,
    12→    ScanPathOption,
    13→    WorkQueueResult,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    16→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import group_queue_items
    17→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.selection import select_queue_items
    18→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    19→
    20→def build_work_queue(
    21→    state: StateModel,
    22→    *,
    23→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    24→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    25→    """Build the active canonical work queue for the current invocation."""
    26→    return _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    27→        state,
    28→        options=options,
    29→        visibility=QueueVisibility.ALL,
    30→    )
    31→
    32→
    33→def _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    34→    state: StateModel,
    35→    *,
    36→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    37→    visibility: str = QueueVisibility.ALL,
    38→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    39→    """Build a ranked work queue from canonical queue partitions.
    40→
    41→    Open-status queue views are resolved from the canonical queue snapshot so
    42→    lifecycle phase, execution visibility, and backlog visibility all come
    43→    from one source of truth. Non-open status views remain issue-list queries.
    44→    """
    45→    opts = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    46→    plan, scan_path, status, threshold = resolve_queue_inputs(opts, state)
    47→    items = select_queue_items(
    48→        state,
    49→        opts=opts,
    50→        plan=plan,
    51→        scan_path=scan_path,
    52→        status=status,
    53→        threshold=threshold,
    54→        visibility=visibility,
    55→    )
    56→    return finalize_queue(items, state=state, plan=plan, opts=opts)
    57→
    58→__all__ = [
    59→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
    60→    "QueueBuildOptions",
    61→    "QueueContext",
    62→    "QueueVisibility",
    63→    "WorkQueueResult",
    64→    "_build_work_queue_with_visibility",
    65→    "build_work_queue",
    66→    "collapse_clusters",
    67→    "group_queue_items",
    68→]
    69→

1→"""Queue item selection from canonical snapshot or raw issue lists."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import scope_matches
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.inputs import gather_subjective_items
     7→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.models import QueueBuildOptions, QueueVisibility
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import build_issue_items
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.snapshot import build_queue_snapshot
    10→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    12→
    13→
    14→def select_queue_items(
    15→    state: StateModel,
    16→    *,
    17→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    18→    plan: dict | None,
    19→    scan_path: str | None,
    20→    status: str,
    21→    threshold: float,
    22→    visibility: str,
    23→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    24→    """Select the raw queue item list before ranking/finalization."""
    25→    if status == "open" and not opts.chronic:
    26→        snapshot = build_queue_snapshot(
    27→            state,
    28→            options=opts,
    29→            plan=plan,
    30→            target_strict=threshold,
    31→        )
    32→        return filter_snapshot_items(
    33→            items_for_visibility(snapshot=snapshot, visibility=visibility),
    34→            opts,
    35→        )
    36→
    37→    items = build_issue_items(
    38→        state,
    39→        scan_path=scan_path,
    40→        status_filter=status,
    41→        scope=opts.scope,
    42→        chronic=opts.chronic,
    43→    )
    44→    if opts.include_subjective and status == "all":
    45→        items += gather_subjective_items(state, opts, threshold)
    46→    return items
    47→
    48→
    49→def items_for_visibility(*, snapshot, visibility: str) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    50→    """Select the snapshot partition for one queue surface."""
    51→    if visibility == QueueVisibility.BACKLOG:
    52→        return [dict(item) for item in snapshot.backlog_items]
    53→    return [dict(item) for item in snapshot.execution_items]
    54→
    55→
    56→def filter_snapshot_items(
    57→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    58→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    59→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    60→    """Apply view-local filtering after snapshot partition selection."""
    61→    filtered = items
    62→    if not opts.include_subjective:
    63→        filtered = [
    64→            item for item in filtered
    65→            if item.get("kind") != "subjective_dimension"
    66→        ]
    67→    if opts.scope:
    68→        filtered = [
    69→            item for item in filtered
    70→            if item.get("kind") != "subjective_dimension" or scope_matches(item, opts.scope)
    71→        ]
    72→    return filtered
    73→
    74→
    75→__all__ = [
    76→    "filter_snapshot_items",
    77→    "items_for_visibility",
    78→    "select_queue_items",
    79→]
    80→

1→"""Unified queue-resolution context.
     2→
     3→A frozen ``QueueContext`` computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``plan`` / ``target_strict`` / ``policy`` threading through function chains.
     5→Callers build one context and pass it everywhere — makes the wrong thing
     6→impossible.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from dataclasses import dataclass
    12→
    13→from desloppify.base.config import (
    14→    DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    15→    target_strict_score_from_config,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.persistence import (
    18→    has_living_plan,
    19→    load_plan,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import PlanLoadStatus
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.snapshot import QueueSnapshot, build_queue_snapshot
    24→
    25→
    26→class _PlanAutoLoad:
    27→    """Sentinel type: auto-load plan from disk."""
    28→
    29→
    30→# Sentinel: "auto-load plan from disk" (the default).
    31→_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD = _PlanAutoLoad()
    32→PlanOption = dict | None | _PlanAutoLoad
    33→
    34→
    35→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    36→class QueueContext:
    37→    """Immutable snapshot of resolved queue parameters.
    38→
    39→    Built once via :func:`queue_context`, then threaded through queue
    40→    builders and command helpers so every call site agrees on plan,
    41→    target, and canonical queue facts.
    42→    """
    43→
    44→    plan: dict | None
    45→    target_strict: float
    46→    plan_load_status: PlanLoadStatus
    47→    snapshot: QueueSnapshot
    48→
    49→
    50→def resolve_plan_load_status(
    51→    *,
    52→    plan: PlanOption = _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD,
    53→) -> PlanLoadStatus:
    54→    """Resolve plan loading and explicitly report degraded mode."""
    55→    if not isinstance(plan, _PlanAutoLoad):
    56→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=plan, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    57→
    58→    if not has_living_plan():
    59→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=None, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    60→
    61→    try:
    62→        resolved_plan = load_plan()
    63→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=resolved_plan, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    64→    except OSError as exc:
    65→        return PlanLoadStatus(
    66→            plan=None,
    67→            degraded=True,
    68→            error_kind=exc.__class__.__name__,
    69→        )
    70→    except (
    71→        ValueError,
    72→        UnicodeDecodeError,
    73→    ) as exc:
    74→        return PlanLoadStatus(
    75→            plan=None,
    76→            degraded=True,
    77→            error_kind=exc.__class__.__name__,
    78→        )
    79→
    80→
    81→def queue_context(
    82→    state: StateModel,
    83→    *,
    84→    config: dict | None = None,
    85→    plan: PlanOption = _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD,
    86→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    87→) -> QueueContext:
    88→    """Build a :class:`QueueContext` with all parameters resolved.
    89→
    90→    Resolution order:
    91→
    92→    1. **plan** — explicit value wins; sentinel ``_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD`` triggers
    93→       ``load_plan()`` (guarded by ``PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS``).
    94→    2. **target_strict** — explicit float wins; ``None`` reads from *config*
    95→       via ``target_strict_score_from_config``; final fallback is ``95.0``.
    96→    3. **snapshot** — canonical queue phase and partitions derived from the
    97→       resolved plan and target_strict.
    98→    """
    99→    # --- resolve plan ---
   100→    plan_load_status = resolve_plan_load_status(plan=plan)
   101→    resolved_plan = plan_load_status.plan
   102→
   103→    # --- resolve target_strict ---
   104→    if target_strict is not None:
   105→        resolved_target = target_strict
   106→    elif config is not None:
   107→        resolved_target = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   108→    else:
   109→        resolved_target = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
   110→
   111→    snapshot = build_queue_snapshot(
   112→        state,
   113→        plan=resolved_plan,
   114→        target_strict=resolved_target,
   115→    )
   116→
   117→    return QueueContext(
   118→        plan=resolved_plan,
   119→        target_strict=resolved_target,
   120→        plan_load_status=plan_load_status,
   121→        snapshot=snapshot,
   122→    )
   123→
   124→
   125→__all__ = [
   126→    "PlanLoadStatus",
   127→    "QueueContext",
   128→    "queue_context",
   129→    "resolve_plan_load_status",
   130→]
   131→

1→"""Plan-order and cluster-collapse helpers for work queues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan_ops import (
     9→    get_issue_description,
    10→    get_issue_note,
    11→    get_issue_override,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    14→from desloppify.state_io import StateModel
    15→
    16→
    17→def _cluster_issue_ids(cluster: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
    18→    """Return canonical cluster members after plan-load normalization."""
    19→    issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
    20→    if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
    21→        return []
    22→    return [issue_id for issue_id in issue_ids if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id]
    23→
    24→
    25→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    26→    """Return issue IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    27→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    28→    if not scan_history:
    29→        return set()
    30→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    31→    if not threshold:
    32→        return set()
    33→    return {
    34→        issue_id
    35→        for issue_id, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    36→        if issue.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    37→    }
    38→
    39→
    40→def enrich_plan_metadata(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> None:
    41→    """Stamp plan description, note, and cluster info from overrides."""
    42→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    43→
    44→    for item in items:
    45→        issue_id = item["id"]
    46→        description = get_issue_description(plan, issue_id)
    47→        note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    48→        override = get_issue_override(plan, issue_id)
    49→        if description:
    50→            item["plan_description"] = description
    51→        if note:
    52→            item["plan_note"] = note
    53→        if override.get("cluster"):
    54→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    55→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    56→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    57→                "name": cluster_name,
    58→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    59→                "total_items": len(_cluster_issue_ids(cluster_data)),
    60→                "action_steps": cluster_data.get("action_steps") or [],
    61→            }
    62→
    63→
    64→def stamp_plan_sort_keys(
    65→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    66→    plan: dict,
    67→    new_ids: set[str],
    68→) -> None:
    69→    """Stamp ``_plan_position`` and ``_is_new`` on each item.
    70→
    71→    These fields are consumed by :func:`item_sort_key` in ``ranking.py``
    72→    to produce the correct final ordering in a single sort pass.
    73→    """
    74→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    75→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    76→
    77→    position_map: dict[str, int] = {}
    78→    for idx, issue_id in enumerate(queue_order):
    79→        if issue_id not in skipped_ids:
    80→            position_map[issue_id] = idx
    81→
    82→    for item in items:
    83→        item_id = item["id"]
    84→        pos = position_map.get(item_id)
    85→        item["_plan_position"] = pos  # None if not in queue_order
    86→        item["_is_new"] = item_id in new_ids
    87→
    88→
    89→def separate_skipped(
    90→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    91→    plan: dict,
    92→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
    93→    """Separate skipped items from the main list.
    94→
    95→    Returns ``(non_skipped, skipped)`` so callers can optionally re-append.
    96→    """
    97→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    98→    if not skipped_ids:
    99→        return items, []
   100→    non_skipped: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   101→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   102→    for item in items:
   103→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   104→            skipped.append(item)
   105→        else:
   106→            non_skipped.append(item)
   107→    return non_skipped, skipped
   108→
   109→
   110→def filter_cluster_focus(
   111→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   112→    plan: dict,
   113→    cluster: str | None,
   114→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   115→    """Filter to only cluster members when a cluster focus is active."""
   116→    effective_cluster = cluster or plan.get("active_cluster")
   117→    if not effective_cluster:
   118→        return items
   119→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   120→    cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   121→    cluster_member_ids = set(_cluster_issue_ids(cluster_data))
   122→    if not cluster_member_ids:
   123→        return items
   124→    return [item for item in items if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   125→
   126→
   127→def stamp_positions(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> None:
   128→    """Stamp queue_position and plan_skipped metadata on each item."""
   129→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   130→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   131→
   132→    for position, item in enumerate(items):
   133→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
   134→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   135→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   136→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   137→            if skip_entry:
   138→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   139→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   140→                if skip_reason:
   141→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   142→
   143→
   144→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   145→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   146→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   147→    if meta:
   148→        return meta.action_type
   149→    return "manual_fix"
   150→
   151→
   152→def _build_cluster_meta(
   153→    cluster_name: str, members: list[WorkQueueItem], cluster_data: dict[str, Any]
   154→) -> WorkQueueItem:
   155→    """Build a cluster meta-item from its member items."""
   156→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   157→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   158→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   159→        action_type = "auto_fix"
   160→    elif "desloppify move" in action:
   161→        action_type = "reorganize"
   162→    else:
   163→        action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
   164→        if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify autofix" not in action:
   165→            action_type = "refactor"
   166→
   167→    stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   168→    total_in_cluster = len(_cluster_issue_ids(cluster_data))
   169→    if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   170→        summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   171→    else:
   172→        summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} issues"
   173→
   174→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   175→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   176→        primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   177→        autofix_hint = action
   178→    else:
   179→        primary_command = action or f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   180→        autofix_hint = None
   181→
   182→    estimated_impact = max(
   183→        (m.get("estimated_impact", 0.0) for m in members), default=0.0
   184→    )
   185→
   186→    return {
   187→        "id": cluster_name,
   188→        "kind": "cluster",
   189→        "action_type": action_type,
   190→        "summary": summary,
   191→        "members": members,
   192→        "member_count": len(members),
   193→        "primary_command": primary_command,
   194→        "autofix_hint": autofix_hint,
   195→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
   196→        "cluster_auto": bool(cluster_data.get("auto")),
   197→        "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
   198→        "confidence": "high",
   199→        "detector": detector,
   200→        "file": "",
   201→        "estimated_impact": estimated_impact,
   202→        "action_steps": cluster_data.get("action_steps") or [],
   203→    }
   204→
   205→
   206→def collapse_clusters(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   207→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   208→
   209→    Both auto-clusters and manual (triage) clusters are collapsed.  Manual
   210→    clusters are inserted at the front in plan order so triage-prioritised
   211→    work appears before auto-clustered mechanical items.
   212→    """
   213→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   214→    if not clusters:
   215→        return items
   216→
   217→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   218→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   219→        for issue_id in _cluster_issue_ids(cluster):
   220→            # Manual clusters take priority when an issue is in both
   221→            if issue_id not in fid_to_cluster or not cluster.get("auto"):
   222→                fid_to_cluster[issue_id] = name
   223→
   224→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   225→        return items
   226→
   227→    # Collect members per cluster (preserving encounter order)
   228→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[WorkQueueItem]] = {}
   229→    for item in items:
   230→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   231→        if cname:
   232→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   233→
   234→    # Build meta-items only for clusters with 2+ members in the queue
   235→    meta_items: dict[str, WorkQueueItem] = {}
   236→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   237→        if len(members) < 2:
   238→            continue
   239→        meta_items[cname] = _build_cluster_meta(
   240→            cname, members, clusters.get(cname, {})
   241→        )
   242→
   243→    # Collect manual cluster names in plan order (for front-insertion)
   244→    manual_names = [
   245→        name for name in clusters
   246→        if not clusters[name].get("auto") and name in meta_items
   247→    ]
   248→
   249→    # Walk in order: replace first auto-cluster member with meta-item,
   250→    # skip subsequent members.  Manual cluster members are always skipped
   251→    # (they'll be inserted at the front).
   252→    seen_clusters: set[str] = set()
   253→    rest: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   254→    for item in items:
   255→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   256→        if cname and cname in meta_items:
   257→            if cname not in seen_clusters:
   258→                seen_clusters.add(cname)
   259→                # Auto-clusters collapse in-place; manual clusters go to front
   260→                if clusters.get(cname, {}).get("auto"):
   261→                    rest.append(meta_items[cname])
   262→            # skip individual member
   263→        else:
   264→            rest.append(item)
   265→
   266→    # Manual clusters at the front in plan order, then everything else
   267→    manual_result = [meta_items[name] for name in manual_names if name in seen_clusters]
   268→    return manual_result + rest
   269→
   270→
   271→__all__ = [
   272→    "collapse_clusters",
   273→    "enrich_plan_metadata",
   274→    "filter_cluster_focus",
   275→    "new_item_ids",
   276→    "separate_skipped",
   277→    "stamp_plan_sort_keys",
   278→    "stamp_positions",
   279→]
   280→

1→"""Queue-policy helpers shared by planning render/select modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import replace
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import queue_context
    10→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
    11→    QueueBuildOptions,
    12→    QueueVisibility,
    13→    WorkQueueResult,
    14→    _build_work_queue_with_visibility,
    15→    build_work_queue,
    16→)
    17→
    18→
    19→def _subjective_threshold(state: StateModel, *, default: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE) -> float:
    20→    config = state.get("config", {})
    21→    raw_target = default
    22→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    23→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", default)
    24→    try:
    25→        value = float(raw_target)
    26→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    27→        value = default
    28→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, value))
    29→
    30→
    31→def _queue_plan_from_options(options: QueueBuildOptions) -> dict | None:
    32→    if options.context is not None:
    33→        return options.context.plan
    34→    return options.plan
    35→
    36→
    37→def build_open_plan_queue(
    38→    state: StateModel,
    39→    *,
    40→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    41→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    42→    """Build one open-status queue with consistent planning policy defaults."""
    43→    opts = replace(options) if options is not None else QueueBuildOptions(count=None)
    44→    if options is None:
    45→        opts = replace(opts, status="open", include_subjective=True)
    46→    elif opts.status == QueueBuildOptions().status:
    47→        opts = replace(opts, status="open")
    48→    if opts.subjective_threshold == QueueBuildOptions().subjective_threshold:
    49→        opts = replace(opts, subjective_threshold=_subjective_threshold(state))
    50→    return build_work_queue(
    51→        state,
    52→        options=opts,
    53→    )
    54→
    55→
    56→def build_execution_queue(
    57→    state: StateModel,
    58→    *,
    59→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    60→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    61→    """Build the execution queue that follows the living plan when present."""
    62→    options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    63→    ctx = options.context or queue_context(
    64→        state,
    65→        plan=_queue_plan_from_options(options),
    66→        target_strict=_subjective_threshold(state),
    67→    )
    68→    return _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    69→        state,
    70→        options=replace(options, context=ctx),
    71→        visibility=QueueVisibility.EXECUTION,
    72→    )
    73→
    74→
    75→def build_backlog_queue(
    76→    state: StateModel,
    77→    *,
    78→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    79→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    80→    """Build the broader backlog, excluding work already tracked in the plan."""
    81→    options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    82→    ctx = options.context or queue_context(
    83→        state,
    84→        plan=_queue_plan_from_options(options),
    85→        target_strict=_subjective_threshold(state),
    86→    )
    87→    return _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    88→        state,
    89→        options=replace(options, context=ctx),
    90→        visibility=QueueVisibility.BACKLOG,
    91→    )
    92→
    93→
    94→__all__ = [
    95→    "build_backlog_queue",
    96→    "build_execution_queue",
    97→    "build_open_plan_queue",
    98→]
    99→

1→"""CLI entry point: parse args, load shared context, dispatch command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import logging
     7→import sys
     8→from collections.abc import Mapping
     9→from functools import lru_cache
    10→from pathlib import Path
    11→from typing import Any
    12→
    13→from desloppify.app.cli_support.parser import create_parser as _create_parser
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    15→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import CommandRuntime
    16→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    17→from desloppify.app.commands.registry import CommandHandler, get_command_handlers
    18→from desloppify.base.config import load_config
    19→from desloppify.base.discovery.source import set_exclusions
    20→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    21→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    22→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    23→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_default_path, get_project_root
    24→from desloppify.base.registry import detector_names, on_detector_registered
    25→from desloppify.base.runtime_state import runtime_scope
    26→from desloppify.languages import available_langs
    27→from desloppify.state_io import load_state
    28→
    29→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    30→
    31→
    32→class _DetectorNamesCacheCompat:
    33→    """Compat shim for tests that poke the legacy detector-name cache."""
    34→
    35→    def __init__(self) -> None:
    36→        self._store: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    37→
    38→    def __contains__(self, key: object) -> bool:
    39→        return key in self._store
    40→
    41→    def __getitem__(self, key: str) -> list[str]:
    42→        return self._store[key]
    43→
    44→    def __setitem__(self, key: str, value: list[str]) -> None:
    45→        self._store[key] = value
    46→
    47→    def pop(self, key: str, default: list[str] | None = None) -> list[str] | None:
    48→        return self._store.pop(key, default)
    49→
    50→
    51→_DETECTOR_NAMES_CACHE = _DetectorNamesCacheCompat()
    52→
    53→
    54→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
    55→def _get_detector_names_cached() -> tuple[str, ...]:
    56→    """Compute detector names once until cache invalidation."""
    57→    return tuple(detector_names())
    58→
    59→
    60→def _get_detector_names() -> list[str]:
    61→    """Return cached detector names, computing on first access."""
    62→    return list(_get_detector_names_cached())
    63→
    64→
    65→def _invalidate_detector_names_cache() -> None:
    66→    """Invalidate detector-name cache when runtime registrations change."""
    67→    _get_detector_names_cached.cache_clear()
    68→    _DETECTOR_NAMES_CACHE.pop("names", None)
    69→
    70→
    71→on_detector_registered(_invalidate_detector_names_cache)
    72→
    73→
    74→def create_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    75→    """Return the top-level argparse parser."""
    76→    return _create_parser(langs=available_langs(), detector_names=_get_detector_names())
    77→
    78→
    79→def _apply_persisted_exclusions(
    80→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    81→    config: Mapping[str, Any],
    82→) -> None:
    83→    """Merge CLI --exclude with persisted config.exclude and apply globally."""
    84→    cli_exclusions = getattr(args, "exclude", None) or []
    85→    persisted_raw = config.get("exclude", [])
    86→    persisted = (
    87→        [entry for entry in persisted_raw if isinstance(entry, str)]
    88→        if isinstance(persisted_raw, list)
    89→        else []
    90→    )
    91→    combined = list(cli_exclusions) + [e for e in persisted if e not in cli_exclusions]
    92→    if not combined:
    93→        return
    94→    set_exclusions(combined)
    95→    if cli_exclusions:
    96→        print(
    97→            colorize(f"  Excluding: {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"),
    98→            file=sys.stderr,
    99→        )
   100→        return
   101→    print(
   102→        colorize(
   103→            f"  Excluding (from config): {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"
   104→        ),
   105→        file=sys.stderr,
   106→    )
   107→
   108→
   109→def _project_root_from_state_path(state_path_value: str | Path | None) -> Path | None:
   110→    """Infer project root from an explicit state path under ``.desloppify``."""
   111→    if state_path_value in (None, ""):
   112→        return None
   113→    try:
   114→        state_file = Path(state_path_value).resolve()
   115→    except OSError:
   116→        return None
   117→    if state_file.parent.name != ".desloppify":
   118→        return None
   119→    if state_file.name == "state.json" or (
   120→        state_file.name.startswith("state-") and state_file.suffix == ".json"
   121→    ):
   122→        return state_file.parent.parent
   123→    return None
   124→
   125→
   126→def _resolve_default_path(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   127→    """Fill args.path from detected language or default source path.
   128→
   129→    For the review command, the last scan path (stored in state) is used as the
   130→    default so that ``desloppify review --prepare`` works on the same scope as
   131→    the preceding scan even when the project files are not under ``src/``.
   132→    """
   133→    if getattr(args, "path", None) is not None:
   134→        return
   135→    runtime_root = get_project_root()
   136→    if getattr(args, "command", None) == "review":
   137→        try:
   138→            state_file = state_path(args)
   139→            if state_file:
   140→                saved = load_state(state_file)
   141→                saved_path = saved.get("scan_path")
   142→                if saved_path:
   143→                    args.path = str((runtime_root / saved_path).resolve())
   144→                    return
   145→        except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
   146→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, "resolve default review path from saved state", exc)
   147→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   148→    if lang:
   149→        args.path = str(runtime_root / lang.default_src)
   150→    else:
```

    50→def _build_next_payload(

    61→    )

    64→    payload["objective_score"] = getattr(
    65→        scores, "objective", state.get("objective_score")
    66→    )
    67→    payload["strict_score"] = getattr(scores, "strict", state.get("strict_score"))
    68→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
    69→        state,
    70→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
    71→    )
    72→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
    73→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
    74→    ]
    75→    return payload

    78→def _emit_requested_output(
    79→    opts: NextOptions,
    80→    payload: dict,
    81→    items: list[dict],

   174→def _render_terminal_queue_view(
   175→    *,

   222→        plan=plan_data,

   228→        next_nudges_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   229→            state,

   232→            strict_score=strict_score,
   233→            target_strict_score=target_strict,
   234→            queue_total=queue_total,
   235→            plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   236→            breakdown=breakdown,
   237→        )
   238→    print()

   240→    if items and plan_data and show_execution_prompt:

   248→def _plan_data_for_view(plan: dict, *, show_plan_context: bool) -> dict | None:
   249→    if not show_plan_context:
   250→        return None
   251→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
   252→        return plan

   256→def _resolve_queue_items(
   257→    *,
   258→    state: dict,
   259→    config: dict,
   260→    opts: NextOptions,
   261→    plan_for_queue: dict,
   262→    target_strict: float,
   263→    build_work_queue_fn,
   264→) -> tuple[object, dict, list[dict], str | None]:
   265→    ctx = queue_context(
   266→        state,
   267→        target_strict=target_strict,
   268→        config=config,
   269→        plan=plan_for_queue,
   270→    )
   271→    effective_cluster = _resolve_cluster_focus(
   272→        plan_for_queue,
   273→        cluster_arg=opts.cluster,
   274→        scope=opts.scope,
   275→    )
   276→    queue = build_work_queue_fn(
   277→        state,
   278→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   279→            count=None,
   280→            scope=opts.scope,
   281→            status=opts.status,
   282→            include_subjective=True,
   283→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   284→            explain=opts.explain,
   285→            include_skipped=opts.include_skipped,
   286→            context=ctx,
   287→        ),
   288→    )
   289→    return ctx, queue, queue.get("items", []), effective_cluster
   290→
   291→
   292→def _apply_queue_view_transforms(
   293→    *,
   294→    items: list[dict],
   295→    queue: dict,
   296→    opts: NextOptions,
   297→    plan_for_queue: dict,
   298→    effective_cluster: str | None,
   299→    collapse_plan_clusters: bool,
   300→) -> list[dict]:
   301→    visible = items
   302→    if collapse_plan_clusters and effective_cluster and plan_for_queue:
   303→        visible = filter_cluster_focus(visible, plan_for_queue, effective_cluster)
   304→    elif (
   305→        collapse_plan_clusters
   306→        and plan_for_queue
   307→        and not effective_cluster
   308→        and not plan_for_queue.get("active_cluster")
   309→    ):
   310→        visible = collapse_clusters(visible, plan_for_queue)
   311→
   312→    if opts.count:
   313→        visible = visible[: opts.count]
   314→        queue["items"] = visible
   315→        queue["total"] = len(visible)
   316→    return visible
   317→
   318→
   319→def _render_non_empty_queue(
   320→    *,
   321→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   322→    state: dict,
   323→    items: list[dict],
   324→    queue: dict,
   325→    plan_data: dict | None,
   326→    guardrail_warnings: list[str],
   327→    write_query_fn,
   328→    view: QueueViewConfig,
   329→    opts: NextOptions,
   330→    plan_for_queue: dict,
   331→    effective_cluster: str | None,
   332→    target_strict: float,
   333→    ctx,
   334→    resolve_lang_fn,
   335→) -> None:
   336→    lang = resolve_lang_fn(args)
   337→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   338→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   339→        state,
   340→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command=view.command_name, plan=plan_data),
   341→    )
   342→    payload = _write_next_payload(
   343→        queue=queue,
   344→        items=items,
   345→        state=state,
   346→        narrative=narrative,
   347→        plan_data=plan_data,
   348→        guardrail_warnings=guardrail_warnings,
   349→        write_query_fn=write_query_fn,
   350→        command_name=view.command_name,
   351→    )
   352→
   353→    if _emit_requested_output(opts, payload, items, command_name=view.command_name):
   354→        return
   355→
   356→    _render_terminal_queue_view(

    51→def _validate_skip_requirements(
    52→    *,
    53→    kind: str,

    55→    note: str | None,
    56→) -> bool:
    57→    if not skip_kind_requires_attestation(kind):

    67→            required_phrases=_TRIAGE_SKIP_ATTESTATION_PHRASES,
    68→        )
    69→        return False
    70→    if skip_kind_requires_note(kind) and not note:
    71→        print(
    72→            colorize("  --permanent requires --note to explain the decision.", "yellow"),
    73→            file=sys.stderr,
    74→        )
    75→        return False
    76→    return True
    77→
    78→
    79→def _apply_state_skip_resolution(
    80→    *,
    81→    kind: str,

    84→    note: str | None,

    97→            attestation=attestation,
    98→        )

   102→def _validate_temporary_skip_confirmation(
   103→    *,
   104→    confirm: bool,
   105→    kind: str,
   106→    reason: str | None,
   107→    attestation: str | None,
   108→) -> bool:
   109→    if not confirm or kind != "temporary":

   117→        )
   118→        return False
   119→    required_phrases = ("i have actually reflected", "not deferring")
   120→    normalized_attest = " ".join((attestation or "").strip().lower().split())
   121→    missing = [phrase for phrase in required_phrases if phrase not in normalized_attest]
   122→    if not missing:
   123→        return True
   124→    print(
   125→        colorize(
   126→            "  Deferring items requires you to confirm you've thought this through.",

   179→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   180→    """Skip issues — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
   181→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   182→    state = runtime.state
   183→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
   184→        return
   185→
   186→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   187→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
   188→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
   189→    permanent: bool = getattr(args, "permanent", False)
   190→    false_positive: bool = getattr(args, "false_positive", False)
   191→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   192→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   193→
   194→    kind = skip_kind_from_flags(permanent=permanent, false_positive=false_positive)
   195→
   196→    if not _validate_temporary_skip_confirmation(
   197→        confirm=bool(getattr(args, "confirm", False)),
   198→        kind=kind,
   199→        reason=reason,
   200→        attestation=attestation,
   201→    ):
   202→        return
   203→    if not _validate_skip_requirements(kind=kind, attestation=attestation, note=note):
   204→        raise CommandError(
   205→            "Invalid plan skip attestation or note.",
   206→            exit_code=2,
   207→        )
   208→
   209→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   210→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   211→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   212→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   213→    if not issue_ids:
   214→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   215→        return
   216→
   217→    _warn_or_block_bulk_skip(issue_ids, confirm=bool(getattr(args, "confirm", False)))
   218→
   219→    state_data = _apply_state_skip_resolution(
   220→        kind=kind,
   221→        state_file=state_file,
   222→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   223→        note=note,
   224→        attestation=attestation,
   225→    )
   226→
   227→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   228→    count = skip_items(
   229→        plan,
   230→        issue_ids,
   231→        kind=kind,
   232→        reason=reason,
   233→        note=note,
   234→        attestation=attestation,
   235→        review_after=review_after,
   236→        scan_count=scan_count,
   237→    )
   238→
   239→    append_log_entry(
   240→        plan,
   241→        "skip",
   242→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   243→        actor="user",
   244→        note=note,
   245→        detail={"kind": kind, "reason": reason},
   246→    )
   247→    clear_postflight_scan_completion(plan, issue_ids=issue_ids)
   248→    _save_skip_plan_state(
   249→        plan=plan,
   250→        plan_file=plan_file,
   251→        state_data=state_data,
   252→        state_file=state_file,
   253→    )
   254→
   255→    print(colorize(f"  {SKIP_KIND_LABELS[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   256→    if review_after:
   257→        print(colorize(f"  Will re-surface after {review_after} scan(s).", "dim"))
   258→    if kind == "temporary":
   259→        print(
   260→            colorize(
   261→                "  If you're actually not going to fix these, use "
   262→                "`desloppify plan skip <patterns> --permanent` instead to wontfix them.",
   263→                "dim",
   264→            )
   265→        )
   266→    print_user_message(
   267→        "Hey — if skipping was the right call, just continue with"
   268→        " what you were doing. If you think a broader re-triage is"
   269→        " needed, use `desloppify plan triage`. Run `desloppify"
   270→        " plan --help` to see all available plan tools. Otherwise"
   271→        " no need to reply, just keep going."
   272→    )
   273→
   274→
   275→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   276→    """Unskip issues — bring back to queue."""
   277→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   278→    state = runtime.state
   279→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
   280→        return
   281→
   282→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   283→
   284→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   285→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   286→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   287→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   288→    if not issue_ids:
   289→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   290→        return
   291→
   292→    include_protected = bool(getattr(args, "force", False))
   293→    count, need_reopen, protected_kept = unskip_items(
   294→        plan,
   295→        issue_ids,
   296→        include_protected=include_protected,
   297→    )
   298→    unskipped_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if fid not in protected_kept]
   299→    append_log_entry(
   300→        plan,
   301→        "unskip",
   302→        issue_ids=unskipped_ids,
   303→        actor="user",
   304→        detail={"need_reopen": need_reopen},
   305→    )
   306→    clear_postflight_scan_completion(plan, issue_ids=unskipped_ids)
   307→
   308→    reopened: list[str] = []
   309→    if need_reopen:
   310→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   311→        for fid in need_reopen:
   312→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, fid, "open"))
   313→        save_plan_state_transactional(
   314→            plan=plan,
   315→            plan_path=plan_file,
   316→            state_data=state_data,
   317→            state_path_value=state_file,
   318→        )
   319→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s) in state.", "dim"))
   320→    else:
   321→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   322→
   323→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))
   324→    if protected_kept:
   325→        print(
   326→            colorize(
   327→                f"  Kept {len(protected_kept)} protected skip(s) "
   328→                f"(permanent/false_positive with notes). Use --force to override.",
   329→                "yellow",
   330→            )
   331→        )
   332→
   333→
   334→__all__ = [

   336→    "_validate_skip_requirements",
   337→    "cmd_plan_skip",
   338→    "cmd_plan_unskip",

     2→

     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations

    11→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS
    12→from desloppify.engine._scoring.subjective.core import DISPLAY_NAMES

    46→
    47→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    48→# Dimension key normalization
    49→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    50→
    51→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    52→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    53→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    54→    target = cleaned.lower()
    55→
    56→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    57→        if str(label).lower() == target:
    58→            return str(dim_key)
    59→    return slugify(cleaned)
    60→
    61→
    62→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
    63→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with issue dimension keys."""
    64→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    65→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
    66→    return {
    67→        cleaned.lower(),
    68→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
    69→        slugify(cleaned),
    70→        canonical.lower(),
    71→        slugify(canonical),
    72→    }
    73→
    74→
    75→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    76→# Subjective strict scores
    77→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    78→
    79→def subjective_strict_scores(state: StateModel | dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, float]:
    80→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    81→    if not dim_scores:
    82→        return {}
    83→
    84→    entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    85→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
    86→    for entry in entries:
    87→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    88→        if not name:
    89→            continue
    90→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    91→        [REDACTED](name)
    92→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
    93→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
    94→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
    95→            if not key:
    96→                continue
    97→            aliases.add(key)
    98→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
    99→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   100→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   101→        for alias in aliases:
   102→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   103→    return scores
   104→
   105→
   106→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   107→# Synthetic item builders
   108→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   109→
   110→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   111→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   112→
   113→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
   114→    """
   115→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   116→    order_set = set(order)
   117→    present_ids = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   118→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   119→    confirmed = confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   120→    recorded_unconfirmed = recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   121→    present_names = {
   122→        name
   123→        for name, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS
   124→        if sid in present_ids
   125→    }
   126→    present_names.update(recorded_unconfirmed)
   127→    if not present_names:
   128→        return []
   129→
   130→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   131→    open_review_count = sum(
   132→        1 for f in issues.values()
   133→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   134→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   135→    )
   136→
   137→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   138→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   139→    for name, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
   140→        if name not in present_names:
   141→            continue
   142→        if name in confirmed:
   143→            continue
   144→
   145→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   146→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   147→        blocked_by = sorted(
   148→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps if dep in present_names and dep not in confirmed
   149→        )
   150→
   151→        only_stages = None if name == "commit" else name
   152→        cmd = triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=only_stages)
   153→
   154→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   155→            "id": sid,
   156→            "tier": 1,
   157→            "confidence": "high",
   158→            "detector": "triage",
   159→            "file": ".",
   160→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   161→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   162→            "detail": {
   163→                "total_review_issues": open_review_count,
   164→                "stage": name,
   165→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   166→                "runner_commands": [
   167→                    {"label": label, "command": command}
   168→                    for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages)
   169→                ],
   170→                "manual_fallback": triage_manual_stage_command(name),
   171→            },
   172→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   173→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   174→        }
   175→        item["primary_command"] = cmd
   176→        items.append(item)
   177→    return items
   178→
   179→
   180→def build_subjective_items(
   181→    state: dict, issues: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   182→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   183→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   184→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   185→    if not dim_scores:
   186→        return []
   187→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   188→
   189→    subjective_entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   190→    if not subjective_entries:
   191→        return []
   192→    unassessed_dims = {
   193→        str(name).strip()
   194→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   195→            dim_scores
   196→        )
   197→    }
   198→
   199→    # Review issues are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   200→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   201→    for issue in issues.values():
   202→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
   203→            continue
   204→        if issue.get("detector") == "review":
   205→            dim_key = str(detail_dict(issue).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   206→            if dim_key:
   207→                review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   208→
   209→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   210→    def _prepare_command(
   211→        cli_keys: list[str],
   212→        *,
   213→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   214→    ) -> str:
   215→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   216→        if cli_keys:
   217→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   218→        if force_review_rerun:
   219→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   220→        return command
   221→
   222→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   223→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   224→        if not name:
   225→            continue
   226→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   227→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   228→            continue
   229→
   230→        [REDACTED](name)
   231→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   232→        cli_keys = [
   233→            str(key).strip().lower()
   234→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   235→            if str(key).strip()
   236→        ]
   237→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   238→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   239→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   240→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   241→            name in unassessed_dims
   242→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("failing", 0)) == 0)
   243→        )
   244→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   245→        # If review issues already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   246→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   247→        if open_review > 0:
   248→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   249→        else:
   250→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   251→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   252→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   253→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   254→            "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   255→            "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   256→            "file": ".",
   257→            "confidence": "medium",
   258→            "summary": summary,
   259→            "detail": {
   260→                "dimension_name": name,
   261→                "dimension": dim_key,
   262→                "failing": int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
   263→                "strict_score": strict_val,
   264→                "open_review_issues": open_review,
   265→                "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   266→            },
   267→            "status": "open",
   268→        }
   269→        item["kind"] = "subjective_dimension"
   270→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command
   271→        item["initial_review"] = is_unassessed
   272→        items.append(item)
   273→    return items
   274→
   275→
   276→__all__ = [
   277→    "build_communicate_score_item",
   278→    "build_create_plan_item",
   279→    "build_deferred_disposition_item",
   280→    "build_import_scores_item",
   281→    "build_run_scan_item",
   282→    "build_score_checkpoint_item",
   283→    "build_subjective_items",
   284→    "build_triage_stage_items",
   285→    "subjective_strict_scores",
   286→]
   287→

    30→def _confirm_attestation_hint(stage: str) -> str:
    31→    if stage == "observe":
    32→        return "I have thoroughly reviewed..."
    33→    if stage == "reflect":
    34→        return "My strategy accounts for..."
    35→    if stage == "organize":
    36→        return "This plan is correct..."
    37→    if stage == "enrich":
    38→        return "Steps are executor-ready..."
    39→    if stage == "sense-check":
    40→        return "Content and structure verified..."
    41→    return "..."
    42→
    43→
    44→def _runner_planning_tools(
    45→    *,
    46→    only_stages: str | None = None,
    47→    manual_fallback: str | None = None,
    48→) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    49→    tools = [
    50→        {"label": label, "command": command}
    51→        for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages)
    52→    ]
    53→    if manual_fallback:
    54→        tools.append({"label": "Manual fallback", "command": manual_fallback})
    55→    tools.append({"label": "Dashboard", "command": "desloppify plan triage"})
    56→    return tools
    57→
    58→
    59→def _triage_progression_target(plan: dict) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:
    60→    """Return current triage stage target and whether it requires confirmation."""
    61→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    62→    triage_stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {}) or {}
    63→    for stage, _sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
    64→        stage_payload = triage_stages.get(stage)
    65→        if isinstance(stage_payload, dict) and stage_payload and not stage_payload.get("confirmed_at"):
    66→            return stage, True
    67→
    68→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    69→    for stage, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
    70→        if sid not in order:
    71→            continue
    72→        return stage, False
    73→
    74→    if "triage::commit" in order:
    75→        return "commit", False
    76→
    77→    return None, False
    78→
    79→
    80→def _create_plan_primary_command(plan: dict) -> str:
    81→    stage, needs_confirm = _triage_progression_target(plan)
    82→    if stage is None:
    83→        return triage_run_stages_command()
    84→    if needs_confirm:
    85→        attestation = _confirm_attestation_hint(stage)
    86→        return (
    87→            f'desloppify plan triage --confirm {stage} '
    88→            f'--attestation "{attestation}"'
    89→        )
    90→    if stage == "commit":
    91→        return triage_run_stages_command()
    92→    return triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=stage)
    93→
    94→
    95→def _create_plan_planning_tools(plan: dict) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    96→    stage, needs_confirm = _triage_progression_target(plan)
    97→    if needs_confirm:
    98→        return []
    99→    if stage == "commit":
   100→        return _runner_planning_tools(
   101→            manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("commit"),
   102→        )
   103→    if stage:
   104→        return _runner_planning_tools(
   105→            only_stages=stage,
   106→            manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command(stage),
   107→        )
   108→    return _runner_planning_tools(
   109→        only_stages="observe",
   110→        manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("observe"),
   111→    )

   275→def _temporary_skipped_ids(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   276→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   277→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
   278→        return []
   279→    deferred: list[str] = []
   280→    for issue_id, entry in skipped.items():
   281→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   282→            continue
   283→        if str(entry.get("kind", "temporary")) == "temporary":
   284→            deferred.append(str(issue_id))
   285→    deferred.sort()
   286→    return deferred
   287→
   288→
   289→def _deferred_cluster_breakdown(
   290→    plan: dict,
   291→    deferred_ids: list[str],
   292→) -> tuple[int, int]:
   293→    deferred_set = set(deferred_ids)
   294→    if not deferred_set:
   295→        return 0, 0
   296→
   297→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   298→    if not isinstance(clusters, dict):
   299→        return 0, len(deferred_ids)
   300→
   301→    covered_by_cluster: set[str] = set()
   302→    cluster_count = 0
   303→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   304→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
   305→            continue
   306→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   307→        if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
   308→            continue
   309→        matched = {str(issue_id) for issue_id in issue_ids if str(issue_id) in deferred_set}
   310→        if not matched:
   311→            continue
   312→        cluster_count += 1
   313→        covered_by_cluster.update(matched)
   314→
   315→    individual_count = len(deferred_set - covered_by_cluster)
   316→    return cluster_count, individual_count
   317→
   318→
   319→def build_deferred_disposition_item(plan: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:
   320→    """Build a synthetic item prompting deferred backlog disposition."""
   321→    deferred_ids = _temporary_skipped_ids(plan)
   322→    if not deferred_ids:
   323→        return None
   324→
   325→    count = len(deferred_ids)
   326→    cluster_count, individual_count = _deferred_cluster_breakdown(plan, deferred_ids)
   327→    cluster_label = "cluster" if cluster_count == 1 else "clusters"
   328→    individual_label = "item" if individual_count == 1 else "items"
   329→    reactivate_cmd = 'desloppify plan unskip "*"'
   330→    subset_reactivate_cmd = "desloppify plan unskip <cluster-or-id>"
   331→    inspect_cmd = "desloppify plan queue --include-skipped"
   332→    inspect_item_cmd = "desloppify show <cluster-or-id>"
   333→    wontfix_cmd = (
   334→        'desloppify plan skip --permanent "*" '
   335→        '--note "<why this deferred work should stay wontfix>" '
   336→        '--attest "I have actually reviewed these deferred items and I am not gaming the score by skipping them permanently." '
   337→        "--confirm"
   338→    )
   339→    subset_wontfix_cmd = (
   340→        "desloppify plan skip --permanent <cluster-or-id> "
   341→        '--note "<why this deferred work should stay wontfix>" '
   342→        '--attest "I have actually reviewed these deferred items and I am not gaming the score by skipping them permanently."'
   343→    )
   344→
   345→    return {
   346→        "id": WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
   347→        "tier": 1,
   348→        "confidence": "high",
   349→        "detector": "workflow",
   350→        "file": ".",

   357→        ),
   358→        "detail": {
