1→"""Preflight guards for subjective review reruns.
     2→
     3→Blocks reruns while backlog remains (objective issues and/or subjective queue
     4→work), unless ``--force-review-rerun`` is set. Also clears stale subjective
     5→markers before a new cycle.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import re
    11→import sys
    12→
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import load_query_result
    14→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    15→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    16→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import queue_context
    17→from desloppify.state_io import StateModel, save_state
    18→
    19→from .helpers import parse_dimensions
    20→
    21→
    22→def clear_stale_subjective_entries(
    23→    state: StateModel,
    24→    *,
    25→    dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    26→) -> list[str]:
    27→    """Clear ``needs_review_refresh`` markers from subjective assessments.
    28→
    29→    When *dimensions* is provided, only those dimensions are cleared;
    30→    otherwise all stale dimensions are cleared.
    31→
    32→    Returns the list of dimension keys that were cleared.
    33→    """
    34→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    35→    cleared: list[str] = []
    36→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    37→        if not isinstance(assessment, dict):
    38→            continue
    39→        if dimensions is not None and dim_key not in dimensions:
    40→            continue
    41→        if assessment.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    42→            assessment.pop("needs_review_refresh", None)
    43→            assessment.pop("stale_since", None)
    44→            assessment.pop("refresh_reason", None)
    45→            cleared.append(dim_key)
    46→    return cleared
    47→
    48→
    49→def _scored_dimensions(state: StateModel) -> list[str]:
    50→    """Return dimension keys that already have a nonzero subjective score."""
    51→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    52→    scored: list[str] = []
    53→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    54→        if isinstance(assessment, dict):
    55→            if assessment.get("score", 0):
    56→                scored.append(dim_key)
    57→        elif isinstance(assessment, int | float) and assessment:
    58→            scored.append(dim_key)
    59→    return sorted(scored)
    60→
    61→
    62→def _normalize_dimension_key(raw: object) -> str:
    63→    """Normalize a dimension key/name to canonical snake_case."""
    64→    text = str(raw or "").strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
    65→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text).strip("_")
    66→
    67→
    68→def _prepared_query_dimensions() -> set[str] | None:
    69→    """Return prepared review dimensions from query.json when available."""
    70→    query_result = load_query_result()
    71→    if not query_result.ok:
    72→        if query_result.error_kind in {"query_parse_error", "query_invalid_shape"}:
    73→            detail = query_result.message or query_result.error_kind
    74→            print(
    75→                colorize(
    76→                    f"  Warning: ignoring invalid query artifact ({detail}).",
    77→                    "yellow",
    78→                ),
    79→                file=sys.stderr,
    80→            )
    81→        return None
    82→    query = query_result.payload or {}
    83→    if not isinstance(query, dict):
    84→        return None
    85→    if query.get("command") != "review" or query.get("mode") != "holistic":
    86→        return None
    87→    if not isinstance(query.get("investigation_batches"), list):
    88→        return None
    89→    dims = query.get("dimensions")
    90→    if not isinstance(dims, list):
    91→        return None
    92→    resolved = {dim.strip() for dim in dims if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()}
    93→    return resolved or None
    94→
    95→
    96→def _target_dimensions(args: object) -> set[str] | None:
    97→    """Resolve review target dimensions from CLI args or prepared query state."""
    98→    dimensions = parse_dimensions(args)
    99→    if dimensions is not None:
   100→        return dimensions
   101→    if getattr(args, "run_batches", False) or getattr(args, "external_start", False):
   102→        return _prepared_query_dimensions()
   103→    return None
   104→
   105→
   106→def _blocking_scored_dimensions(
   107→    state: StateModel,
   108→    *,
   109→    dimensions: set[str] | None,
   110→    normalized_dimensions: set[str] | None,
   111→) -> list[str]:
   112→    scored_dims = _scored_dimensions(state)
   113→    if dimensions is None:
   114→        return scored_dims
   115→    normalized = normalized_dimensions or set()
   116→    return sorted(
   117→        dim
   118→        for dim in scored_dims
   119→        if _normalize_dimension_key(dim) in normalized
   120→    )
   121→
   122→
   123→def _objective_and_subjective_backlog(
   124→    state: StateModel,
   125→    *,
   126→    blocking_dims: list[str],
   127→) -> tuple[int, int]:
   128→    ctx = queue_context(state)
   129→    if not blocking_dims:
   130→        return ctx.snapshot.objective_in_scope_count, 0
   131→
   132→    normalized_blocking_dims = {_normalize_dimension_key(dim) for dim in blocking_dims}
   133→
   134→    def _item_dimension_key(item: dict) -> str | None:
   135→        detail = item.get("detail")
   136→        if not isinstance(detail, dict):
   137→            return None
   138→        dimension = detail.get("dimension")
   139→        if not isinstance(dimension, str) or not dimension.strip():
   140→            return None
   141→        return _normalize_dimension_key(dimension)
   142→
   143→    subjective_total = sum(
   144→        1
   145→        for item in ctx.snapshot.all_postflight_review_items
   146→        if item.get("kind") != "subjective_dimension"
   147→        if (dim_key := _item_dimension_key(item)) is not None
   148→        and dim_key in normalized_blocking_dims
   149→    )
   150→    return ctx.snapshot.objective_in_scope_count, subjective_total
   151→
   152→
   153→def _print_backlog_blocked_message(
   154→    *,
   155→    blocking_dims: list[str],
   156→    objective_total: int,
   157→    subjective_total: int,
   158→    dimensions: set[str] | None,
   159→) -> None:
   160→    print(
   161→        colorize(
   162→            "  Blocked: rerun requires drained backlog "
   163→            f"(objective: {objective_total}, subjective: {subjective_total}).",
   164→            "red",
   165→        ),
   166→        file=sys.stderr,
   167→    )
   168→    print(
   169→        colorize(
   170→            f"  Scored dimensions: {', '.join(blocking_dims)}",
   171→            "yellow",
   172→        ),
   173→        file=sys.stderr,
   174→    )
   175→    if objective_total > 0:
   176→        print(
   177→            colorize(
   178→                f"  Open objective issue(s): {objective_total}",
   179→                "yellow",
   180→            ),
   181→            file=sys.stderr,
   182→        )
   183→    if subjective_total > 0:
   184→        print(
   185→            colorize(
   186→                f"  Open subjective queue item(s): {subjective_total}",
   187→                "yellow",
   188→            ),
   189→            file=sys.stderr,
   190→        )
   191→    print("", file=sys.stderr)
   192→    if dimensions is not None:
   193→        unscored = sorted(dimensions - set(blocking_dims))
   194→        if unscored:
   195→            print(
   196→                colorize(
   197→                    f"  Tip: target only unscored dimensions with "
   198→                    f"--dimensions {','.join(unscored)}",
   199→                    "dim",
   200→                ),
   201→                file=sys.stderr,
   202→            )
   203→    print(
   204→        colorize(
   205→            "  Resolve open items first, or override with --force-review-rerun",
   206→            "dim",
   207→        ),
   208→        file=sys.stderr,
   209→    )
   210→
   211→
   212→def review_rerun_preflight(
   213→    state: StateModel,
   214→    args,
   215→    *,
   216→    state_file=None,
   217→    save_fn=save_state,
   218→) -> None:
   219→    """Single entry point: gate check -> clear stale -> save.
   220→
   221→    Exits with code 1 when open backlog exists and ``--force-review-rerun`` is
   222→    not set. On success, clears stale subjective markers for targeted
   223→    dimensions and persists state.
   224→    """
   225→    dimensions = _target_dimensions(args)
   226→    normalized_dimensions = (
   227→        {_normalize_dimension_key(dim) for dim in dimensions}
   228→        if dimensions is not None
   229→        else None
   230→    )
   231→
   232→    # --force-review-rerun bypasses the gate
   233→    if getattr(args, "force_review_rerun", False):
   234→        print(
   235→            colorize(
   236→                "  --force-review-rerun: bypassing rerun backlog checks.",
   237→                "yellow",
   238→            )
   239→        )
   240→    else:
   241→        # Only gate dimensions that are actually targeted by this review run.
   242→        blocking_dims = _blocking_scored_dimensions(
   243→            state,
   244→            dimensions=dimensions,
   245→            normalized_dimensions=normalized_dimensions,
   246→        )
   247→
   248→        # No gate when none of the targeted dimensions have prior scores —
   249→        # this is a first run for these dimensions, not a rerun.
   250→        if blocking_dims:
   251→            objective_total, subjective_total = _objective_and_subjective_backlog(
   252→                state,
   253→                blocking_dims=blocking_dims,
   254→            )
   255→            if objective_total > 0 or subjective_total > 0:
   256→                _print_backlog_blocked_message(
   257→                    blocking_dims=blocking_dims,
   258→                    objective_total=objective_total,
   259→                    subjective_total=subjective_total,
   260→                    dimensions=dimensions,
   261→                )
   262→                raise CommandError(
   263→                    f"rerun blocked: open backlog (objective: {objective_total}, "
   264→                    f"subjective: {subjective_total})",
   265→                    exit_code=1,
   266→                )
   267→
   268→    # Gate passed — clear stale for targeted dims
   269→    cleared = clear_stale_subjective_entries(state, dimensions=dimensions)
   270→    if cleared and state_file:
   271→        save_fn(state, state_file)
   272→        print(
   273→            colorize(
   274→                f"  Cleared stale review markers: {', '.join(sorted(cleared))}",
   275→                "cyan",
   276→            )
   277→        )
   278→
```

   186→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
   187→    version: Required[int]

   210→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   211→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   212→    now = utc_now()
   213→    return {
   214→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   215→        "created": now,
   216→        "updated": now,
   217→        "queue_order": [],
   218→        "deferred": [],
   219→        "skipped": {},
   220→        "active_cluster": None,
   221→        "overrides": {},
   222→        "clusters": {},
   223→        "superseded": {},
   224→        "promoted_ids": [],
   225→        "plan_start_scores": {},
   226→        "refresh_state": {},
   227→        "execution_log": [],
   228→        "epic_triage_meta": {},
   229→        "commit_log": [],
   230→        "uncommitted_issues": [],
   231→        "commit_tracking_branch": None,
   232→    }
   233→
   234→
   235→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   236→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   237→
   238→    Runtime contract is v8-only. Legacy payloads are upgraded in-place once.
   239→    """
   240→    defaults = empty_plan()
   241→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   242→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   243→    _upgrade_plan_to_v8(plan)
   244→    subjective_defer_meta = plan.get("subjective_defer_meta")
   245→    if isinstance(subjective_defer_meta, dict):
   246→        subjective_defer_meta.pop("force_visible_ids", None)
   247→    epic_triage_meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta")
   248→    if isinstance(epic_triage_meta, dict):
   249→        epic_triage_meta.pop("triage_force_visible", None)
   250→
   251→
   252→def triage_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   253→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   254→    return {
   255→        name: cluster
   256→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   257→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   258→    }
   259→
   260→
   261→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   262→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   263→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   264→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   265→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   266→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   267→
   268→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   269→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   270→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   271→    if overlap:
   272→        raise ValueError(
   273→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   274→        )
   275→
   276→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   277→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   278→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   279→            raise ValueError(f"plan.skipped[{fid!r}] must be an object")
   280→        if "kind" not in entry:
   281→            raise ValueError(f"plan.skipped[{fid!r}] missing required key 'kind'")
   282→        kind = entry["kind"]
   283→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   284→            raise ValueError(
   285→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   286→            )
   287→
   288→
   289→__all__ = [
   290→    "ActionStep",
   291→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   292→    "EpicTriageMeta",
   293→    "ExecutionLogEntry",
   294→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   295→    "Cluster",
   296→    "CommitRecord",

   307→    "empty_plan",
   308→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   309→    "triage_clusters",

   311→]

1→"""Queue ranking and plan-aware finalization."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.models import QueueBuildOptions, WorkQueueResult
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import (
     7→    enrich_plan_metadata,
     8→    separate_skipped,
     9→    stamp_plan_sort_keys,
    10→    stamp_positions,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import new_item_ids as _new_item_ids
    13→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    14→    enrich_with_impact,
    15→    group_queue_items,
    16→    item_explain,
    17→    item_sort_key,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    20→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    21→
    22→
    23→def finalize_queue(
    24→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    25→    *,
    26→    state: StateModel,
    27→    plan: dict | None,
    28→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    29→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    30→    """Apply ranking, plan ordering, limits, and explain metadata."""
    31→    enrich_with_impact(items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
    32→
    33→    new_ids, skipped = _plan_presort(items, state, plan)
    34→    items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
    35→    _plan_postsort(items, skipped, plan, opts)
    36→
    37→    total = len(items)
    38→    if opts.count is not None and opts.count > 0:
    39→        items = items[:opts.count]
    40→    if opts.explain:
    41→        for item in items:
    42→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
    43→
    44→    return {
    45→        "items": items,
    46→        "total": total,
    47→        "grouped": group_queue_items(items, "item"),
    48→        "new_ids": new_ids,
    49→    }
    50→
    51→
    52→def _plan_presort(
    53→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    54→    state: StateModel,
    55→    plan: dict | None,
    56→) -> tuple[set[str], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
    57→    """Enrich plan metadata and stamp sort keys before sorting."""
    58→    if not plan:
    59→        return set(), []
    60→
    61→    new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
    62→    enrich_plan_metadata(items, plan)
    63→    stamp_plan_sort_keys(items, plan, new_ids)
    64→    remaining, skipped = separate_skipped(items, plan)
    65→    items[:] = remaining
    66→    return new_ids, skipped
    67→
    68→
    69→def _plan_postsort(
    70→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    71→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem],
    72→    plan: dict | None,
    73→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    74→) -> None:
    75→    """Re-append skipped items and stamp positions."""
    76→    if not plan:
    77→        return
    78→    if opts.include_skipped:
    79→        items.extend(skipped)
    80→    stamp_positions(items, plan)
    81→
    82→
    83→__all__ = ["finalize_queue"]
    84→

1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from typing import Any, cast
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
     9→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import TRIAGE_STAGE_ORDER
    10→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_breakdown
    11→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.impact import get_dimension_for_detector
    12→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import path_scoped_issues
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    15→    ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY,
    16→    detail_dict,
    17→    is_review_issue,
    18→    is_subjective_issue,
    19→    primary_command_for_issue,
    20→    review_issue_weight,
    21→    scope_matches,
    22→    slugify,
    23→    status_matches,
    24→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    25→    workflow_stage_name,
    26→)
    27→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking_output import (
    28→    group_queue_items,
    29→    item_explain,
    30→    subjective_score_value,
    31→)
    32→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic import subjective_strict_scores
    33→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    34→from desloppify.engine.planning.helpers import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    35→
    36→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    37→
    38→# Plan-aware sort tiers (item_sort_key)
    39→_TIER_PLANNED = 0   # Items with explicit plan position
    40→_TIER_EXISTING = 1  # Known items, natural ranking
    41→_TIER_NEW = 2       # Newly discovered items
    42→
    43→# Natural ranking groups (_natural_sort_key)
    44→_RANK_INITIAL_REVIEW = -3  # Unassessed subjective dimensions
    45→_RANK_WORKFLOW = -2         # Score checkpoints, create-plan
    46→_RANK_TRIAGE_STAGE = -1     # Epic triage workflow stages
    47→_RANK_CLUSTER = 0           # Auto-clustered issues
    48→_RANK_ISSUE = 1           # Individual issues + assessed subjective
    49→
    50→def _workflow_stage_index(item: WorkQueueItem) -> int:
    51→    raw_index = item.get("stage_index")
    52→    if raw_index is not None:
    53→        if isinstance(raw_index, int):
    54→            return raw_index
    55→        if isinstance(raw_index, str):
    56→            stripped = raw_index.strip()
    57→            if stripped.lstrip("-").isdigit():
    58→                return int(stripped)
    59→        logger.warning("Invalid workflow stage index %r", raw_index)
    60→    return TRIAGE_STAGE_ORDER.get(workflow_stage_name(item).lower(), 0)
    61→
    62→
    63→def enrich_with_impact(
    64→    items: list[WorkQueueItem], dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]
    65→) -> None:
    66→    """Stamp ``estimated_impact`` on each item based on dimension-level headroom.
    67→
    68→    Impact = ``overall_per_point * headroom`` where headroom = ``100 - score``.
    69→    Items in dimensions with more score headroom sort first.
    70→    """
    71→    if not dimension_scores:
    72→        for item in items:
    73→            item["estimated_impact"] = 0.0
    74→        return
    75→
    76→    breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores, score_key="strict")
    77→    entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])
    78→
    79→    # Build lookup: normalized dimension name -> {per_point, headroom}
    80→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    81→    for entry in entries:
    82→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    83→        if not name:
    84→            continue
    85→        per_point = float(entry.get("overall_per_point", 0.0))
    86→        score = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
    87→        headroom = 100.0 - score
    88→        dim_impact[name.lower()] = {"per_point": per_point, "headroom": headroom}
    89→
    90→    for item in items:
    91→        impact = _compute_item_impact(item, dim_impact, get_dimension_for_detector)
    92→        item["estimated_impact"] = impact
    93→
    94→
    95→def _compute_item_impact(
    96→    item: WorkQueueItem,
    97→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]],
    98→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    99→) -> float:
   100→    """Compute impact value for a single queue item."""
   101→    kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   102→
   103→    # Subjective items (synthetic dimensions or subjective issues):
   104→    # look up by detail.dimension_name
   105→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   106→        dim_name = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
   107→        entry = dim_impact.get(dim_name.lower())
   108→        if entry:
   109→            return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
   110→        return 0.0
   111→
   112→    # Mechanical issues: use detector -> dimension mapping
   113→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   114→    if detector:
   115→        dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   116→        if dimension:
   117→            entry = dim_impact.get(dimension.name.lower())
   118→            if entry:
   119→                return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
   120→
   121→    return 0.0
   122→
   123→
   124→def build_issue_items(
   125→    state: StateModel,
   126→    *,
   127→    scan_path: str | None,
   128→    status_filter: str,
   129→    scope: str | None,
   130→    chronic: bool,
   131→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   132→    scoped = path_scoped_issues(state.get("issues", {}), scan_path)
   133→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
   134→    out: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   135→
   136→    for issue_id, issue in scoped.items():
   137→        if issue.get("suppressed"):
   138→            continue
   139→        if not status_matches(issue.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
   140→            continue
   141→        if chronic and not (
   142→            issue.get("status") == "open" and issue.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
   143→        ):
   144→            continue
   145→
   146→        # Evidence-only: skip issues below standalone confidence threshold
   147→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
   148→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   149→        if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
   150→            threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
   151→            issue_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(issue.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
   152→            if issue_rank > threshold_rank:
   153→                continue
   154→
   155→        item = cast(WorkQueueItem, dict(issue))
   156→        item["id"] = issue_id
   157→        item["kind"] = "issue"
   158→        item["is_review"] = is_review_issue(item)
   159→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_issue(item)
   160→        item["review_weight"] = (
   161→            review_issue_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
   162→        )
   163→        subjective_score = None
   164→        if item["is_subjective"]:
   165→            detail = detail_dict(issue)
   166→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
   167→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
   168→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
   169→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
   170→            )
   171→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
   172→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
   173→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_issue(
   174→            item,
   175→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
   176→        )
   177→
   178→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
   179→            continue
   180→        out.append(item)
   181→
   182→    return out
   183→
   184→
   185→def _natural_sort_key(item: WorkQueueItem) -> tuple:
   186→    """Compute natural (non-plan-aware) ranking for queue items."""
   187→    kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   188→
   189→    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority

    47→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

    51→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    52→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    53→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    54→    target = cleaned.lower()
    55→
    56→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    57→        if str(label).lower() == target:
    58→            return str(dim_key)
    59→    return slugify(cleaned)
    60→
    61→
    62→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
    63→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with issue dimension keys."""
    64→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    65→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
    66→    return {
    67→        cleaned.lower(),
    68→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
    69→        slugify(cleaned),
    70→        canonical.lower(),
    71→        slugify(canonical),
    72→    }
    73→
    74→
    75→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    76→# Subjective strict scores
    77→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    78→
    79→def subjective_strict_scores(state: StateModel | dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, float]:

    86→    for entry in entries:

    88→        if not name:
    89→            continue
    90→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    91→        [REDACTED](name)

    95→            if not key:
    96→                continue
    97→            aliases.add(key)
    98→            aliases.add(slugify(key))

    53→    if manual_fallback:

    55→    tools.append({"label": "Dashboard", "command": "desloppify plan triage"})
    56→    return tools

    66→            return stage, True
    67→
    68→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    69→    for stage, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
    70→        if sid not in order:
    71→            continue
    72→        return stage, False
    73→
    74→    if "triage::commit" in order:
    75→        return "commit", False
    76→
    77→    return None, False
    78→
    79→

    84→    if needs_confirm:

    86→        return (
    87→            f'desloppify plan triage --confirm {stage} '
    88→            f'--attestation "{attestation}"'
    89→        )
    90→    if stage == "commit":
    91→        return triage_run_stages_command()
    92→    return triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=stage)

1→"""Helpers for the persisted queue lifecycle phase.
     2→
     3→The persisted phase is the normal source of truth for CLI/debugging, but it is
     4→not trusted blindly. Queue assembly still re-resolves the active phase from the
     5→currently visible items as a safety net so stale saved state cannot strand the
     6→user in the wrong phase after out-of-band changes.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from typing import Iterable
    12→
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    15→
    16→[REDACTED]
    17→_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY = "lifecycle_phase"
    18→
    19→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN = "scan"
    20→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW = "review"
    21→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW = "workflow"
    22→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE = "triage"
    23→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE = "execute"
    24→VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES = frozenset(
    25→    {
    26→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN,
    27→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    28→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
    29→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    30→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE,
    31→    }
    32→)
    33→
    34→
    35→def _refresh_state(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, object]:
    36→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    37→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    38→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
    39→        refresh_state = {}
    40→        plan["refresh_state"] = refresh_state
    41→    return refresh_state
    42→
    43→
    44→def _is_real_queue_issue(issue_id: str) -> bool:
    45→    return not any(str(issue_id).startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
    46→
    47→
    48→def current_lifecycle_phase(plan: PlanModel) -> str | None:
    49→    """Return the persisted lifecycle phase, falling back for legacy plans."""
    50→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    51→    if isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
    52→        phase = refresh_state.get(_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY)
    53→        if isinstance(phase, str) and phase in VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES:
    54→            return phase
    55→    if postflight_scan_pending(plan):
    56→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
    57→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    58→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
    59→    return None
    60→
    61→
    62→def set_lifecycle_phase(plan: PlanModel, phase: str) -> bool:
    63→    """Persist the current queue lifecycle phase."""
    64→    if phase not in VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES:
    65→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported lifecycle phase: {phase}")
    66→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
    67→    if refresh_state.get(_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY) == phase:
    68→        return False
    69→    refresh_state[_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY] = phase
    70→    return True
    71→
    72→
    73→def sync_lifecycle_phase(
    74→    plan: PlanModel,
    75→    *,
    76→    has_initial_reviews: bool,
    77→    has_objective_backlog: bool,
    78→    has_postflight_review: bool,
    79→    has_postflight_workflow: bool,
    80→    has_triage: bool,
    81→    has_deferred: bool,
    82→) -> tuple[str, bool]:
    83→    """Resolve and persist the current lifecycle phase from queue-state facts."""
    84→    phase = resolve_lifecycle_phase(
    85→        plan,
    86→        has_initial_reviews=has_initial_reviews,
    87→        has_objective_backlog=has_objective_backlog,
    88→        has_postflight_review=has_postflight_review,
    89→        has_postflight_workflow=has_postflight_workflow,
    90→        has_triage=has_triage,
    91→        has_deferred=has_deferred,
    92→    )
    93→    return phase, set_lifecycle_phase(plan, phase)
    94→
    95→
    96→def resolve_lifecycle_phase(
    97→    plan: PlanModel,
    98→    *,
    99→    has_initial_reviews: bool,
   100→    has_objective_backlog: bool,

    17→    item_sort_key,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    20→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    21→
    22→
    23→def finalize_queue(
    24→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    25→    *,
    26→    state: StateModel,
    27→    plan: dict | None,
    28→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    29→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    30→    """Apply ranking, plan ordering, limits, and explain metadata."""
    31→    enrich_with_impact(items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
    32→
    33→    new_ids, skipped = _plan_presort(items, state, plan)
    34→    items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
    35→    _plan_postsort(items, skipped, plan, opts)
    36→
    37→    total = len(items)
    38→    if opts.count is not None and opts.count > 0:
    39→        items = items[:opts.count]
    40→    if opts.explain:
    41→        for item in items:
    42→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
    43→
    44→    return {
    45→        "items": items,
    46→        "total": total,
    47→        "grouped": group_queue_items(items, "item"),
    48→        "new_ids": new_ids,
    49→    }
    50→
    51→
    52→def _plan_presort(
    53→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    54→    state: StateModel,
    55→    plan: dict | None,
    56→) -> tuple[set[str], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
    57→    """Enrich plan metadata and stamp sort keys before sorting."""
    58→    if not plan:
    59→        return set(), []
    60→
    61→    new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
    62→    enrich_plan_metadata(items, plan)
    63→    stamp_plan_sort_keys(items, plan, new_ids)
    64→    remaining, skipped = separate_skipped(items, plan)
    65→    items[:] = remaining
    66→    return new_ids, skipped
    67→
    68→
    69→def _plan_postsort(
    70→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    71→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem],
    72→    plan: dict | None,
    73→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    74→) -> None:
    75→    """Re-append skipped items and stamp positions."""
    76→    if not plan:
    77→        return
    78→    if opts.include_skipped:
    79→        items.extend(skipped)
    80→    stamp_positions(items, plan)
    81→
    82→
    83→__all__ = ["finalize_queue"]
    84→
```

1→"""Queue input resolution and non-open item gathering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import ALL_STATUSES, scope_matches
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.models import QueueBuildOptions
     7→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic import build_subjective_items
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
     9→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    10→
    11→
    12→def resolve_queue_inputs(
    13→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    14→    state: StateModel,
    15→) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None, str, float]:
    16→    """Resolve plan, scan path, status, and subjective threshold."""
    17→    ctx = opts.context
    18→    plan = ctx.plan if ctx is not None else opts.plan
    19→
    20→    scan_path: str | None = (
    21→        state.get("scan_path")
    22→        if opts.scan_path.__class__.__name__ == "_ScanPathFromState"
    23→        else opts.scan_path
    24→    )
    25→
    26→    status = opts.status
    27→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
    28→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
    29→
    30→    try:
    31→        threshold = float(opts.subjective_threshold)
    32→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    33→        threshold = 100.0
    34→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, threshold))
    35→    return plan, scan_path, status, threshold
    36→
    37→
    38→def gather_subjective_items(
    39→    state: StateModel,
    40→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    41→    threshold: float,
    42→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    43→    """Build subjective dimension candidates for non-open issue list views."""
    44→    if not opts.include_subjective:
    45→        return []
    46→    if opts.status not in {"open", "all"}:
    47→        return []
    48→    if opts.chronic:
    49→        return []
    50→
    51→    candidates = build_subjective_items(
    52→        state,
    53→        state.get("issues", {}),
    54→        threshold=threshold,
    55→    )
    56→    return [item for item in candidates if scope_matches(item, opts.scope)]
    57→
    58→
    59→__all__ = ["gather_subjective_items", "resolve_queue_inputs"]
    60→

   119→    persisted = current_lifecycle_phase(plan)
   120→    if persisted is not None:

   122→    return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
   123→
   124→
   125→def postflight_scan_pending(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
   126→    """Return True when the current empty-queue boundary still needs a scan."""
   127→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
   128→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
   129→        return True
   130→    return not isinstance(refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY), int)
   131→
   132→
   133→def mark_postflight_scan_completed(
   134→    plan: PlanModel,
   135→    *,
   136→    scan_count: int | None,
   137→) -> bool:
   138→    """Record that the scan stage completed for the current refresh cycle."""
   139→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   140→    try:
   141→        normalized_scan_count = int(scan_count or 0)
   142→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   143→        normalized_scan_count = 0
   144→    if refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY) == normalized_scan_count:
   145→        return False
   146→    refresh_state[_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY] = normalized_scan_count
   147→    return True
   148→
   149→
   150→def clear_postflight_scan_completion(
   151→    plan: PlanModel,
   152→    *,
   153→    issue_ids: Iterable[str] | None = None,
   154→) -> bool:
   155→    """Require a fresh scan after queue-changing work on real issues."""
   156→    if issue_ids is not None and not any(
   157→        _is_real_queue_issue(issue_id) for issue_id in issue_ids
   158→    ):
   159→        return False
   160→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   161→    if _POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY not in refresh_state:
   162→        return False
   163→    refresh_state[_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY] = LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   164→    refresh_state.pop(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY, None)
   165→    return True
   166→

    47→WORKFLOW_PRIORITY_ORDER = (

    57→
    58→@dataclass
    59→class QueueSyncResult:
    60→    """Unified result for all queue sync operations."""
    61→
    62→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    63→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    64→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    65→    deferred: bool = False
    66→
    67→    @property
    68→    def changes(self) -> int:
    69→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned) + len(self.resurfaced)
    70→
    71→
    72→def _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    73→    """Extract the triage stages dict from meta or a raw stages dict."""
    74→    if not isinstance(meta_or_stages, dict):
    75→        return {}
    76→    if "triage_stages" in meta_or_stages:
    77→        raw = meta_or_stages.get("triage_stages")
    78→        return raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}
    79→    candidate_names = {str(name) for name in meta_or_stages.keys()}
    80→    if candidate_names and candidate_names.issubset(_TRIAGE_STAGE_NAMES):
    81→        return meta_or_stages
    82→    return {}
    83→
    84→
    85→def confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
    86→    """Return triage stage names with an explicit ``confirmed_at`` marker."""
    87→    return {
    88→        str(name)
    89→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
    90→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload.get("confirmed_at")
    91→    }
    92→
    93→
    94→def recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
    95→    """Return recorded triage stage names that still need confirmation."""
    96→    return {
    97→        str(name)
    98→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
    99→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload and not payload.get("confirmed_at")
   100→    }
   101→
   102→
   103→def normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(queue_order: list[str]) -> None:
   104→    """Keep workflow items ahead of triage, then preserve the rest as-is."""
   105→    seen: set[str] = set()
   106→    normalized: list[str] = []
   107→
   108→    for issue_id in WORKFLOW_PRIORITY_ORDER:
   109→        if issue_id in queue_order and issue_id not in seen:
   110→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   111→            seen.add(issue_id)
   112→
   113→    for issue_id in queue_order:
   114→        if issue_id.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX) and issue_id not in seen:
   115→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   116→            seen.add(issue_id)
   117→
   118→    for issue_id in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:

   129→    queue_order[:] = normalized

    62→def _option_value(options: object | None, name: str, default: Any) -> Any:
    63→    if options is None:
    64→        return default
    65→    return getattr(options, name, default)
    66→
    67→
    68→def _resolved_scan_path(options: object | None, state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    69→    scan_path = _option_value(options, "scan_path", state.get("scan_path"))
    70→    if hasattr(scan_path, "__class__") and scan_path.__class__.__name__ == "_ScanPathFromState":
    71→        return state.get("scan_path")
    72→    return scan_path
    73→
    74→
    75→def _tracked_plan_ids(plan: dict | None) -> set[str]:
    76→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
    77→        return set()
    78→    tracked: set[str] = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    79→    tracked.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    80→    tracked.update(plan.get("overrides", {}).keys())
    81→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    82→        tracked.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    83→        for step in cluster.get("action_steps", []):
    84→            if isinstance(step, dict):
    85→                tracked.update(step.get("issue_refs", []))
    86→    return tracked
    87→
    88→
    89→def _is_fresh_boundary(plan: dict | None) -> bool:
    90→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
    91→        return True
    92→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    93→    if not scores:
    94→        return True
    95→    return isinstance(scores, dict) and bool(scores.get("reset"))
    96→
    97→
    98→def _is_objective_item(item: WorkQueueItem, *, skipped_ids: set[str]) -> bool:
    99→    return (
   100→        item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
   101→        and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   102→        and item.get("id", "") not in skipped_ids
   103→    )
   104→
   105→
   106→def _review_issue_items(items: Iterable[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   107→    return [
   108→        item for item in items
   109→        if item.get("detector", "") in {"review", "concerns", "subjective_review"}
   110→    ]
   111→
   112→
   113→def _subjective_partitions(
   114→    state: StateModel,
   115→    *,
   116→    scoped_issues: dict[str, dict],
   117→    threshold: float,
   118→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   119→    candidates = build_subjective_items(state, scoped_issues, threshold=threshold)
   120→    initial = [item for item in candidates if item.get("initial_review")]
   121→    postflight = [item for item in candidates if not item.get("initial_review")]
   122→    return initial, postflight
   123→
   124→
   125→def _workflow_partitions(
   126→    plan: dict | None,
   127→    state: StateModel,
   128→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   129→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   130→        return [], [], []
   131→    scan_items = [
   132→        item
   133→        for item in (
   134→            build_deferred_disposition_item(plan),
   135→            build_run_scan_item(plan),
   136→        )
   137→        if item is not None
   138→    ]
   139→    postflight_workflow = [
   140→        item
   141→        for item in (
   142→            build_score_checkpoint_item(plan, state),
   143→            build_import_scores_item(plan, state),
   144→            build_communicate_score_item(plan, state),
   145→            build_create_plan_item(plan),
   146→        )
   147→        if item is not None
   148→    ]
   149→    triage_items = build_triage_stage_items(plan, state)
   150→    return scan_items, postflight_workflow, triage_items

1→"""Queue input resolution and non-open item gathering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import ALL_STATUSES, scope_matches
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.models import QueueBuildOptions
     7→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic import build_subjective_items
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
     9→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    10→
    11→
    12→def resolve_queue_inputs(
    13→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    14→    state: StateModel,
    15→) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None, str, float]:
    16→    """Resolve plan, scan path, status, and subjective threshold."""
    17→    ctx = opts.context
    18→    plan = ctx.plan if ctx is not None else opts.plan
    19→
    20→    scan_path: str | None = (
    21→        state.get("scan_path")
    22→        if opts.scan_path.__class__.__name__ == "_ScanPathFromState"
    23→        else opts.scan_path
    24→    )
    25→
    26→    status = opts.status
    27→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
    28→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
    29→
    30→    try:
    31→        threshold = float(opts.subjective_threshold)
    32→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    33→        threshold = 100.0
    34→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, threshold))
    35→    return plan, scan_path, status, threshold
    36→
    37→
    38→def gather_subjective_items(
    39→    state: StateModel,
    40→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    41→    threshold: float,
    42→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    43→    """Build subjective dimension candidates for non-open issue list views."""
    44→    if not opts.include_subjective:
    45→        return []
    46→    if opts.status not in {"open", "all"}:
    47→        return []
    48→    if opts.chronic:
    49→        return []
    50→
    51→    candidates = build_subjective_items(
    52→        state,
    53→        state.get("issues", {}),
    54→        threshold=threshold,
    55→    )
    56→    return [item for item in candidates if scope_matches(item, opts.scope)]
    57→
    58→
    59→__all__ = ["gather_subjective_items", "resolve_queue_inputs"]
    60→

     9→
    10→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"

    13→TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"

    23→    (stage_id.removeprefix("triage::"), stage_id) for stage_id in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS
    24→)
    25→TRIAGE_STAGE_ORDER = {
    26→    stage_name: index for index, (stage_name, _stage_id) in enumerate(TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS)
    27→}
    28→TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    29→_TRIAGE_STAGE_NAMES = {
    30→    stage_id.removeprefix("triage::") for stage_id in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS
    31→}
    32→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    33→WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
    34→WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
    35→WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"

    44→    WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,

    46→}
    47→WORKFLOW_PRIORITY_ORDER = (
    48→    WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
    49→    WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID,
    50→    WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
    51→    WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
    52→    WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
    53→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
    54→)
    55→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    56→
    57→
    58→@dataclass
    59→class QueueSyncResult:
    60→    """Unified result for all queue sync operations."""
    61→
    62→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    63→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    64→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    65→    deferred: bool = False
    66→
    67→    @property
    68→    def changes(self) -> int:
    69→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned) + len(self.resurfaced)
    70→
    71→
    72→def _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    73→    """Extract the triage stages dict from meta or a raw stages dict."""
    74→    if not isinstance(meta_or_stages, dict):
    75→        return {}
    76→    if "triage_stages" in meta_or_stages:
    77→        raw = meta_or_stages.get("triage_stages")
    78→        return raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}
    79→    candidate_names = {str(name) for name in meta_or_stages.keys()}
    80→    if candidate_names and candidate_names.issubset(_TRIAGE_STAGE_NAMES):
    81→        return meta_or_stages
    82→    return {}
    83→
    84→
    85→def confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
    86→    """Return triage stage names with an explicit ``confirmed_at`` marker."""
    87→    return {
    88→        str(name)
    89→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
    90→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload.get("confirmed_at")
    91→    }
    92→
    93→
    94→def recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
    95→    """Return recorded triage stage names that still need confirmation."""
    96→    return {
    97→        str(name)
    98→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
    99→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload and not payload.get("confirmed_at")
   100→    }
   101→
   102→
   103→def normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(queue_order: list[str]) -> None:
   104→    """Keep workflow items ahead of triage, then preserve the rest as-is."""
   105→    seen: set[str] = set()
   106→    normalized: list[str] = []
   107→
   108→    for issue_id in WORKFLOW_PRIORITY_ORDER:
   109→        if issue_id in queue_order and issue_id not in seen:
   110→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   111→            seen.add(issue_id)
   112→
   113→    for issue_id in queue_order:
   114→        if issue_id.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX) and issue_id not in seen:
   115→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   116→            seen.add(issue_id)
   117→
   118→    for issue_id in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   119→        if issue_id in queue_order and issue_id not in seen:
   120→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   121→            seen.add(issue_id)
   122→
   123→    for issue_id in queue_order:
   124→        if issue_id in seen:
   125→            continue
   126→        normalized.append(issue_id)
   127→        seen.add(issue_id)
   128→
   129→    queue_order[:] = normalized
   130→

    54→def print_score_section(
    55→    state: StateModel,
    56→    scores: ScoreSnapshot,
    57→    plan: dict,
    58→    target_strict_score: float | None,
    59→    ctx: object | None = None,
    60→):
    61→    """Print score header using live or frozen plan-start mode."""
    62→    plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan)
    63→    breakdown = None
    64→    try:
    65→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan, context=ctx)
    66→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
    67→        _logger.debug("Plan-aware queue count failed: %s", exc)
    68→
    69→    mode = score_display_mode(breakdown, plan_start_strict)
    70→    if mode is ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN:
    71→        print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
    72→            breakdown,
    73→            frozen_strict=plan_start_strict,
    74→            live_score=scores.strict,
    75→        )
    76→    else:
    77→        for line, style in score_summary_lines(
    78→            overall_score=scores.overall,
    79→            objective_score=scores.objective,
    80→            strict_score=scores.strict,
    81→            verified_strict_score=scores.verified,
    82→            target_strict=target_strict_score,
    83→        ):
    84→            print(colorize(line, style))
    85→        if breakdown is not None and breakdown.queue_total > 0:
    86→            block = format_queue_block(breakdown)
    87→            for text, style in block:
    88→                print(colorize(text, style))
    89→        if mode is ScoreDisplayMode.PHASE_TRANSITION:
    90→            print_objective_drained_banner(plan_start_strict, breakdown.queue_total, breakdown)
    91→    return breakdown

    93→
    94→def render_terminal_status(
    95→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    96→    *,
    97→    state: dict,
    98→    config: dict,

   100→    dim_scores: dict,

   104→) -> None:

   106→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
   107→    if skill_warning:
   108→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
   109→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   110→    if config_warning:
   111→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   112→
   113→    scores = score_snapshot(state)
   114→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
   115→    target_strict_score = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   116→
   117→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   118→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   119→
   120→    plan = load_plan()
   121→    active_plan = plan if (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters")) else None
   122→
   123→    print_triage_guardrail_info(plan=plan, state=state)
   124→
   125→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   126→        state,
   127→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="status", plan=active_plan),
   128→    )
   129→    ignores = config.get("ignore", [])
   130→
   131→    queue_ctx = queue_context(
   132→        state,
   133→        config=config,
   134→        plan=active_plan,

   160→    show_review_summary(state)

   165→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:

   167→

1→"""Shared triage guardrail helpers for command entrypoints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.display import short_issue_id
     9→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS, CommandError
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.triage import (
    12→    compute_new_issue_ids,
    13→    is_triage_stale,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import load_plan
    16→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (
    17→    TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE,
    18→    TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX,
    19→    triage_phase_banner,
    20→)
    21→
    22→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass
    26→class TriageGuardrailResult:
    27→    """Structured result from triage staleness detection."""
    28→
    29→    is_stale: bool = False
    30→    new_ids: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    31→    _plan: dict | None = field(default=None, repr=False)
    32→
    33→
    34→def triage_guardrail_status(
    35→    *,
    36→    plan: dict | None = None,
    37→    state: dict | None = None,
    38→) -> TriageGuardrailResult:
    39→    """Pure detection: is triage stale? Returns structured result, no side effects."""
    40→    try:
    41→        resolved_plan = plan if isinstance(plan, dict) else load_plan()
    42→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
    43→        logger.debug("Triage guardrail status skipped: plan could not be loaded.", exc_info=exc)
    44→        return TriageGuardrailResult()
    45→
    46→    resolved_state = state or {}
    47→
    48→    if not is_triage_stale(resolved_plan, resolved_state):
    49→        return TriageGuardrailResult(_plan=resolved_plan)
    50→
    51→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
    52→    if resolved_state:
    53→        new_ids = compute_new_issue_ids(resolved_plan, resolved_state)
    54→
    55→    return TriageGuardrailResult(is_stale=True, new_ids=new_ids, _plan=resolved_plan)
    56→
    57→
    58→def triage_guardrail_messages(
    59→    *,
    60→    plan: dict | None = None,
    61→    state: dict | None = None,
    62→) -> list[str]:
    63→    """Return warning strings without printing."""
    64→    resolved_state = state or {}
    65→    result = triage_guardrail_status(plan=plan, state=state)
    66→    if not result.is_stale:
    67→        return []
    68→
    69→    messages: list[str] = []
    70→    if result.new_ids:
    71→        messages.append(
    72→            f"{len(result.new_ids)} new review issue(s) not yet triaged."
    73→            " Run the staged triage runner to incorporate them "
    74→            f"(`{TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}` or `{TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}`)."
    75→        )
    76→
    77→    if result._plan is not None:
    78→        banner = triage_phase_banner(result._plan, resolved_state)
    79→        if banner:
    80→            messages.append(banner)
    81→
    82→    return messages
    83→
    84→
    85→def print_triage_guardrail_info(
    86→    *,
    87→    plan: dict | None = None,
    88→    state: dict | None = None,
    89→) -> bool:
    90→    """Print yellow info banner if triage is stale. Returns True if banner was shown."""
    91→    messages = triage_guardrail_messages(plan=plan, state=state)
    92→    for msg in messages:
    93→        print(colorize(f"  {msg}", "yellow"))
    94→    return bool(messages)
    95→
    96→
    97→def require_triage_current_or_exit(
    98→    *,
    99→    state: dict,
   100→    bypass: bool = False,
   101→    attest: str = "",
   102→) -> None:
   103→    """Gate: exit(1) if triage is stale and not bypassed. Name signals the exit."""
   104→    result = triage_guardrail_status(state=state)
   105→    if not result.is_stale:
   106→        return
   107→
   108→    if bypass and attest and len(attest.strip()) >= 30:
   109→        print(colorize(
   110→            "  Triage guardrail bypassed with attestation.",
   111→            "yellow",
   112→        ))
   113→        return
   114→
   115→    new_ids = result.new_ids
   116→    lines = [
   117→        f"BLOCKED: {len(new_ids) or 'some'} new review issue(s) have not been triaged."
   118→    ]
   119→    if new_ids:
   120→        for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:5]:
   121→            issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(fid, {})
   122→            lines.append(f"    * [{short_issue_id(fid)}] {issue.get('summary', '')}")
   123→        if len(new_ids) > 5:
   124→            lines.append(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 5} more")
   125→    lines.append("")
   126→    lines.append(f"  NEXT STEP (Codex):  {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}")
   127→    lines.append(f"  NEXT STEP (Claude): {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}")
   128→    lines.append("  Manual fallback:    desloppify plan triage")
   129→    lines.append("  (Review new issues, then either --confirm-existing or re-plan.)")
   130→    lines.append("")
   131→    lines.append("  View new execution items:  desloppify plan queue --sort recent")
   132→    lines.append("  View broader backlog:      desloppify backlog")
   133→    lines.append('  To bypass: --force-resolve --attest "I understand the plan may be stale..."')
   134→    raise CommandError("\n".join(lines))
   135→
   136→
   137→__all__ = [
   138→    "TriageGuardrailResult",
   139→    "print_triage_guardrail_info",
   140→    "require_triage_current_or_exit",
   141→    "triage_guardrail_messages",
   142→    "triage_guardrail_status",
   143→]
   144→

    46→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    47→
    48→
    49→def triage_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel, state: dict | None = None) -> str:
    50→    """Return a banner string describing triage status."""
    51→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    52→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    53→    has_triage = any(stage_id in order for stage_id in TRIAGE_IDS)
    54→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    55→    run_hint = (
    56→        f"Run: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX} "
    57→        f"(or {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE})"
    58→    )
    59→
    60→    if not has_triage:
    61→        undispositioned = meta.get("undispositioned_issue_count", 0)
    62→        if undispositioned:
    63→            return (
    64→                "TRIAGE RECOVERY NEEDED — "
    65→                f"{undispositioned} review issue(s) still need cluster/skip dispositions. "
    66→                f"{run_hint}"
    67→            )
    68→        if meta.get("triage_recommended"):
    69→            return (
    70→                "TRIAGE RECOMMENDED — review issues changed since last triage. "
    71→                f"{run_hint}"
    72→            )
    73→        return ""
    74→
    75→    if state and has_objective_backlog(state, None):
    76→        undispositioned = meta.get("undispositioned_issue_count", 0)
    77→        if undispositioned:
    78→            return (
    79→                "TRIAGE PENDING — "
    80→                f"{undispositioned} review issue(s) still need cluster/skip dispositions after current work. "
    81→                f"{run_hint}"
    82→            )
    83→        return (
    84→            "TRIAGE PENDING — queued and will activate after objective work "
    85→            "is complete."
    86→        )
    87→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    88→    completed = [
    89→        stage
    90→        for stage in ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check")
    91→        if stage in stages
    92→    ]
    93→    if completed:
    94→        return (
    95→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({len(completed)}/5 stages complete) — "
    96→            f"complete all stages to exit. {run_hint}"
    97→        )
    98→    return (
    99→        "TRIAGE MODE — review issues need analysis before fixing. "
   100→        f"{run_hint}"
   101→    )
   102→
   103→__all__ = [
   104→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD",
   105→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_CREATE",
   106→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH",
   107→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT",
   108→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_STEPS",

1→"""Unified queue-resolution context.
     2→
     3→A frozen ``QueueContext`` computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``plan`` / ``target_strict`` / ``policy`` threading through function chains.
     5→Callers build one context and pass it everywhere — makes the wrong thing
     6→impossible.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from dataclasses import dataclass
    12→
    13→from desloppify.base.config import (
    14→    DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    15→    target_strict_score_from_config,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.persistence import (
    18→    has_living_plan,
    19→    load_plan,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import PlanLoadStatus
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.snapshot import QueueSnapshot, build_queue_snapshot
    24→
    25→
    26→class _PlanAutoLoad:
    27→    """Sentinel type: auto-load plan from disk."""
    28→
    29→
    30→# Sentinel: "auto-load plan from disk" (the default).
    31→_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD = _PlanAutoLoad()
    32→PlanOption = dict | None | _PlanAutoLoad
    33→
    34→
    35→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    36→class QueueContext:
    37→    """Immutable snapshot of resolved queue parameters.
    38→
    39→    Built once via :func:`queue_context`, then threaded through queue
    40→    builders and command helpers so every call site agrees on plan,
    41→    target, and canonical queue facts.
    42→    """
    43→
    44→    plan: dict | None
    45→    target_strict: float
    46→    plan_load_status: PlanLoadStatus
    47→    snapshot: QueueSnapshot
    48→
    49→
    50→def resolve_plan_load_status(
    51→    *,
    52→    plan: PlanOption = _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD,
    53→) -> PlanLoadStatus:
    54→    """Resolve plan loading and explicitly report degraded mode."""
    55→    if not isinstance(plan, _PlanAutoLoad):
    56→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=plan, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    57→
    58→    if not has_living_plan():
    59→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=None, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    60→
    61→    try:
    62→        resolved_plan = load_plan()
    63→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=resolved_plan, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    64→    except OSError as exc:
    65→        return PlanLoadStatus(
    66→            plan=None,
    67→            degraded=True,
    68→            error_kind=exc.__class__.__name__,
    69→        )
    70→    except (
    71→        ValueError,
    72→        UnicodeDecodeError,
    73→    ) as exc:
    74→        return PlanLoadStatus(
    75→            plan=None,
    76→            degraded=True,
    77→            error_kind=exc.__class__.__name__,
    78→        )
    79→
    80→
    81→def queue_context(
    82→    state: StateModel,
    83→    *,
    84→    config: dict | None = None,
    85→    plan: PlanOption = _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD,
    86→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    87→) -> QueueContext:
    88→    """Build a :class:`QueueContext` with all parameters resolved.
    89→
    90→    Resolution order:
    91→
    92→    1. **plan** — explicit value wins; sentinel ``_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD`` triggers
    93→       ``load_plan()`` (guarded by ``PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS``).
    94→    2. **target_strict** — explicit float wins; ``None`` reads from *config*
    95→       via ``target_strict_score_from_config``; final fallback is ``95.0``.
    96→    3. **snapshot** — canonical queue phase and partitions derived from the
    97→       resolved plan and target_strict.
    98→    """
    99→    # --- resolve plan ---
   100→    plan_load_status = resolve_plan_load_status(plan=plan)
   101→    resolved_plan = plan_load_status.plan
   102→
   103→    # --- resolve target_strict ---
   104→    if target_strict is not None:
   105→        resolved_target = target_strict
   106→    elif config is not None:
   107→        resolved_target = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   108→    else:
   109→        resolved_target = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
   110→
   111→    snapshot = build_queue_snapshot(
   112→        state,
   113→        plan=resolved_plan,
   114→        target_strict=resolved_target,
   115→    )
   116→
   117→    return QueueContext(
   118→        plan=resolved_plan,
   119→        target_strict=resolved_target,
   120→        plan_load_status=plan_load_status,
   121→        snapshot=snapshot,
   122→    )
   123→
   124→
   125→__all__ = [
   126→    "PlanLoadStatus",
   127→    "QueueContext",
   128→    "queue_context",
   129→    "resolve_plan_load_status",
   130→]
   131→
