```
1→"""Canonical queue snapshot for phase and visibility decisions."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Any, Iterable
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (
    10→    WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
    11→    WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import path_scoped_issues
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import build_issue_items
    16→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic import (
    17→    build_subjective_items,
    18→    build_triage_stage_items,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic_workflow import (
    21→    build_communicate_score_item,
    22→    build_create_plan_item,
    23→    build_deferred_disposition_item,
    24→    build_import_scores_item,
    25→    build_run_scan_item,
    26→    build_score_checkpoint_item,
    27→)
    28→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    29→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    30→
    31→PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL = "review_initial"
    32→PHASE_EXECUTE = "execute"
    33→PHASE_SCAN = "scan"
    34→PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT = "review_postflight"
    35→PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT = "workflow_postflight"
    36→PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT = "triage_postflight"
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    40→class QueueSnapshot:
    41→    """Canonical queue facts and partitions for one invocation."""
    42→
    43→    phase: str
    44→    all_objective_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    45→    all_initial_review_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    46→    all_postflight_review_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    47→    all_scan_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    48→    all_postflight_workflow_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    49→    all_postflight_triage_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    50→    execution_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    51→    backlog_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    52→    objective_in_scope_count: int
    53→    objective_execution_count: int
    54→    objective_backlog_count: int
    55→    subjective_initial_count: int
    56→    subjective_postflight_count: int
    57→    workflow_postflight_count: int
    58→    triage_pending_count: int
    59→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers: bool
    60→
    61→
    62→def _option_value(options: object | None, name: str, default: Any) -> Any:
    63→    if options is None:
    64→        return default
    65→    return getattr(options, name, default)
    66→
    67→
    68→def _resolved_scan_path(options: object | None, state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    69→    scan_path = _option_value(options, "scan_path", state.get("scan_path"))
    70→    if hasattr(scan_path, "__class__") and scan_path.__class__.__name__ == "_ScanPathFromState":
    71→        return state.get("scan_path")
    72→    return scan_path
    73→
    74→
    75→def _tracked_plan_ids(plan: dict | None) -> set[str]:
    76→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
    77→        return set()
    78→    tracked: set[str] = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    79→    tracked.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    80→    tracked.update(plan.get("overrides", {}).keys())
    81→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    82→        tracked.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    83→        for step in cluster.get("action_steps", []):
    84→            if isinstance(step, dict):
    85→                tracked.update(step.get("issue_refs", []))
    86→    return tracked
    87→
    88→
    89→def _is_fresh_boundary(plan: dict | None) -> bool:
    90→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
    91→        return True
    92→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    93→    if not scores:
    94→        return True
    95→    return isinstance(scores, dict) and bool(scores.get("reset"))
    96→
    97→
    98→def _is_objective_item(item: WorkQueueItem, *, skipped_ids: set[str]) -> bool:
    99→    return (
   100→        item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
   101→        and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   102→        and item.get("id", "") not in skipped_ids
   103→    )
   104→
   105→
   106→def _review_issue_items(items: Iterable[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   107→    return [
   108→        item for item in items
   109→        if item.get("detector", "") in {"review", "concerns", "subjective_review"}
   110→    ]
   111→
   112→
   113→def _subjective_partitions(
   114→    state: StateModel,
   115→    *,
   116→    scoped_issues: dict[str, dict],
   117→    threshold: float,
   118→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   119→    candidates = build_subjective_items(state, scoped_issues, threshold=threshold)
   120→    initial = [item for item in candidates if item.get("initial_review")]
   121→    postflight = [item for item in candidates if not item.get("initial_review")]
   122→    return initial, postflight
   123→
   124→
   125→def _workflow_partitions(
   126→    plan: dict | None,
   127→    state: StateModel,
   128→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   129→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   130→        return [], [], []
   131→    scan_items = [
   132→        item
   133→        for item in (
   134→            build_deferred_disposition_item(plan),
   135→            build_run_scan_item(plan),
   136→        )
   137→        if item is not None
   138→    ]
   139→    postflight_workflow = [
   140→        item
   141→        for item in (
   142→            build_score_checkpoint_item(plan, state),
   143→            build_import_scores_item(plan, state),
   144→            build_communicate_score_item(plan, state),
   145→            build_create_plan_item(plan),
   146→        )
   147→        if item is not None
   148→    ]
   149→    triage_items = build_triage_stage_items(plan, state)
   150→    return scan_items, postflight_workflow, triage_items
   151→
   152→
   153→def _phase_for_snapshot(
   154→    *,
   155→    fresh_boundary: bool,
   156→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   157→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   158→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   159→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   160→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   161→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   162→) -> str:
   163→    if fresh_boundary and initial_review_items:
   164→        return PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL
   165→    if objective_items:
   166→        return PHASE_EXECUTE
   167→    if scan_items:
   168→        return PHASE_SCAN
   169→    if postflight_review_items:
   170→        return PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT
   171→    if postflight_workflow_items:
   172→        return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   173→    if triage_items:
   174→        return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   175→    return PHASE_SCAN
   176→
   177→
   178→def _execution_items_for_phase(
   179→    phase: str,
   180→    *,
   181→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   182→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   183→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   184→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   185→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   186→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   187→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   188→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL:
   189→        return initial_review_items
   190→    if phase == PHASE_EXECUTE:
   191→        return objective_items
   192→    if phase == PHASE_SCAN:
   193→        deferred_items = [
   194→            item for item in scan_items
   195→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID
   196→        ]
   197→        if deferred_items:
   198→            return deferred_items
   199→        return [
   200→            item for item in scan_items
   201→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID
   202→        ]
   203→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   204→        return postflight_review_items
   205→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   206→        return postflight_workflow_items
   207→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   208→        return triage_items
   209→    return []
   210→
   211→
   212→def build_queue_snapshot(
   213→    state: StateModel,
   214→    *,
   215→    options: object | None = None,
   216→    plan: dict | None = None,
   217→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   218→) -> QueueSnapshot:
   219→    """Build the canonical queue snapshot for the current state."""
   220→    context = _option_value(options, "context", None)
   221→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else (
   222→        plan if plan is not None else _option_value(options, "plan", None)
   223→    )
   224→    scan_path = _resolved_scan_path(options, state)
   225→    skipped_ids = set((effective_plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   226→    tracked_ids = _tracked_plan_ids(effective_plan)
   227→    scoped_issues = path_scoped_issues(state.get("issues", {}), scan_path)
   228→    scope = _option_value(options, "scope", None)
   229→    chronic = bool(_option_value(options, "chronic", False))
   230→
   231→    all_issue_items = build_issue_items(
   232→        state,
   233→        scan_path=scan_path,
   234→        status_filter="open",
   235→        scope=scope,
   236→        chronic=chronic,
   237→    )
   238→    objective_items = [
   239→        item for item in all_issue_items
   240→        if _is_objective_item(item, skipped_ids=skipped_ids)
   241→    ]
   242→    review_issue_items = _review_issue_items(all_issue_items)
   243→    initial_review_items, subjective_postflight_items = _subjective_partitions(
   244→        state,
   245→        scoped_issues=scoped_issues,
   246→        threshold=target_strict,
   247→    )
   248→    postflight_review_items = [*subjective_postflight_items, *review_issue_items]
   249→    scan_items, postflight_workflow_items, triage_items = _workflow_partitions(
   250→        effective_plan,
   251→        state,
   252→    )
   253→
   254→    fresh_boundary = _is_fresh_boundary(effective_plan)
   255→    phase = _phase_for_snapshot(
   256→        fresh_boundary=fresh_boundary,
   257→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   258→        objective_items=objective_items,
   259→        scan_items=scan_items,
   260→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   261→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   262→        triage_items=triage_items,
   263→    )
   264→    execution_items = _execution_items_for_phase(
   265→        phase,
   266→        objective_items=objective_items,
   267→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   268→        scan_items=scan_items,
   269→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   270→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   271→        triage_items=triage_items,
   272→    )
   273→
   274→    execution_ids = {item.get("id", "") for item in execution_items}
   275→    backlog_items = [
   276→        item for item in (
   277→            [*objective_items, *initial_review_items, *postflight_review_items, *scan_items, *postflight_workflow_items, *triage_items]
   278→        )
   279→        if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   280→    ]
   281→    objective_backlog_count = sum(
   282→        1 for item in objective_items if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   283→    )
   284→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers = any(
   285→        item.get("id", "") not in tracked_ids for item in objective_items
   286→    )
   287→
   288→    return QueueSnapshot(
   289→        phase=phase,
   290→        all_objective_items=tuple(objective_items),
   291→        all_initial_review_items=tuple(initial_review_items),
   292→        all_postflight_review_items=tuple(postflight_review_items),
   293→        all_scan_items=tuple(scan_items),
   294→        all_postflight_workflow_items=tuple(postflight_workflow_items),
   295→        all_postflight_triage_items=tuple(triage_items),
   296→        execution_items=tuple(execution_items),
   297→        backlog_items=tuple(backlog_items),
   298→        objective_in_scope_count=len(objective_items),
   299→        objective_execution_count=sum(
   300→            1
   301→            for item in execution_items
   302→            if item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
   303→            and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   304→        ),
   305→        objective_backlog_count=objective_backlog_count,
   306→        subjective_initial_count=len(initial_review_items),
   307→        subjective_postflight_count=len(postflight_review_items),
   308→        workflow_postflight_count=len(postflight_workflow_items),
   309→        triage_pending_count=len(triage_items),
   310→        has_unplanned_objective_blockers=has_unplanned_objective_blockers,
   311→    )
   312→
   313→
   314→def coarse_phase_name(phase: str) -> str:
   315→    """Map internal queue phases to the persisted coarse lifecycle value."""
   316→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL or phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   317→        return "review"
   318→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   319→        return "workflow"
   320→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   321→        return "triage"
   322→    return phase
   323→
   324→
   325→__all__ = [
   326→    "PHASE_EXECUTE",
   327→    "PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL",
   328→    "PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT",
   329→    "PHASE_SCAN",
   330→    "PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT",
   331→    "PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT",
   332→    "QueueSnapshot",
   333→    "build_queue_snapshot",
   334→    "coarse_phase_name",
   335→]
   336→
```

```
1→"""backlog command: show open backlog items outside the execution queue."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_issue_inventory
    11→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    12→from desloppify.base.tooling import check_config_staleness
    13→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import load_plan
    14→from desloppify.engine.planning.queue_policy import build_backlog_queue
    15→
    16→from desloppify.app.commands.next.queue_flow import build_and_render_backlog_queue
    17→
    18→
    19→def cmd_backlog(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    20→    """Show backlog items that are not currently part of the execution queue."""
    21→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    22→    state = runtime.state
    23→    config = runtime.config
    24→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
    25→        return
    26→
    27→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    28→    if config_warning:
    29→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    30→
    31→    build_and_render_backlog_queue(
    32→        args,
    33→        state,
    34→        config,
    35→        resolve_lang_fn=resolve_lang,
    36→        load_plan_fn=load_plan,
    37→        build_work_queue_fn=build_backlog_queue,
    38→        write_query_fn=write_query,
    39→    )
    40→
    41→
    42→__all__ = ["build_and_render_backlog_queue", "cmd_backlog"]
    43→
```

```
1→"""Shared helpers for queue-sync decisions.
     2→
     3→Every sync module (``sync_triage``, ``sync_dimensions``, ``sync_workflow``,
     4→``auto_cluster_sync``) needs to answer two questions:
     5→
     6→1. **Is there an objective backlog?**  (Should we defer optional items?)
     7→2. **Are we mid-cycle?**  (Has a queue cycle started and not yet completed?)
     8→
     9→This module provides a single definition of each so the logic stays
    10→consistent and changes propagate everywhere at once.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    17→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS as _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    20→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→def has_objective_backlog(
    26→    state_or_issues: StateModel | dict,
    27→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
    28→) -> bool:
    29→    """Return whether an objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues).
    30→
    31→    Prefers the pre-computed *policy* snapshot when available.
    32→    Falls back to scanning *state_or_issues* directly — accepts either a
    33→    full ``StateModel`` (looks up ``issues``) or a raw issues dict.
    34→    """
    35→    if policy is not None:
    36→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
    37→
    38→    # Accept either full state payload (`{"issues": ...}`) or raw issues dict.
    39→    issues = state_or_issues
    40→    if isinstance(state_or_issues, dict):
    41→        maybe_issues = state_or_issues.get("issues")
    42→        if isinstance(maybe_issues, dict):
    43→            issues = maybe_issues
    44→    return any(
    45→        f.get("status") == "open"
    46→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    47→        and not f.get("suppressed")
    48→        for f in issues.values()
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def is_mid_cycle(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    53→    """True when a queue cycle is in progress.
    54→
    55→    A cycle is active when ``plan_start_scores`` is set to real score
    56→    values.  The ``{"reset": True}`` sentinel (set by lifecycle reset)
    57→    does NOT count as mid-cycle — it means "seed real scores on next scan".
    58→    """
    59→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    60→    return bool(scores) and not (isinstance(scores, dict) and scores.get("reset"))
    61→
    62→
    63→__all__ = ["has_objective_backlog", "is_mid_cycle"]
    64→
```

```
1→"""Queue-policy helpers shared by planning render/select modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import replace
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import queue_context
    10→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
    11→    QueueBuildOptions,
    12→    QueueVisibility,
    13→    WorkQueueResult,
    14→    _build_work_queue_with_visibility,
    15→    build_work_queue,
    16→)
    17→
    18→
    19→def _subjective_threshold(state: StateModel, *, default: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE) -> float:
    20→    config = state.get("config", {})
    21→    raw_target = default
    22→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    23→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", default)
    24→    try:
    25→        value = float(raw_target)
    26→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    27→        value = default
    28→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, value))
    29→
    30→
    31→def _queue_plan_from_options(options: QueueBuildOptions) -> dict | None:
    32→    if options.context is not None:
    33→        return options.context.plan
    34→    return options.plan
    35→
    36→
    37→def build_open_plan_queue(
    38→    state: StateModel,
    39→    *,
    40→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    41→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    42→    """Build one open-status queue with consistent planning policy defaults."""
    43→    opts = replace(options) if options is not None else QueueBuildOptions(count=None)
    44→    if options is None:
    45→        opts = replace(opts, status="open", include_subjective=True)
    46→    elif opts.status == QueueBuildOptions().status:
    47→        opts = replace(opts, status="open")
    48→    if opts.subjective_threshold == QueueBuildOptions().subjective_threshold:
    49→        opts = replace(opts, subjective_threshold=_subjective_threshold(state))
    50→    return build_work_queue(
    51→        state,
    52→        options=opts,
    53→    )
    54→
    55→
    56→def build_execution_queue(
    57→    state: StateModel,
    58→    *,
    59→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    60→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    61→    """Build the execution queue that follows the living plan when present."""
    62→    options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    63→    ctx = options.context or queue_context(
    64→        state,
    65→        plan=_queue_plan_from_options(options),
    66→        target_strict=_subjective_threshold(state),
    67→    )
    68→    return _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    69→        state,
    70→        options=replace(options, context=ctx),
    71→        visibility=QueueVisibility.EXECUTION,
    72→    )
    73→
    74→
    75→def build_backlog_queue(
    76→    state: StateModel,
    77→    *,
    78→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    79→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    80→    """Build the broader backlog, excluding work already tracked in the plan."""
    81→    options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    82→    ctx = options.context or queue_context(
    83→        state,
    84→        plan=_queue_plan_from_options(options),
    85→        target_strict=_subjective_threshold(state),
    86→    )
    87→    return _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    88→        state,
    89→        options=replace(options, context=ctx),
    90→        visibility=QueueVisibility.BACKLOG,
    91→    )
    92→
    93→
    94→__all__ = [
    95→    "build_backlog_queue",
    96→    "build_execution_queue",
    97→    "build_open_plan_queue",
    98→]
    99→
```

```
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import QueueContext
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.finalize import finalize_queue
     7→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.inputs import resolve_queue_inputs
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.models import (

    20→def build_work_queue(
    21→    state: StateModel,
    22→    *,
    23→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    24→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    25→    """Build the active canonical work queue for the current invocation."""
    26→    return _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    27→        state,
    28→        options=options,
    29→        visibility=QueueVisibility.ALL,

    32→
    33→def _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    34→    state: StateModel,
    35→    *,
    36→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    37→    visibility: str = QueueVisibility.ALL,
    38→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    39→    """Build a ranked work queue from canonical queue partitions.
    40→
    41→    Open-status queue views are resolved from the canonical queue snapshot so
    42→    lifecycle phase, execution visibility, and backlog visibility all come
    43→    from one source of truth. Non-open status views remain issue-list queries.
    44→    """

    46→    plan, scan_path, status, threshold = resolve_queue_inputs(opts, state)
    47→    items = select_queue_items(

    49→        opts=opts,
    50→        plan=plan,
    51→        scan_path=scan_path,
    52→        status=status,
    53→        threshold=threshold,
    54→        visibility=visibility,
    55→    )
    56→    return finalize_queue(items, state=state, plan=plan, opts=opts)
```

```
1→"""Queue item selection from canonical snapshot or raw issue lists."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import scope_matches
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.inputs import gather_subjective_items
     7→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.models import QueueBuildOptions, QueueVisibility
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import build_issue_items
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.snapshot import build_queue_snapshot
    10→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    12→
    13→
    14→def select_queue_items(
    15→    state: StateModel,
    16→    *,
    17→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    18→    plan: dict | None,
    19→    scan_path: str | None,
    20→    status: str,
    21→    threshold: float,
    22→    visibility: str,
    23→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    24→    """Select the raw queue item list before ranking/finalization."""
    25→    if status == "open" and not opts.chronic:
    26→        snapshot = build_queue_snapshot(
    27→            state,
    28→            options=opts,
    29→            plan=plan,
    30→            target_strict=threshold,
    31→        )
    32→        return filter_snapshot_items(
    33→            items_for_visibility(snapshot=snapshot, visibility=visibility),
    34→            opts,
    35→        )
    36→
    37→    items = build_issue_items(
    38→        state,
    39→        scan_path=scan_path,
    40→        status_filter=status,
    41→        scope=opts.scope,
    42→        chronic=opts.chronic,
    43→    )
    44→    if opts.include_subjective and status == "all":
    45→        items += gather_subjective_items(state, opts, threshold)
    46→    return items
    47→
    48→
    49→def items_for_visibility(*, snapshot, visibility: str) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    50→    """Select the snapshot partition for one queue surface."""
    51→    if visibility == QueueVisibility.BACKLOG:
    52→        return [dict(item) for item in snapshot.backlog_items]
    53→    return [dict(item) for item in snapshot.execution_items]
    54→
    55→
    56→def filter_snapshot_items(
    57→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    58→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    59→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    60→    """Apply view-local filtering after snapshot partition selection."""
    61→    filtered = items
    62→    if not opts.include_subjective:
    63→        filtered = [
    64→            item for item in filtered
    65→            if item.get("kind") != "subjective_dimension"
    66→        ]
    67→    if opts.scope:
    68→        filtered = [
    69→            item for item in filtered
    70→            if item.get("kind") != "subjective_dimension" or scope_matches(item, opts.scope)
    71→        ]
    72→    return filtered
    73→
    74→
    75→__all__ = [
    76→    "filter_snapshot_items",
    77→    "items_for_visibility",
    78→    "select_queue_items",
    79→]
    80→
```

```
    50→def _build_next_payload(

    52→    queue: dict,

    71→    )
    72→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [

    74→    ]
    75→    return payload

   131→def _render_empty_queue_view(
   132→    *,
   133→    queue: dict,

   174→def _render_terminal_queue_view(
   175→    *,
   176→    queue: dict,
   177→    items: list[dict],
   178→    state: dict,
   179→    opts: NextOptions,
   180→    plan_for_queue: dict,

   187→) -> None:

   193→    )

   201→        )

   203→
   204→    _render_queue_header(queue, opts.explain)
   205→    strict_score = state_mod.score_snapshot(state).strict
   206→    if _show_empty_queue(

   211→    ):
   212→        return
   213→
   214→    potentials = _merge_potentials_safe(state.get("potentials", {}))
   215→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   216→        items,
   217→        dim_scores,
   218→        issues_scoped,
   219→        group=opts.group,
   220→        explain=opts.explain,
   221→        potentials=potentials,
   222→        plan=plan_data,
   223→        cluster_filter=effective_cluster,
   224→    )
   225→    next_nudges_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   226→    if show_plan_context:
   227→        next_nudges_mod.render_uncommitted_reminder(plan_data)
   228→        next_nudges_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   229→            state,

   231→            issues_scoped,
   232→            strict_score=strict_score,
   233→            target_strict_score=target_strict,
   234→            queue_total=queue_total,
   235→            plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   236→            breakdown=breakdown,
   237→        )
   238→    print()
   239→
   240→    if items and plan_data and show_execution_prompt:
   241→        print_user_message(

   244→            " `desloppify plan show`."
   245→        )

   249→    if not show_plan_context:

   286→            context=ctx,
   287→        ),
   288→    )
   289→    return ctx, queue, queue.get("items", []), effective_cluster
```

Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive understanding. Let me create a detailed report.

## Complete Queue System Structure and Architecture

Based on my thorough exploration of the queue system, here's the full structure:

### 1. **Queue Item Types and Kinds**

The queue system recognizes 5 main item kinds (from `WorkQueueItem` TypedDict in `types.py`):

- **`"issue"`** – Real detector issues from the state, either mechanical or subjective
- **`"cluster"`** – Meta-items that aggregate multiple issue members (both auto and manual)
- **`"workflow_stage"`** – Triage workflow stages (observe, reflect, organize, enrich, sense-check, commit)
- **`"workflow_action"`** – Synthetic workflow items (score checkpoints, create-plan, etc.)
- **`"subjective_dimension"`** – Synthetic dimension items for unassessed or postflight subjective review

### 2. **Queue Phases (Lifecycle Partitions)**

`snapshot.py` defines 6 canonical phases that form the **queue execution lifecycle**:

1. **`PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL`** – Unassessed subjective dimensions (when `fresh_boundary=True`)
2. **`PHASE_EXECUTE`** – Active objective issues (planned or unplanned mechanical work)
3. **`PHASE_SCAN`** – Deferred disposition OR run-scan workflow items
4. **`PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT`** – Postflight subjective dimensions and review issues
5. **`PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT`** – Score checkpoint, import-scores, communicate-score, create-plan
6. **`PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT`** – Triage workflow stages

The phase is determined by `_phase_for_snapshot()` — it picks the **first non-empty phase** in this order. This creates a waterfall: once initial review items drain, execution begins; once execution items drain, scan runs; etc.

### 3. **Execution vs. Backlog Visibility**

The queue snapshot splits items into two visibility modes:

- **`execution_items`** – Items active in the current phase (what `desloppify next` shows by default)
  - For PHASE_EXECUTE: all objective items
  - For PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL: initial review items
  - For PHASE_SCAN: deferred disposition (if present), otherwise run-scan
  - For PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT: postflight review items
  - For PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT: workflow items
  - For PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT: triage items

- **`backlog_items`** – Everything else (everything except current execution items)
  - These are surfaced by the fallback queue view (not normally shown, but available when execution queue drains)

### 4. **Execution Mode Fallback (Critical!)**

In `queue_policy.py`, there are **three queue builders**:

1. **`build_open_plan_queue()`** – All open-status items (used for `plan` command)
2. **`build_execution_queue()`** – Only **execution items** from the current phase (used for `next` command)
3. **`build_backlog_queue()`** – Only **backlog items** outside the current phase

When the **execution queue drains empty**, the fallback is implicit: the backlog queue contains the next phase's items. The architecture does **not** require explicit "execution mode fallback" — instead, the phase advances automatically as the active partition empties, and `next` continues to show the new phase's items.

### 5. **Plan-Tracked vs. Unplanned Work**

Key distinction in `snapshot.py`:

- **`tracked_ids`** – Set of issue IDs explicitly listed in `plan.queue_order`, `plan.skipped`, `plan.overrides`, or cluster members
- **`has_unplanned_objective_blockers`** – Boolean flag: `True` if any objective issue in scope is **not** in `tracked_ids`

This flags when unplanned work is blocking execution. The `next` command can surface these unplanned blockers when the plan-tracked items drain.

### 6. **Ranking and Sort Order**

`ranking.py` implements a three-tier sort system (`item_sort_key`):

1. **Primary tier (plan-aware)**:
   - `_TIER_PLANNED = 0` – Items with explicit plan position (from `queue_order`)
   - `_TIER_EXISTING = 1` – Known items without plan position
   - `_TIER_NEW = 2` – Newly discovered items (from latest scan)

2. **Secondary tier (natural ranking)**:
   - `_RANK_INITIAL_REVIEW = -3` – Unassessed subjective dimensions
   - `_RANK_WORKFLOW = -2` – Score checkpoints, create-plan
   - `_RANK_TRIAGE_STAGE = -1` – Triage workflow stages
   - `_RANK_CLUSTER = 0` – Auto-clustered issues
   - `_RANK_ISSUE = 1` – Individual issues

3. **Tertiary criteria**:
   - Within each tier: subjective score (lower is better), issue ID
   - Blocked workflow items sort after unblocked

### 7. **Plan States: Skipped, Deferred, and Deferred Disposition**

**Skipped items** (in `plan.skipped`):
- Each entry has a `kind` ("temporary" or "permanent") and optional `reason`
- Skipped items are **filtered out** during presort (`separate_skipped()`)
- Can be re-included with `--include-skipped` flag

**Deferred work**:
- Not explicitly modeled as a separate state
- Instead, items can be deferred via the plan's `overrides` or by moving items out of `queue_order`

**Deferred disposition** (`workflow::deferred-disposition` ID):
- A synthetic workflow action item that surfaces when the plan has deferred issues
- Built by `build_deferred_disposition_item()` in `synthetic_workflow.py`
- If present during PHASE_SCAN, it becomes the execution item (takes priority over `run-scan`)

### 8. **Queue Builder Flow (end-to-end)**

```
build_work_queue()  [or build_execution_queue()]
    ↓
resolve_queue_inputs()  – Extract plan, scan_path, status, subjective_threshold from options
    ↓
select_queue_items()
    ├─ If status == "open" && not chronic:
    │   ├─ build_queue_snapshot()  – Build canonical phases + partitions
    │   ├─ items_for_visibility()  – Select execution or backlog items
    │   └─ filter_snapshot_items() – Apply scope/subjective filters
    └─ Otherwise: build_issue_items() – Raw issue list (for "all"/"closed" status)
    ↓
finalize_queue()
    ├─ enrich_with_impact()  – Stamp estimated_impact based on dimension headroom
    ├─ _plan_presort()
    │   ├─ enrich_plan_metadata()  – Add plan description/note/cluster info
    │   ├─ stamp_plan_sort_keys() – Stamp _plan_position, _is_new
    │   └─ separate_skipped()     – Remove skipped items temporarily
    ├─ items.sort(key=item_sort_key)  – Apply three-tier ranking
    ├─ _plan_postsort()
    │   ├─ Re-append skipped (if --include-skipped)
    │   └─ stamp_positions()  – Assign queue_position for display
    └─ Return { items, total, grouped, new_ids }
```

### 9. **How Snapshot is Built (snapshot.py)**

The snapshot building process creates the canonical queue structure:

```python
build_queue_snapshot(state, options, plan, target_strict)
    ├─ Resolve scan_path, plan, subjective_threshold
    ├─ Track which IDs are in the plan (tracked_ids, skipped_ids)
    ├─ build_issue_items()  – Create issue items from state
    ├─ Partition objective items: filter out skipped, non-objective detectors
    ├─ _subjective_partitions() – Split subjective items into initial vs postflight
    ├─ _review_issue_items() – Extract review/concerns detector items
    ├─ _workflow_partitions() – Build deferred disposition, run-scan, workflow items
    ├─ _phase_for_snapshot() – Determine current phase (waterfall logic)
    ├─ _execution_items_for_phase() – Select items for execution in current phase
    ├─ Compute backlog = all items - execution items
    └─ Return QueueSnapshot(phase, all_*, execution_items, backlog_items, counts)
```

### 10. **`next` Command Flow**

```
cmd_next(args)
    ↓
build_and_render_execution_queue(args, state, config)
    ├─ NextOptions.from_args(args)  – Parse --count, --cluster, --scope, etc.
    ├─ load_plan()  – Auto-load or explicit plan
    ├─ queue_context() – Build unified context with snapshot
    ├─ build_execution_queue(state, options)  – Get only execution items
    ├─ collapse_clusters() – Collapse cluster members if plan present
    ├─ Optionally slice first N items (--count)
    └─ Render terminal or JSON output with narrative coaching
```

The key insight: **`next` always shows the current phase's execution items**. When they drain, the phase automatically advances and `next` shows the next phase.

### 11. **Distinction Summary: Backlog, Planned, Deferred, Skipped**

| Concept | How Tracked | Where Visible |
|---------|-------------|---------------|
| **Backlog** | All items except execution_items in current phase | `backlog_queue`, implicit fallback |
| **Planned** | Items in `plan.queue_order` | Execution queue (if in current phase), plan metadata |
| **Deferred** | Items removed from `queue_order` or via overrides | Backlog, or resurfaced via deferred disposition |
| **Skipped** | Items in `plan.skipped` dict | Hidden by default, visible with `--include-skipped` |
| **Unplanned** | Issues in scope but NOT in `tracked_ids` | Flagged as `has_unplanned_objective_blockers` |

### Key Files

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/snapshot.py` – Phase logic, snapshot building, partitioning
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` – Queue builder entrypoints
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/selection.py` – Phase-aware item filtering
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py` – Three-tier sort key logic
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/plan_order.py` – Cluster collapse, plan enrichment
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/finalize.py` – Ranking, plan presort/postsort
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/queue_policy.py` – High-level queue builders
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next/queue_flow.py` – `next` command rendering pipeline
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/constants.py` – Workflow IDs, triage stage specs

## Report: Desloppify Skip, Deferral, and Postflight System

Based on my exploration of the codebase, here's how desloppify handles skipping, deferral, and the postflight sequence:

### 1. SKIP SYSTEM

**Skip Kinds** (`override_skip.py`, `skip_policy.py`):
- **temporary**: Defers work; can be auto-resurfaced after N scans via `review_after` parameter. User must confirm with attestation: "I have actually reflected and I am not deferring this for lazy reasons."
- **permanent**: Marks as wontfix (won't fix) — requires a `--note` explaining why and attestation confirming review
- **false_positive**: Marks as false positive — requires attestation confirming review was thorough

**Skip Operations** (`engine/_plan/operations/skip.py`, `plan_ops.py`):
- `skip_items()`: Moves issues from `queue_order` to `skipped` dict with SkipEntry metadata (kind, reason, note, attestation, created_at, skipped_at_scan)
- `unskip_items()`: Moves issues back from `skipped` to `queue_order`
- `resurface_stale_skips()`: Auto-resurfaces temporarily skipped items when scan count exceeds the `skipped_at_scan + review_after` threshold
- Each skip entry stores: `kind`, `reason`, `note`, `attestation`, `created_at`, `review_after` (N scans), `skipped_at_scan` (state.scan_count when skipped)

**Skip Validation** (`override_skip.py`):
- Bulk skip warning: > 5 items requires `--confirm`
- Temporary skip requires `--confirm` + attestation
- Permanent skip requires `--note` + attestation
- When skip/unskip happens, `clear_postflight_scan_completion()` is called to force a fresh postflight scan

---

### 2. DEFERRED SYSTEM

**What "Deferred" Means**:
- **Not a data field** — there is no separate "deferred" status in the plan schema
- **Operational definition**: Issues with `skip.kind == "temporary"` are conceptually "deferred" work
- The plan schema has an empty `deferred: list[str]` field (line 191 in schema/__init__.py) kept for migration compatibility but not actively used

**Key Functions** (`synthetic_workflow.py`):
- `_temporary_skipped_ids(plan)`: Collects all issues with `skip.kind == "temporary"` from `plan.skipped`
- `_deferred_cluster_breakdown(plan, deferred_ids)`: Counts how many deferred items belong to clusters vs. are standalone
- `build_deferred_disposition_item(plan)`: Creates synthetic workflow item prompting user to decide on deferred backlog

**Deferred Disposition Workflow Item** (`WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID = "workflow::deferred-disposition"`):
- Appears in the **scan phase** when there are temporarily skipped items awaiting decision
- Provides two paths:
  1. **Reactivate**: `desloppify plan unskip "*"` — brings deferred work back into active queue
  2. **Convert to wontfix**: `desloppify plan skip --permanent ...` — permanently marks deferred items as wontfix
- Includes detailed breakdown: cluster count, individual count, planning tools for review and inspection

---

### 3. POSTFLIGHT/ENDING SEQUENCE

The postflight flow is driven by **lifecycle phases** and **queue snapshots** (`refresh_lifecycle.py`, `snapshot.py`):

**Lifecycle Phases** (in order of progression):
```
LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW     → Initial subjective review
LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE    → Resolve objective issues
LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN       → Post-flight scan (re-balance, surface new work)
LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW     → Secondary (postflight) review rounds
LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW   → Workflow actions (score checkpoints, communications, etc.)
LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE     → Multi-stage triage planning
```

**Postflight Scan Phase**:
- Triggered when `postflight_scan_pending(plan)` → checks if `refresh_state.postflight_scan_completed_at_scan_count` is unset
- Builds two synthetic items (via `_workflow_partitions()`):
  1. `build_deferred_disposition_item()` — if there are temporary skips, shown first
  2. `build_run_scan_item()` — if postflight scan is pending (WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID)

**Queue Snapshot Phases** (`snapshot.py` constants):
```
PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL       → Subjective dimension initialization
PHASE_EXECUTE              → Objective issues from queue_order
PHASE_SCAN                 → Deferred disposition + run postflight scan
PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT    → Follow-up review work (subjective items created after execute)
PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT  → Score checkpoints, import scores, communicate, create plan
PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT    → Triage stage items (observe, reflect, organize, enrich, sense-check, commit)
```

**Resolution Method** (`resolve_lifecycle_phase()`, lines 96–122 in refresh_lifecycle.py):
```python
if has_initial_reviews:          → REVIEW
if has_objective_backlog:        → EXECUTE
if has_deferred or postflight_scan_pending(plan):  → SCAN (deferred disposition comes here)
if has_postflight_review:        → REVIEW (postflight subjective)
if has_postflight_workflow:      → WORKFLOW (score checkpoints, etc.)
if has_triage:                   → TRIAGE
otherwise:                       → SCAN (default)
```

---

### 4. SYNTHETIC WORKFLOW ITEMS

**All Synthetic Items** (defined in `synthetic_workflow.py`, `synthetic.py`):

1. **WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID** (`build_deferred_disposition_item`):
   - Tier: 1, Confidence: high, Kind: workflow_action
   - Execution visibility: always
   - Summary: "Deferred backlog decision required: X clusters + Y individual items must be reactivated or marked wontfix."
   - Appears in **scan phase** when temp skips exist
   - Primary command: `desloppify plan unskip "*"` (reactivate all)
   - Alternative: convert to permanent skip

2. **WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID** (`build_run_scan_item`):
   - Tier: 1, Confidence: high, Kind: workflow_action
   - Execution visibility: always
   - Summary: "Run post-flight scan to refresh queue and surface follow-up review work."
   - Appears in **scan phase** when `postflight_scan_pending(plan)` is true
   - Primary command: `desloppify scan`

3. **WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID** (`build_score_checkpoint_item`):
   - Summarizes score progress (delta from plan start)
   - Appears when queued and in **workflow postflight**

4. **WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID** (`build_import_scores_item`):
   - For re-importing review assessments with attestation
   - Appears when queued and in **workflow postflight**

5. **WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID** (`build_communicate_score_item`):
   - For broadcasting final score to user
   - Appears when queued and in **workflow postflight**

6. **WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID** (`build_create_plan_item`):
   - Multi-stage triage planning (observe → reflect → organize → enrich → sense-check → commit)
   - Appears when queued and in **workflow postflight**

7. **Triage Stage Items** (`build_triage_stage_items`):
   - Each stage (observe, reflect, organize, enrich, sense-check, commit) is a synthetic item
   - Appears in **triage postflight** when triage stages are queued
   - Tracks stage dependencies to block dependent stages

8. **Subjective Dimension Items** (`build_subjective_items`):
   - Created for dimensions below target strict score
   - Partitioned into "initial" (first assessment) vs. "postflight" (reassessment after execution)
   - Marked with `initial_review` flag

---

### 5. PREFLIGHT CHECKS

**Review Rerun Preflight** (`review/preflight.py`):
- Blocks re-running subjective review if backlog remains (objective + subjective queue work)
- Unless `--force-review-rerun` is set
- Clears `needs_review_refresh` markers on targeted dimensions before rerun
- Counts open objective issues and open subjective review items for blocking dimensions

---

### 6. PLAN SCHEMA DEFERRED FIELD

**Schema Location** (`_plan/schema/__init__.py`):
```python
deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
```
- Line 191: Empty list, kept only for backward-compatibility with older plans
- **Active storage**: Temporary skips are in `skipped[issue_id].kind == "temporary"`
- No active use of the `deferred` field; all deferral tracking is via the skip system

---

### Summary Diagram

```
Queue Lifecycle:
  ┌─ Initial Review (subjective dimensions)
  ├─ Execute (objective backlog)
  ├─ Scan ←────────────────┐  (postflight)
  │  ├─ build_deferred_disposition_item (if temp skips)
  │  └─ build_run_scan_item (if postflight_scan_pending)
  │
  ├─ Postflight Review (new subjective items discovered during execute)
  │
  ├─ Workflow Postflight (score checkpoints, import, communicate, plan creation)
  │
  └─ Triage Postflight (multi-stage planning)

Skip System:
  queue_order ←→ skipped (via skip_items/unskip_items)
  
  temporary → resurface after review_after scans
  permanent → wontfix (requires note + attestation)
  false_positive → skip (requires attestation)
```

---

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_skip.py` — CLI skip/unskip commands
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations/skip.py` — Skip implementation
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/synthetic_workflow.py` — Deferred disposition + workflow items
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/refresh_lifecycle.py` — Lifecycle phase resolution

## Comprehensive Report: Backlog, Blockers, and Postflight Transitions

### 1. What "Backlog" Means in the Current System

**Backlog** is the set of work items that are NOT currently part of the execution queue. It's defined in two contexts:

**Snapshot-based definition** (`snapshot.py`):
- **Execution items**: The items currently being worked on (determined by the queue phase)
- **Backlog items**: All other items in the broader set (objective items, initial review items, postflight review items, scan items, postflight workflow items, triage items) that are NOT in `execution_items`

**Command-based definition**:
- `desloppify next` shows **execution queue** (only the current work phase)
- `desloppify backlog` shows **backlog queue** (everything else) via `build_backlog_queue()`
- These are two visibility modes over the same snapshot: `QueueVisibility.EXECUTION` vs `QueueVisibility.BACKLOG`

**Objective backlog** (what "blocks" postflight transitions):
- Open issues with detectors NOT in `NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS` (e.g., "smells", "concerns", not "review" or "subjective_review")
- Not suppressed
- Not in the `skipped` set of the plan

The function `has_objective_backlog(state_or_issues, policy)` in `/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/context.py` is the canonical check.

### 2. How the System Decides If Objective Work Is "Done"

**Phase Transitions** happen via `_phase_for_snapshot()` in `snapshot.py` (lines 153-176):

The queue moves through phases in this order:
1. **PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL** — if fresh boundary + initial review items
2. **PHASE_EXECUTE** — if objective items exist (the work phase)
3. **PHASE_SCAN** — run scan/defer disposition
4. **PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT** — subjective/review items after objective work
5. **PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT** — score checkpoint, import scores, communicate, create plan
6. **PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT** — triage stages

**What blocks postflight transitions**:
- **Unplanned objective blockers**: Any objective item with an ID NOT in the plan's `tracked_ids` (queue_order, skipped, overrides, clusters)
  - Tracked in `has_unplanned_objective_blockers` field of `QueueSnapshot`
  - If true, execution items include these unplanned blockers BEFORE postflight items can surface
- **Mid-cycle with objective backlog**: `is_mid_cycle(plan) and has_objective_backlog(state, policy)` prevents deferral and triage injection
- **Stale triage**: New review issues that haven't been triaged yet block certain operations via `require_triage_current_or_exit()`
- **Preflight backlog**: `desloppify review` reruns are blocked until objective backlog is drained (unless `--force-review-rerun` is set)

### 3. QueueContext and Metadata Tracking

**QueueContext** (`context.py`, lines 36-48) is an immutable snapshot containing:
- `plan`: The living plan or None
- `target_strict`: The threshold for subjective visibility
- `plan_load_status`: Degraded flag for load errors
- `snapshot`: A `QueueSnapshot` with all the counts and partitions

**QueueSnapshot** (`snapshot.py`, lines 39-60) tracks:
- `phase`: Current lifecycle phase (string constant like "execute")
- `all_objective_items`, `all_initial_review_items`, `all_postflight_review_items`, `all_scan_items`, `all_postflight_workflow_items`, `all_postflight_triage_items`: Item lists by type
- `execution_items`: Currently active items for the phase
- `backlog_items`: Everything else
- `objective_in_scope_count`: Total objective items in path scope
- `objective_execution_count`: Objective items in current execution set
- `objective_backlog_count`: Objective items NOT in execution
- `subjective_initial_count`, `subjective_postflight_count`: Review item counts
- `workflow_postflight_count`, `triage_pending_count`: Postflight work counts
- `has_unplanned_objective_blockers`: Boolean flag for unplanned issues

### 4. How `desloppify next` Surfaces Items

**Flow** (`queue_flow.py`):

1. `build_and_render_execution_queue()` calls `_build_and_render_queue_view()` with `build_work_queue_fn=build_execution_queue`
2. `build_execution_queue()` calls `_build_work_queue_with_visibility(visibility=QueueVisibility.EXECUTION)`
3. `_build_work_queue_with_visibility()` → `select_queue_items()` with the visibility mode
4. `select_queue_items()` calls `build_queue_snapshot()` then `items_for_visibility(snapshot, "execution")`
5. `items_for_visibility()` returns `snapshot.execution_items` (the current phase's active work)

**Phase-based execution selection** happens in `_execution_items_for_phase()`:
- If phase == "execute": return objective items (work on these)
- If phase == "review_postflight": return postflight review items
- If phase == "workflow_postflight": return workflow items (scores, plan)
- If phase == "triage_postflight": return triage stages

**Unplanned blockers are forced into execution** (line 284-286 of snapshot.py):
```python
has_unplanned_objective_blockers = any(
    item.get("id", "") not in tracked_ids for item in objective_items
)
```
If true, these items remain in the execution set even if they're not explicitly in the plan—forcing them to be addressed before postflight work can proceed.

### 5. What `inputs.py` Does

`inputs.py` has two functions:

**`resolve_queue_inputs()`** (lines 12-35):
- Resolves plan, scan_path, status filter, and subjective threshold from queue build options
- Returns tuple: `(plan, scan_path, status, threshold)`
- Validates status is in `{"open", "all", "chronic"}`
- Constrains threshold to [0.0, 100.0]

**`gather_subjective_items()`** (lines 38-56):
- Builds subjective dimension candidates for "non-open" issue list views
- Only called if `include_subjective=True` and status in `{"open", "all"}` and NOT chronic
- Returns items filtered by scope that are NOT in the execution set

These are utility functions for queue input resolution and subjective item synthesis (when not using the snapshot-based phase logic).

---

### Summary: The Blocking Mechanism

1. **Backlog** = all items not currently executing per the phase
2. **Objective backlog** = open, non-subjective issues not suppressed or skipped
3. **Postflight is blocked by**:
   - **Unplanned objective blockers**: Issues found by scanner not in plan → forced into execution
   - **Stale triage**: New review issues without cluster/skip disposition
   - **Preflight rerun guard**: Can't re-review subjective dimensions while backlog exists (unless forced)
4. **`desloppify next`** shows only execution items for the current phase; unplanned blockers get surfaced here
5. **`desloppify backlog`** shows items outside execution via a separate visibility filter

The key insight: the queue is **phase-driven**—the system won't surface postflight work until the current phase's execution items are handled, and unplanned objectives block that transition.

Design an implementation plan for changes to the desloppify queue system. Here's the context and requirements:

## Current System
- Phase waterfall in `snapshot.py`: PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL → PHASE_EXECUTE → PHASE_SCAN → PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT → PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT → PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
- `_phase_for_snapshot()` determines current phase. It checks `if objective_items:` to stay in PHASE_EXECUTE. `objective_items` includes ALL open non-skipped objective items, including unplanned ones.
- Temporary skips (kind="temporary") serve as "deferred" items
- `build_deferred_disposition_item()` in `synthetic_workflow.py` already surfaces a checkpoint in PHASE_SCAN with reactivate/wontfix options
- `_tracked_plan_ids()` returns IDs in queue_order, skipped, overrides, and cluster members
- `has_unplanned_objective_blockers` flag already exists in QueueSnapshot

## Required Changes

### Change 1: Unplanned objective items should NOT block postflight
Currently in `snapshot.py:238-241`:
```python
objective_items = [
    item for item in all_issue_items
    if _is_objective_item(item, skipped_ids=skipped_ids)
]
```
This includes unplanned items. Then `_phase_for_snapshot()` checks `if objective_items:` — if ANY objective item exists (planned or not), execution phase continues.

**Fix**: Split objective items into planned and unplanned. Only planned items should determine the EXECUTE phase. Unplanned items should still be visible but not block phase transitions.

Key question: How to split? A planned objective item is one whose ID is in `tracked_ids` (specifically in `queue_order` — not in `skipped`). Items in `skipped` are already filtered out by `_is_objective_item`. Items in overrides/clusters should count as planned.

### Change 2: Add "postpone" option to deferred disposition
The existing `build_deferred_disposition_item()` offers reactivate and wontfix. Add a third: "postpone to backlog" which means removing the item from `plan.skipped` entirely (the issue stays open in state but is no longer tracked by the plan).

Need a new plan operation: `postpone_items()` — removes from `plan.skipped` without adding to `queue_order`. Currently `unskip_items()` moves from skipped → queue_order. We need a variant that just removes.

### Change 3: Terminology cleanup
The deferred disposition item's summary says "Deferred backlog decision required." This is fine — it's describing the checkpoint for deferred/temporary-skipped items. But the three options should be clearly labeled: reactivate, postpone (to backlog), wontfix.

```
    18→    build_triage_stage_items,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic_workflow import (
    21→    build_communicate_score_item,
    22→    build_create_plan_item,
    23→    build_deferred_disposition_item,
    24→    build_import_scores_item,
    25→    build_run_scan_item,
    26→    build_score_checkpoint_item,
    27→)
    28→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    29→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    30→
    31→PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL = "review_initial"
    32→PHASE_EXECUTE = "execute"
    33→PHASE_SCAN = "scan"
    34→PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT = "review_postflight"
    35→PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT = "workflow_postflight"
    36→PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT = "triage_postflight"
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    40→class QueueSnapshot:
    41→    """Canonical queue facts and partitions for one invocation."""
    42→
    43→    phase: str
    44→    all_objective_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    45→    all_initial_review_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    46→    all_postflight_review_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    47→    all_scan_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    48→    all_postflight_workflow_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    49→    all_postflight_triage_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    50→    execution_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    51→    backlog_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    52→    objective_in_scope_count: int
    53→    objective_execution_count: int
    54→    objective_backlog_count: int
    55→    subjective_initial_count: int
    56→    subjective_postflight_count: int
    57→    workflow_postflight_count: int
    58→    triage_pending_count: int
    59→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers: bool
    60→
    61→
    62→def _option_value(options: object | None, name: str, default: Any) -> Any:
    63→    if options is None:
    64→        return default
    65→    return getattr(options, name, default)
    66→
    67→
    68→def _resolved_scan_path(options: object | None, state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    69→    scan_path = _option_value(options, "scan_path", state.get("scan_path"))
    70→    if hasattr(scan_path, "__class__") and scan_path.__class__.__name__ == "_ScanPathFromState":
    71→        return state.get("scan_path")
    72→    return scan_path
    73→
    74→
    75→def _tracked_plan_ids(plan: dict | None) -> set[str]:
    76→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
    77→        return set()
    78→    tracked: set[str] = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    79→    tracked.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    80→    tracked.update(plan.get("overrides", {}).keys())
    81→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    82→        tracked.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    83→        for step in cluster.get("action_steps", []):
    84→            if isinstance(step, dict):
    85→                tracked.update(step.get("issue_refs", []))
    86→    return tracked
    87→
    88→
    89→def _is_fresh_boundary(plan: dict | None) -> bool:
    90→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
    91→        return True
    92→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    93→    if not scores:
    94→        return True
    95→    return isinstance(scores, dict) and bool(scores.get("reset"))
    96→
    97→
    98→def _is_objective_item(item: WorkQueueItem, *, skipped_ids: set[str]) -> bool:
    99→    return (
   100→        item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
   101→        and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   102→        and item.get("id", "") not in skipped_ids
   103→    )
   104→
   105→
   106→def _review_issue_items(items: Iterable[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   107→    return [
   108→        item for item in items
   109→        if item.get("detector", "") in {"review", "concerns", "subjective_review"}
   110→    ]
   111→
   112→
   113→def _subjective_partitions(
   114→    state: StateModel,
   115→    *,
   116→    scoped_issues: dict[str, dict],
   117→    threshold: float,
   118→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   119→    candidates = build_subjective_items(state, scoped_issues, threshold=threshold)
   120→    initial = [item for item in candidates if item.get("initial_review")]
   121→    postflight = [item for item in candidates if not item.get("initial_review")]
   122→    return initial, postflight
   123→
   124→
   125→def _workflow_partitions(
   126→    plan: dict | None,
   127→    state: StateModel,
   128→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   129→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   130→        return [], [], []
   131→    scan_items = [
   132→        item
   133→        for item in (
   134→            build_deferred_disposition_item(plan),
   135→            build_run_scan_item(plan),
   136→        )
   137→        if item is not None
   138→    ]
   139→    postflight_workflow = [
   140→        item
   141→        for item in (
   142→            build_score_checkpoint_item(plan, state),
   143→            build_import_scores_item(plan, state),
   144→            build_communicate_score_item(plan, state),
   145→            build_create_plan_item(plan),
   146→        )
   147→        if item is not None
   148→    ]
   149→    triage_items = build_triage_stage_items(plan, state)
   150→    return scan_items, postflight_workflow, triage_items
   151→
   152→
   153→def _phase_for_snapshot(
   154→    *,
   155→    fresh_boundary: bool,
   156→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   157→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   158→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   159→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   160→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   161→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   162→) -> str:
   163→    if fresh_boundary and initial_review_items:
   164→        return PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL
   165→    if objective_items:
   166→        return PHASE_EXECUTE
   167→    if scan_items:
   168→        return PHASE_SCAN
   169→    if postflight_review_items:
   170→        return PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT
   171→    if postflight_workflow_items:
   172→        return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   173→    if triage_items:
   174→        return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   175→    return PHASE_SCAN
   176→
   177→
   178→def _execution_items_for_phase(
   179→    phase: str,
   180→    *,
   181→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   182→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   183→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   184→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   185→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   186→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   187→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   188→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL:
   189→        return initial_review_items
   190→    if phase == PHASE_EXECUTE:
   191→        return objective_items
   192→    if phase == PHASE_SCAN:
   193→        deferred_items = [
   194→            item for item in scan_items
   195→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID
   196→        ]
   197→        if deferred_items:
   198→            return deferred_items
   199→        return [
   200→            item for item in scan_items
   201→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID
   202→        ]
   203→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   204→        return postflight_review_items
   205→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   206→        return postflight_workflow_items
   207→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   208→        return triage_items
   209→    return []
   210→
   211→
   212→def build_queue_snapshot(
   213→    state: StateModel,
   214→    *,
   215→    options: object | None = None,
   216→    plan: dict | None = None,
   217→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   218→) -> QueueSnapshot:
   219→    """Build the canonical queue snapshot for the current state."""
   220→    context = _option_value(options, "context", None)
   221→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else (
   222→        plan if plan is not None else _option_value(options, "plan", None)
   223→    )
   224→    scan_path = _resolved_scan_path(options, state)
   225→    skipped_ids = set((effective_plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   226→    tracked_ids = _tracked_plan_ids(effective_plan)
   227→    scoped_issues = path_scoped_issues(state.get("issues", {}), scan_path)
   228→    scope = _option_value(options, "scope", None)
   229→    chronic = bool(_option_value(options, "chronic", False))
   230→
   231→    all_issue_items = build_issue_items(
   232→        state,
   233→        scan_path=scan_path,
   234→        status_filter="open",
   235→        scope=scope,
   236→        chronic=chronic,
   237→    )
   238→    objective_items = [
   239→        item for item in all_issue_items
   240→        if _is_objective_item(item, skipped_ids=skipped_ids)
   241→    ]
   242→    review_issue_items = _review_issue_items(all_issue_items)
   243→    initial_review_items, subjective_postflight_items = _subjective_partitions(
   244→        state,
   245→        scoped_issues=scoped_issues,
   246→        threshold=target_strict,
   247→    )
   248→    postflight_review_items = [*subjective_postflight_items, *review_issue_items]
   249→    scan_items, postflight_workflow_items, triage_items = _workflow_partitions(
   250→        effective_plan,
   251→        state,
   252→    )
   253→
   254→    fresh_boundary = _is_fresh_boundary(effective_plan)
   255→    phase = _phase_for_snapshot(
   256→        fresh_boundary=fresh_boundary,
   257→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   258→        objective_items=objective_items,
   259→        scan_items=scan_items,
   260→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   261→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   262→        triage_items=triage_items,
   263→    )
   264→    execution_items = _execution_items_for_phase(
   265→        phase,
   266→        objective_items=objective_items,
   267→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   268→        scan_items=scan_items,
   269→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   270→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   271→        triage_items=triage_items,
   272→    )
   273→
   274→    execution_ids = {item.get("id", "") for item in execution_items}
   275→    backlog_items = [
   276→        item for item in (
   277→            [*objective_items, *initial_review_items, *postflight_review_items, *scan_items, *postflight_workflow_items, *triage_items]
   278→        )
   279→        if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   280→    ]
   281→    objective_backlog_count = sum(
   282→        1 for item in objective_items if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   283→    )
   284→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers = any(
   285→        item.get("id", "") not in tracked_ids for item in objective_items
   286→    )
   287→
   288→    return QueueSnapshot(
   289→        phase=phase,
   290→        all_objective_items=tuple(objective_items),
   291→        all_initial_review_items=tuple(initial_review_items),
   292→        all_postflight_review_items=tuple(postflight_review_items),
   293→        all_scan_items=tuple(scan_items),
   294→        all_postflight_workflow_items=tuple(postflight_workflow_items),
   295→        all_postflight_triage_items=tuple(triage_items),
   296→        execution_items=tuple(execution_items),
   297→        backlog_items=tuple(backlog_items),
   298→        objective_in_scope_count=len(objective_items),
   299→        objective_execution_count=sum(
   300→            1
   301→            for item in execution_items
   302→            if item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
   303→            and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   304→        ),
   305→        objective_backlog_count=objective_backlog_count,
   306→        subjective_initial_count=len(initial_review_items),
   307→        subjective_postflight_count=len(postflight_review_items),
   308→        workflow_postflight_count=len(postflight_workflow_items),
   309→        triage_pending_count=len(triage_items),
   310→        has_unplanned_objective_blockers=has_unplanned_objective_blockers,
   311→    )
   312→
   313→
   314→def coarse_phase_name(phase: str) -> str:
   315→    """Map internal queue phases to the persisted coarse lifecycle value."""
   316→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL or phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   317→        return "review"
   318→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   319→        return "workflow"
   320→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   321→        return "triage"
   322→    return phase
   323→
   324→
   325→__all__ = [
   326→    "PHASE_EXECUTE",
   327→    "PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL",
   328→    "PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT",
   329→    "PHASE_SCAN",
   330→    "PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT",
   331→    "PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT",
   332→    "QueueSnapshot",
   333→    "build_queue_snapshot",
   334→    "coarse_phase_name",
   335→]
   336→
```

```
1→"""Workflow-specific synthetic work queue item builders."""

    28→
    29→
    30→def _confirm_attestation_hint(stage: str) -> str:
    31→    if stage == "observe":
    32→        return "I have thoroughly reviewed..."
    33→    if stage == "reflect":
    34→        return "My strategy accounts for..."
    35→    if stage == "organize":
    36→        return "This plan is correct..."
    37→    if stage == "enrich":
    38→        return "Steps are executor-ready..."
    39→    if stage == "sense-check":
    40→        return "Content and structure verified..."
    41→    return "..."
    42→
    43→
    44→def _runner_planning_tools(
    45→    *,
    46→    only_stages: str | None = None,
    47→    manual_fallback: str | None = None,
    48→) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    49→    tools = [
    50→        {"label": label, "command": command}
    51→        for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages)
    52→    ]
    53→    if manual_fallback:
    54→        tools.append({"label": "Manual fallback", "command": manual_fallback})
    55→    tools.append({"label": "Dashboard", "command": "desloppify plan triage"})
    56→    return tools
    57→
    58→
    59→def _triage_progression_target(plan: dict) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:
    60→    """Return current triage stage target and whether it requires confirmation."""
    61→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    62→    triage_stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {}) or {}
    63→    for stage, _sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
    64→        stage_payload = triage_stages.get(stage)
    65→        if isinstance(stage_payload, dict) and stage_payload and not stage_payload.get("confirmed_at"):
    66→            return stage, True
    67→
    68→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    69→    for stage, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
    70→        if sid not in order:
    71→            continue
    72→        return stage, False
    73→
    74→    if "triage::commit" in order:
    75→        return "commit", False
    76→
    77→    return None, False
    78→
    79→
    80→def _create_plan_primary_command(plan: dict) -> str:
    81→    stage, needs_confirm = _triage_progression_target(plan)
    82→    if stage is None:
    83→        return triage_run_stages_command()
    84→    if needs_confirm:
    85→        attestation = _confirm_attestation_hint(stage)
    86→        return (
    87→            f'desloppify plan triage --confirm {stage} '
    88→            f'--attestation "{attestation}"'
    89→        )
    90→    if stage == "commit":
    91→        return triage_run_stages_command()
    92→    return triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=stage)
    93→
    94→
    95→def _create_plan_planning_tools(plan: dict) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    96→    stage, needs_confirm = _triage_progression_target(plan)
    97→    if needs_confirm:
    98→        return []
    99→    if stage == "commit":
   100→        return _runner_planning_tools(
   101→            manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("commit"),
   102→        )
   103→    if stage:
   104→        return _runner_planning_tools(
   105→            only_stages=stage,
   106→            manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command(stage),
   107→        )
   108→    return _runner_planning_tools(
   109→        only_stages="observe",
   110→        manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("observe"),
   111→    )
   112→
   113→
   114→def build_score_checkpoint_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:
   115→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` if queued."""
   116→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   117→        return None
   118→
   119→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   120→
   121→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   122→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
   123→    plan_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   124→    delta = round(strict - plan_start, 1) if plan_start is not None else None
   125→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
   126→
   127→    return {
   128→        "id": WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
   129→        "tier": 1,
   130→        "confidence": "high",
   131→        "detector": "workflow",
   132→        "file": ".",
   133→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   134→        "summary": f"Score checkpoint: strict {strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}",
   135→        "detail": {
   136→            "strict": strict,
   137→            "plan_start_strict": plan_start,
   138→            "delta": delta,
   139→            "planning_tools": _runner_planning_tools(
   140→                only_stages="observe",
   141→                manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("observe"),
   142→            ),
   143→        },
   144→        "primary_command": triage_run_stages_command(only_stages="observe"),
   145→        "blocked_by": [],
   146→        "is_blocked": False,
   147→    }

   172→def build_import_scores_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:

   183→    )

   186→    if packet_sha:
   187→        explanation += f" Expected packet sha256: `{packet_sha}`."
   188→
   189→    return {
   190→        "id": WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
   191→        "tier": 1,
   192→        "confidence": "high",
   193→        "detector": "workflow",

   201→        },
   202→        "primary_command": (
   203→            f"desloppify review --import {quoted_import_file} --attested-external "
   204→            '--attest "I validated this review was completed without awareness '
   205→            'of overall score and is unbiased."'
   206→        ),
```
