   212→def build_communicate_score_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:
   213→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::communicate-score`` if queued."""
   214→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   215→        return None
   216→
   217→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   218→
   219→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   220→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
   221→
   222→    # Use previous_plan_start_scores (stashed at rebaseline) for the delta
   223→    # so the user sees old-baseline → current, not current → current.
   224→    prev_start = (plan.get("previous_plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   225→    if prev_start is None:
   226→        prev_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   227→    delta = round(strict - prev_start, 1) if prev_start is not None else None
   228→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
   229→
   230→    return {
   231→        "id": WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
   232→        "tier": 1,
   233→        "confidence": "high",
   234→        "detector": "workflow",
   235→        "file": ".",
   236→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   237→        "summary": f"Communicate updated score to user: strict {strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}",
   238→        "detail": {
   239→            "strict": strict,
   240→            "previous_plan_start_strict": prev_start,
   241→            "delta": delta,
   242→        },
   243→        "primary_command": (
   244→            f'desloppify plan resolve "{WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID}" '
   245→            '--note "Score communicated" --confirm'
   246→        ),
   247→        "blocked_by": [],
   248→        "is_blocked": False,
   249→    }
   250→
   251→
   252→def build_run_scan_item(plan: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:
   253→    """Build a synthetic item for the first post-flight scan step."""
   254→    if not postflight_scan_pending(plan):
   255→        return None
   256→
   257→    return {
   258→        "id": WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID,
   259→        "tier": 1,
   260→        "confidence": "high",
   261→        "detector": "workflow",
   262→        "file": ".",
   263→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   264→        "summary": "Run post-flight scan to refresh queue and surface follow-up review work.",
   265→        "detail": {
   266→            "phase": "postflight_scan",
   267→        },
   268→        "execution_visibility": "always",
   269→        "primary_command": "desloppify scan",
   270→        "blocked_by": [],
   271→        "is_blocked": False,
   272→    }
   273→
   274→
   275→def _temporary_skipped_ids(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   276→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   277→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
   278→        return []
   279→    deferred: list[str] = []
   280→    for issue_id, entry in skipped.items():
   281→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   282→            continue
   283→        if str(entry.get("kind", "temporary")) == "temporary":
   284→            deferred.append(str(issue_id))
   285→    deferred.sort()
   286→    return deferred
   287→
   288→
   289→def _deferred_cluster_breakdown(
   290→    plan: dict,
   291→    deferred_ids: list[str],
   292→) -> tuple[int, int]:
   293→    deferred_set = set(deferred_ids)
   294→    if not deferred_set:
   295→        return 0, 0
   296→
   297→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   298→    if not isinstance(clusters, dict):
   299→        return 0, len(deferred_ids)
   300→
   301→    covered_by_cluster: set[str] = set()
   302→    cluster_count = 0
   303→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   304→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
   305→            continue
   306→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   307→        if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
   308→            continue
   309→        matched = {str(issue_id) for issue_id in issue_ids if str(issue_id) in deferred_set}
   310→        if not matched:
   311→            continue
   312→        cluster_count += 1
   313→        covered_by_cluster.update(matched)
   314→
   315→    individual_count = len(deferred_set - covered_by_cluster)
   316→    return cluster_count, individual_count
   317→
   318→
   319→def build_deferred_disposition_item(plan: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:
   320→    """Build a synthetic item prompting deferred backlog disposition."""
   321→    deferred_ids = _temporary_skipped_ids(plan)
   322→    if not deferred_ids:
   323→        return None
   324→
   325→    count = len(deferred_ids)
   326→    cluster_count, individual_count = _deferred_cluster_breakdown(plan, deferred_ids)
   327→    cluster_label = "cluster" if cluster_count == 1 else "clusters"
   328→    individual_label = "item" if individual_count == 1 else "items"
   329→    reactivate_cmd = 'desloppify plan unskip "*"'
   330→    subset_reactivate_cmd = "desloppify plan unskip <cluster-or-id>"
   331→    inspect_cmd = "desloppify plan queue --include-skipped"
   332→    inspect_item_cmd = "desloppify show <cluster-or-id>"
   333→    wontfix_cmd = (
   334→        'desloppify plan skip --permanent "*" '
   335→        '--note "<why this deferred work should stay wontfix>" '
   336→        '--attest "I have actually reviewed these deferred items and I am not gaming the score by skipping them permanently." '
   337→        "--confirm"
   338→    )
   339→    subset_wontfix_cmd = (
   340→        "desloppify plan skip --permanent <cluster-or-id> "
   341→        '--note "<why this deferred work should stay wontfix>" '
   342→        '--attest "I have actually reviewed these deferred items and I am not gaming the score by skipping them permanently."'
   343→    )
   344→
   345→    return {
   346→        "id": WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
   347→        "tier": 1,
   348→        "confidence": "high",
   349→        "detector": "workflow",
   350→        "file": ".",
   351→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   352→        "execution_visibility": "always",
   353→        "summary": (
   354→            "Deferred backlog decision required: "
   355→            f"{cluster_count} {cluster_label} + {individual_count} individual {individual_label} "
   356→            "must be reactivated or marked wontfix."
   357→        ),
   358→        "detail": {
   359→            "temporary_skipped_count": count,
   360→            "deferred_cluster_count": cluster_count,
   361→            "deferred_individual_count": individual_count,
   362→            "reactivate_command": reactivate_cmd,
   363→            "wontfix_command": wontfix_cmd,
   364→            "planning_tools": [
   365→                {
   366→                    "label": "Review deferred backlog",
   367→                    "command": inspect_cmd,
   368→                },
   369→                {
   370→                    "label": "Inspect a specific cluster or item",
   371→                    "command": inspect_item_cmd,
   372→                },
   373→                {
   374→                    "label": "Reactivate a subset",
   375→                    "command": subset_reactivate_cmd,
   376→                },
   377→                {
   378→                    "label": "Mark a subset as permanent wontfix",
   379→                    "command": subset_wontfix_cmd,
   380→                },
   381→            ],
   382→            "decision_options": [
   383→                {
   384→                    "label": "Reactivate deferred work",
   385→                    "command": reactivate_cmd,
   386→                },
   387→                {
   388→                    "label": "Convert deferred work to wontfix",
   389→                    "command": wontfix_cmd,
   390→                },
   391→            ],
   392→        },
   393→        "primary_command": reactivate_cmd,
   394→        "blocked_by": [],
   395→        "is_blocked": False,
   396→    }
   397→
   398→
   399→__all__ = [
   400→    "build_communicate_score_item",
   401→    "build_create_plan_item",
   402→    "build_deferred_disposition_item",
   403→    "build_import_scores_item",
   404→    "build_run_scan_item",
   405→    "build_score_checkpoint_item",
   406→]
   407→

1→"""Unified queue-resolution context.
     2→
     3→A frozen ``QueueContext`` computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``plan`` / ``target_strict`` / ``policy`` threading through function chains.
     5→Callers build one context and pass it everywhere — makes the wrong thing
     6→impossible.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from dataclasses import dataclass
    12→
    13→from desloppify.base.config import (
    14→    DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    15→    target_strict_score_from_config,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.persistence import (
    18→    has_living_plan,
    19→    load_plan,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import PlanLoadStatus
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.snapshot import QueueSnapshot, build_queue_snapshot
    24→
    25→
    26→class _PlanAutoLoad:
    27→    """Sentinel type: auto-load plan from disk."""
    28→
    29→
    30→# Sentinel: "auto-load plan from disk" (the default).
    31→_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD = _PlanAutoLoad()
    32→PlanOption = dict | None | _PlanAutoLoad
    33→
    34→
    35→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    36→class QueueContext:
    37→    """Immutable snapshot of resolved queue parameters.
    38→
    39→    Built once via :func:`queue_context`, then threaded through queue
    40→    builders and command helpers so every call site agrees on plan,
    41→    target, and canonical queue facts.
    42→    """
    43→
    44→    plan: dict | None
    45→    target_strict: float
    46→    plan_load_status: PlanLoadStatus
    47→    snapshot: QueueSnapshot
    48→
    49→
    50→def resolve_plan_load_status(
    51→    *,
    52→    plan: PlanOption = _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD,
    53→) -> PlanLoadStatus:
    54→    """Resolve plan loading and explicitly report degraded mode."""
    55→    if not isinstance(plan, _PlanAutoLoad):
    56→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=plan, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    57→
    58→    if not has_living_plan():
    59→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=None, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    60→
    61→    try:
    62→        resolved_plan = load_plan()
    63→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=resolved_plan, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    64→    except OSError as exc:
    65→        return PlanLoadStatus(
    66→            plan=None,
    67→            degraded=True,
    68→            error_kind=exc.__class__.__name__,
    69→        )
    70→    except (
    71→        ValueError,
    72→        UnicodeDecodeError,
    73→    ) as exc:
    74→        return PlanLoadStatus(
    75→            plan=None,
    76→            degraded=True,
    77→            error_kind=exc.__class__.__name__,
    78→        )
    79→
    80→
    81→def queue_context(
    82→    state: StateModel,
    83→    *,
    84→    config: dict | None = None,
    85→    plan: PlanOption = _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD,
    86→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    87→) -> QueueContext:
    88→    """Build a :class:`QueueContext` with all parameters resolved.
    89→
    90→    Resolution order:
    91→
    92→    1. **plan** — explicit value wins; sentinel ``_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD`` triggers
    93→       ``load_plan()`` (guarded by ``PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS``).
    94→    2. **target_strict** — explicit float wins; ``None`` reads from *config*
    95→       via ``target_strict_score_from_config``; final fallback is ``95.0``.
    96→    3. **snapshot** — canonical queue phase and partitions derived from the
    97→       resolved plan and target_strict.
    98→    """
    99→    # --- resolve plan ---
   100→    plan_load_status = resolve_plan_load_status(plan=plan)
   101→    resolved_plan = plan_load_status.plan
   102→
   103→    # --- resolve target_strict ---
   104→    if target_strict is not None:
   105→        resolved_target = target_strict
   106→    elif config is not None:
   107→        resolved_target = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   108→    else:
   109→        resolved_target = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
   110→
   111→    snapshot = build_queue_snapshot(
   112→        state,
   113→        plan=resolved_plan,
   114→        target_strict=resolved_target,
   115→    )
   116→
   117→    return QueueContext(
   118→        plan=resolved_plan,
   119→        target_strict=resolved_target,
   120→        plan_load_status=plan_load_status,
   121→        snapshot=snapshot,
   122→    )
   123→
   124→
   125→__all__ = [
   126→    "PlanLoadStatus",
   127→    "QueueContext",
   128→    "queue_context",
   129→    "resolve_plan_load_status",
   130→]
   131→
```

1→"""Skip and unskip command handlers for plan overrides."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import logging
     7→import sys
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→
    10→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.attestation import (
    12→    show_attestation_requirement,
    13→    validate_attestation,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    16→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_issue_inventory
    17→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.shared.patterns import resolve_ids_from_patterns
    18→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_io import (

    21→)
    22→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    23→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    24→from desloppify.base.output.user_message import print_user_message
    25→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import (

    27→    save_plan,
    28→)
    29→from desloppify.engine.plan_ops import (
    30→    SKIP_KIND_LABELS,
    31→    append_log_entry,
    32→    skip_items,
    33→    skip_kind_from_flags,
    34→    skip_kind_requires_attestation,

    38→)
    39→from desloppify.engine._plan.refresh_lifecycle import clear_postflight_scan_completion
    40→
    41→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    42→
    43→_BULK_SKIP_THRESHOLD = 5
    44→_TRIAGE_SKIP_ATTESTATION_PHRASES = ("reviewed", "not gaming")
    45→_TRIAGE_SKIP_ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    46→    "I have reviewed this triage skip against the code and I am not gaming "
    47→    "the score by suppressing a real defect."
    48→)
    49→
    50→
    51→def _validate_skip_requirements(
    52→    *,
    53→    kind: str,
    54→    attestation: str | None,
    55→    note: str | None,
    56→) -> bool:
    57→    if not skip_kind_requires_attestation(kind):
    58→        return True
    59→    if not validate_attestation(
    60→        attestation,
    61→        required_phrases=_TRIAGE_SKIP_ATTESTATION_PHRASES,
    62→    ):
    63→        show_attestation_requirement(
    64→            "Permanent skip" if kind == "permanent" else "False positive",
    65→            attestation,
    66→            _TRIAGE_SKIP_ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    67→            required_phrases=_TRIAGE_SKIP_ATTESTATION_PHRASES,
    68→        )
    69→        return False
    70→    if skip_kind_requires_note(kind) and not note:
    71→        print(
    72→            colorize("  --permanent requires --note to explain the decision.", "yellow"),
    73→            file=sys.stderr,
    74→        )
    75→        return False
    76→    return True
    77→
    78→
    79→def _apply_state_skip_resolution(
    80→    *,
    81→    kind: str,
    82→    state_file: Path | None,
    83→    issue_ids: list[str],
    84→    note: str | None,
    85→    attestation: str | None,
    86→) -> dict | None:
    87→    status = skip_kind_state_status(kind)
    88→    if status is None:
    89→        return None
    90→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    91→    for fid in issue_ids:
    92→        state_mod.resolve_issues(
    93→            state_data,
    94→            fid,
    95→            status,
    96→            note or "",
    97→            attestation=attestation,
    98→        )
    99→    return state_data
   100→
   101→
   102→def _validate_temporary_skip_confirmation(
   103→    *,
   104→    confirm: bool,
   105→    kind: str,
   106→    reason: str | None,
   107→    attestation: str | None,
   108→) -> bool:
   109→    if not confirm or kind != "temporary":
   110→        return True
   111→    if not reason:
   112→        print(
   113→            colorize(
   114→                "  --confirm requires --reason to describe why this is being deferred.",
   115→                "red",
   116→            )
   117→        )
   118→        return False
   119→    required_phrases = ("i have actually reflected", "not deferring")
   120→    normalized_attest = " ".join((attestation or "").strip().lower().split())
   121→    missing = [phrase for phrase in required_phrases if phrase not in normalized_attest]
   122→    if not missing:
   123→        return True
   124→    print(
   125→        colorize(
   126→            "  Deferring items requires you to confirm you've thought this through.",
   127→            "yellow",
   128→        )
   129→    )
   130→    print(colorize("  Add this to your command:", "dim"))
   131→    print()
   132→    print(
   133→        colorize(
   134→            '    --attest "I have actually reflected and '
   135→            'I am not deferring this for lazy reasons."',
   136→            "dim",
   137→        )
   138→    )
   139→    print()
   140→    return False
   141→
   142→
   143→def _warn_or_block_bulk_skip(issue_ids: list[str], *, confirm: bool) -> None:
   144→    if len(issue_ids) <= _BULK_SKIP_THRESHOLD:
   145→        return
   146→    print(
   147→        colorize(
   148→            f"  Bulk skip: {len(issue_ids)} items will be removed from the active queue.",
   149→            "yellow",
   150→        ),
   151→        file=sys.stderr,
   152→    )
   153→    if confirm:
   154→        return
   155→    raise CommandError(
   156→        f"Skipping {len(issue_ids)} items requires --confirm. "
   157→        "Review the items first, or skip individually."
   158→    )
   159→
   160→
   161→def _save_skip_plan_state(
   162→    *,
   163→    plan: dict,
   164→    plan_file: Path | None,
   165→    state_data: dict | None,
   166→    state_file: Path | None,
   167→) -> None:
   168→    if state_data is None:
   169→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   170→        return
   171→    save_plan_state_transactional(
   172→        plan=plan,
   173→        plan_path=plan_file,
   174→        state_data=state_data,
   175→        state_path_value=state_file,
   176→    )
   177→
   178→
   179→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   180→    """Skip issues — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
   181→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   182→    state = runtime.state
   183→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
   184→        return
   185→
   186→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   187→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
   188→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
   189→    permanent: bool = getattr(args, "permanent", False)
   190→    false_positive: bool = getattr(args, "false_positive", False)
   191→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   192→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   193→
   194→    kind = skip_kind_from_flags(permanent=permanent, false_positive=false_positive)
   195→
   196→    if not _validate_temporary_skip_confirmation(
   197→        confirm=bool(getattr(args, "confirm", False)),
   198→        kind=kind,
   199→        reason=reason,
   200→        attestation=attestation,
   201→    ):
   202→        return
   203→    if not _validate_skip_requirements(kind=kind, attestation=attestation, note=note):
   204→        raise CommandError(
   205→            "Invalid plan skip attestation or note.",
   206→            exit_code=2,
   207→        )
   208→
   209→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   210→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   211→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   212→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   213→    if not issue_ids:
   214→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   215→        return
   216→
   217→    _warn_or_block_bulk_skip(issue_ids, confirm=bool(getattr(args, "confirm", False)))
   218→
   219→    state_data = _apply_state_skip_resolution(
   220→        kind=kind,
   221→        state_file=state_file,
   222→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   223→        note=note,
   224→        attestation=attestation,
   225→    )
   226→
   227→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   228→    count = skip_items(
   229→        plan,
   230→        issue_ids,
   231→        kind=kind,
   232→        reason=reason,
   233→        note=note,
   234→        attestation=attestation,
   235→        review_after=review_after,
   236→        scan_count=scan_count,
   237→    )
   238→
   239→    append_log_entry(
   240→        plan,
   241→        "skip",
   242→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   243→        actor="user",
   244→        note=note,
   245→        detail={"kind": kind, "reason": reason},
   246→    )
   247→    clear_postflight_scan_completion(plan, issue_ids=issue_ids)
   248→    _save_skip_plan_state(
   249→        plan=plan,
   250→        plan_file=plan_file,
   251→        state_data=state_data,
   252→        state_file=state_file,
   253→    )
   254→
   255→    print(colorize(f"  {SKIP_KIND_LABELS[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   256→    if review_after:
   257→        print(colorize(f"  Will re-surface after {review_after} scan(s).", "dim"))
   258→    if kind == "temporary":
   259→        print(
   260→            colorize(
   261→                "  If you're actually not going to fix these, use "
   262→                "`desloppify plan skip <patterns> --permanent` instead to wontfix them.",
   263→                "dim",
   264→            )
   265→        )
   266→    print_user_message(
   267→        "Hey — if skipping was the right call, just continue with"
   268→        " what you were doing. If you think a broader re-triage is"
   269→        " needed, use `desloppify plan triage`. Run `desloppify"
   270→        " plan --help` to see all available plan tools. Otherwise"
   271→        " no need to reply, just keep going."
   272→    )
   273→
   274→
   275→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   276→    """Unskip issues — bring back to queue."""
   277→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   278→    state = runtime.state
   279→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
   280→        return
   281→
   282→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   283→
   284→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   285→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   286→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   287→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   288→    if not issue_ids:
   289→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   290→        return
   291→
   292→    include_protected = bool(getattr(args, "force", False))
   293→    count, need_reopen, protected_kept = unskip_items(
   294→        plan,
   295→        issue_ids,
   296→        include_protected=include_protected,
   297→    )
   298→    unskipped_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if fid not in protected_kept]
   299→    append_log_entry(
   300→        plan,
   301→        "unskip",
   302→        issue_ids=unskipped_ids,
   303→        actor="user",
   304→        detail={"need_reopen": need_reopen},
   305→    )
   306→    clear_postflight_scan_completion(plan, issue_ids=unskipped_ids)
   307→
   308→    reopened: list[str] = []
   309→    if need_reopen:
   310→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   311→        for fid in need_reopen:
   312→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, fid, "open"))
   313→        save_plan_state_transactional(
   314→            plan=plan,
   315→            plan_path=plan_file,
   316→            state_data=state_data,
   317→            state_path_value=state_file,
   318→        )
   319→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s) in state.", "dim"))
   320→    else:
   321→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   322→
   323→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))
   324→    if protected_kept:
   325→        print(
   326→            colorize(
   327→                f"  Kept {len(protected_kept)} protected skip(s) "
   328→                f"(permanent/false_positive with notes). Use --force to override.",
   329→                "yellow",
   330→            )
   331→        )
   332→
   333→

   336→    "_validate_skip_requirements",
   337→    "cmd_plan_skip",
   338→    "cmd_plan_unskip",

   208→
   209→
   210→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   211→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   212→    now = utc_now()
   213→    return {
   214→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   215→        "created": now,
   216→        "updated": now,
   217→        "queue_order": [],
   218→        "deferred": [],
   219→        "skipped": {},
   220→        "active_cluster": None,
   221→        "overrides": {},
   222→        "clusters": {},
   223→        "superseded": {},
   224→        "promoted_ids": [],
   225→        "plan_start_scores": {},
   226→        "refresh_state": {},
   227→        "execution_log": [],
   228→        "epic_triage_meta": {},
   229→        "commit_log": [],
   230→        "uncommitted_issues": [],
   231→        "commit_tracking_branch": None,
   232→    }
   233→
   234→
   235→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   236→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   237→
   238→    Runtime contract is v8-only. Legacy payloads are upgraded in-place once.
   239→    """
   240→    defaults = empty_plan()
   241→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   242→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   243→    _upgrade_plan_to_v8(plan)
   244→    subjective_defer_meta = plan.get("subjective_defer_meta")
   245→    if isinstance(subjective_defer_meta, dict):
   246→        subjective_defer_meta.pop("force_visible_ids", None)
   247→    epic_triage_meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta")
   248→    if isinstance(epic_triage_meta, dict):
   249→        epic_triage_meta.pop("triage_force_visible", None)
   250→
   251→
   252→def triage_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   253→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   254→    return {
   255→        name: cluster
   256→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   257→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   258→    }
   259→
   260→
   261→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   262→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   263→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   264→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   265→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   266→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   267→
   268→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   269→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   270→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   271→    if overlap:
   272→        raise ValueError(
   273→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   274→        )
   275→
   276→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   277→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   278→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   279→            raise ValueError(f"plan.skipped[{fid!r}] must be an object")
   280→        if "kind" not in entry:
   281→            raise ValueError(f"plan.skipped[{fid!r}] missing required key 'kind'")
   282→        kind = entry["kind"]
   283→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   284→            raise ValueError(
   285→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   286→            )
   287→
   288→

1→"""Preflight guards for subjective review reruns.
     2→
     3→Blocks reruns while backlog remains (objective issues and/or subjective queue
     4→work), unless ``--force-review-rerun`` is set. Also clears stale subjective
     5→markers before a new cycle.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import re
    11→import sys
    12→
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import load_query_result
    14→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    15→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    16→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import queue_context
    17→from desloppify.state_io import StateModel, save_state
    18→
    19→from .helpers import parse_dimensions
    20→
    21→
    22→def clear_stale_subjective_entries(
    23→    state: StateModel,
    24→    *,
    25→    dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    26→) -> list[str]:
    27→    """Clear ``needs_review_refresh`` markers from subjective assessments.
    28→
    29→    When *dimensions* is provided, only those dimensions are cleared;
    30→    otherwise all stale dimensions are cleared.
    31→
    32→    Returns the list of dimension keys that were cleared.
    33→    """
    34→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    35→    cleared: list[str] = []
    36→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    37→        if not isinstance(assessment, dict):
    38→            continue
    39→        if dimensions is not None and dim_key not in dimensions:
    40→            continue
    41→        if assessment.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    42→            assessment.pop("needs_review_refresh", None)
    43→            assessment.pop("stale_since", None)
    44→            assessment.pop("refresh_reason", None)
    45→            cleared.append(dim_key)
    46→    return cleared
    47→
    48→
    49→def _scored_dimensions(state: StateModel) -> list[str]:
    50→    """Return dimension keys that already have a nonzero subjective score."""
    51→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    52→    scored: list[str] = []
    53→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    54→        if isinstance(assessment, dict):
    55→            if assessment.get("score", 0):
    56→                scored.append(dim_key)
    57→        elif isinstance(assessment, int | float) and assessment:
    58→            scored.append(dim_key)
    59→    return sorted(scored)
    60→
    61→
    62→def _normalize_dimension_key(raw: object) -> str:
    63→    """Normalize a dimension key/name to canonical snake_case."""
    64→    text = str(raw or "").strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
    65→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text).strip("_")
    66→
    67→
    68→def _prepared_query_dimensions() -> set[str] | None:
    69→    """Return prepared review dimensions from query.json when available."""
    70→    query_result = load_query_result()
    71→    if not query_result.ok:
    72→        if query_result.error_kind in {"query_parse_error", "query_invalid_shape"}:
    73→            detail = query_result.message or query_result.error_kind
    74→            print(
    75→                colorize(
    76→                    f"  Warning: ignoring invalid query artifact ({detail}).",
    77→                    "yellow",
    78→                ),
    79→                file=sys.stderr,
    80→            )
    81→        return None
    82→    query = query_result.payload or {}
    83→    if not isinstance(query, dict):
    84→        return None
    85→    if query.get("command") != "review" or query.get("mode") != "holistic":
    86→        return None
    87→    if not isinstance(query.get("investigation_batches"), list):
    88→        return None
    89→    dims = query.get("dimensions")
    90→    if not isinstance(dims, list):
    91→        return None
    92→    resolved = {dim.strip() for dim in dims if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()}
    93→    return resolved or None
    94→
    95→
    96→def _target_dimensions(args: object) -> set[str] | None:
    97→    """Resolve review target dimensions from CLI args or prepared query state."""
    98→    dimensions = parse_dimensions(args)
    99→    if dimensions is not None:
   100→        return dimensions
   101→    if getattr(args, "run_batches", False) or getattr(args, "external_start", False):
   102→        return _prepared_query_dimensions()
   103→    return None
   104→
   105→
   106→def _blocking_scored_dimensions(
   107→    state: StateModel,
   108→    *,
   109→    dimensions: set[str] | None,
   110→    normalized_dimensions: set[str] | None,
   111→) -> list[str]:
   112→    scored_dims = _scored_dimensions(state)
   113→    if dimensions is None:
   114→        return scored_dims
   115→    normalized = normalized_dimensions or set()
   116→    return sorted(
   117→        dim
   118→        for dim in scored_dims
   119→        if _normalize_dimension_key(dim) in normalized
   120→    )
   121→
   122→
   123→def _objective_and_subjective_backlog(
   124→    state: StateModel,
   125→    *,
   126→    blocking_dims: list[str],
   127→) -> tuple[int, int]:
   128→    ctx = queue_context(state)
   129→    if not blocking_dims:
   130→        return ctx.snapshot.objective_in_scope_count, 0
   131→
   132→    normalized_blocking_dims = {_normalize_dimension_key(dim) for dim in blocking_dims}
   133→
   134→    def _item_dimension_key(item: dict) -> str | None:
   135→        detail = item.get("detail")
   136→        if not isinstance(detail, dict):
   137→            return None
   138→        dimension = detail.get("dimension")
   139→        if not isinstance(dimension, str) or not dimension.strip():
   140→            return None
   141→        return _normalize_dimension_key(dimension)
   142→
   143→    subjective_total = sum(
   144→        1
   145→        for item in ctx.snapshot.all_postflight_review_items
   146→        if item.get("kind") != "subjective_dimension"
   147→        if (dim_key := _item_dimension_key(item)) is not None
   148→        and dim_key in normalized_blocking_dims
   149→    )
   150→    return ctx.snapshot.objective_in_scope_count, subjective_total
   151→
   152→
   153→def _print_backlog_blocked_message(
   154→    *,
   155→    blocking_dims: list[str],
   156→    objective_total: int,
   157→    subjective_total: int,
   158→    dimensions: set[str] | None,
   159→) -> None:
   160→    print(
   161→        colorize(
   162→            "  Blocked: rerun requires drained backlog "
   163→            f"(objective: {objective_total}, subjective: {subjective_total}).",
   164→            "red",
   165→        ),
   166→        file=sys.stderr,
   167→    )
   168→    print(
   169→        colorize(
   170→            f"  Scored dimensions: {', '.join(blocking_dims)}",
   171→            "yellow",
   172→        ),
   173→        file=sys.stderr,
   174→    )
   175→    if objective_total > 0:
   176→        print(
   177→            colorize(
   178→                f"  Open objective issue(s): {objective_total}",
   179→                "yellow",
   180→            ),
   181→            file=sys.stderr,
   182→        )
   183→    if subjective_total > 0:
   184→        print(
   185→            colorize(
   186→                f"  Open subjective queue item(s): {subjective_total}",
   187→                "yellow",
   188→            ),
   189→            file=sys.stderr,
   190→        )
   191→    print("", file=sys.stderr)
   192→    if dimensions is not None:
   193→        unscored = sorted(dimensions - set(blocking_dims))
   194→        if unscored:
   195→            print(
   196→                colorize(
   197→                    f"  Tip: target only unscored dimensions with "
   198→                    f"--dimensions {','.join(unscored)}",
   199→                    "dim",
   200→                ),
   201→                file=sys.stderr,
   202→            )
   203→    print(
   204→        colorize(
   205→            "  Resolve open items first, or override with --force-review-rerun",
   206→            "dim",
   207→        ),
   208→        file=sys.stderr,
   209→    )
   210→
   211→
   212→def review_rerun_preflight(
   213→    state: StateModel,
   214→    args,
   215→    *,
   216→    state_file=None,
   217→    save_fn=save_state,
   218→) -> None:
   219→    """Single entry point: gate check -> clear stale -> save.
   220→
   221→    Exits with code 1 when open backlog exists and ``--force-review-rerun`` is
   222→    not set. On success, clears stale subjective markers for targeted
   223→    dimensions and persists state.
   224→    """
   225→    dimensions = _target_dimensions(args)
   226→    normalized_dimensions = (
   227→        {_normalize_dimension_key(dim) for dim in dimensions}
   228→        if dimensions is not None
   229→        else None
   230→    )
   231→
   232→    # --force-review-rerun bypasses the gate
   233→    if getattr(args, "force_review_rerun", False):
   234→        print(
   235→            colorize(
   236→                "  --force-review-rerun: bypassing rerun backlog checks.",
   237→                "yellow",
   238→            )
   239→        )
   240→    else:
   241→        # Only gate dimensions that are actually targeted by this review run.
   242→        blocking_dims = _blocking_scored_dimensions(
   243→            state,
   244→            dimensions=dimensions,
   245→            normalized_dimensions=normalized_dimensions,
   246→        )
   247→
   248→        # No gate when none of the targeted dimensions have prior scores —
   249→        # this is a first run for these dimensions, not a rerun.
   250→        if blocking_dims:
   251→            objective_total, subjective_total = _objective_and_subjective_backlog(
   252→                state,
   253→                blocking_dims=blocking_dims,
   254→            )
   255→            if objective_total > 0 or subjective_total > 0:
   256→                _print_backlog_blocked_message(
   257→                    blocking_dims=blocking_dims,
   258→                    objective_total=objective_total,
   259→                    subjective_total=subjective_total,
   260→                    dimensions=dimensions,
   261→                )
   262→                raise CommandError(
   263→                    f"rerun blocked: open backlog (objective: {objective_total}, "
   264→                    f"subjective: {subjective_total})",
   265→                    exit_code=1,
   266→                )
   267→
   268→    # Gate passed — clear stale for targeted dims
   269→    cleared = clear_stale_subjective_entries(state, dimensions=dimensions)
   270→    if cleared and state_file:
   271→        save_fn(state, state_file)
   272→        print(
   273→            colorize(
   274→                f"  Cleared stale review markers: {', '.join(sorted(cleared))}",
   275→                "cyan",
   276→            )
   277→        )
   278→

1→"""Helpers for the persisted queue lifecycle phase.
     2→
     3→The persisted phase is the normal source of truth for CLI/debugging, but it is
     4→not trusted blindly. Queue assembly still re-resolves the active phase from the
     5→currently visible items as a safety net so stale saved state cannot strand the
     6→user in the wrong phase after out-of-band changes.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from typing import Iterable
    12→
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    15→
    16→[REDACTED]
    17→_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY = "lifecycle_phase"
    18→
    19→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN = "scan"
    20→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW = "review"
    21→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW = "workflow"
    22→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE = "triage"
    23→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE = "execute"
    24→VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES = frozenset(
    25→    {
    26→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN,
    27→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    28→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
    29→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    30→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE,
    31→    }
    32→)
    33→
    34→
    35→def _refresh_state(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, object]:
    36→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    37→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    38→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
    39→        refresh_state = {}
    40→        plan["refresh_state"] = refresh_state
    41→    return refresh_state
    42→
    43→
    44→def _is_real_queue_issue(issue_id: str) -> bool:
    45→    return not any(str(issue_id).startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
    46→
    47→
    48→def current_lifecycle_phase(plan: PlanModel) -> str | None:
    49→    """Return the persisted lifecycle phase, falling back for legacy plans."""
    50→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    51→    if isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
    52→        phase = refresh_state.get(_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY)
    53→        if isinstance(phase, str) and phase in VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES:
    54→            return phase
    55→    if postflight_scan_pending(plan):
    56→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
    57→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    58→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
    59→    return None
    60→
    61→
    62→def set_lifecycle_phase(plan: PlanModel, phase: str) -> bool:
    63→    """Persist the current queue lifecycle phase."""
    64→    if phase not in VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES:
    65→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported lifecycle phase: {phase}")
    66→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
    67→    if refresh_state.get(_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY) == phase:
    68→        return False
    69→    refresh_state[_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY] = phase
    70→    return True
    71→
    72→
    73→def sync_lifecycle_phase(
    74→    plan: PlanModel,
    75→    *,
    76→    has_initial_reviews: bool,
    77→    has_objective_backlog: bool,
    78→    has_postflight_review: bool,
    79→    has_postflight_workflow: bool,
    80→    has_triage: bool,
    81→    has_deferred: bool,
    82→) -> tuple[str, bool]:
    83→    """Resolve and persist the current lifecycle phase from queue-state facts."""
    84→    phase = resolve_lifecycle_phase(
    85→        plan,
    86→        has_initial_reviews=has_initial_reviews,
    87→        has_objective_backlog=has_objective_backlog,
    88→        has_postflight_review=has_postflight_review,
    89→        has_postflight_workflow=has_postflight_workflow,
    90→        has_triage=has_triage,
    91→        has_deferred=has_deferred,
    92→    )
    93→    return phase, set_lifecycle_phase(plan, phase)
    94→
    95→
    96→def resolve_lifecycle_phase(
    97→    plan: PlanModel,
    98→    *,
    99→    has_initial_reviews: bool,
   100→    has_objective_backlog: bool,
   101→    has_postflight_review: bool,
   102→    has_postflight_workflow: bool,
   103→    has_triage: bool,
   104→    has_deferred: bool,
   105→) -> str:
   106→    """Resolve the lifecycle phase from explicit queue-state facts."""
   107→    if has_initial_reviews:
   108→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW
   109→    if has_objective_backlog:
   110→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   111→    if has_deferred or postflight_scan_pending(plan):
   112→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
   113→    if has_postflight_review:
   114→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW
   115→    if has_postflight_workflow:
   116→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW
   117→    if has_triage:
   118→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE
   119→    persisted = current_lifecycle_phase(plan)
   120→    if persisted is not None:
   121→        return persisted
   122→    return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
   123→
   124→
   125→def postflight_scan_pending(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
   126→    """Return True when the current empty-queue boundary still needs a scan."""
   127→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
   128→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
   129→        return True
   130→    return not isinstance(refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY), int)
   131→
   132→
   133→def mark_postflight_scan_completed(
   134→    plan: PlanModel,
   135→    *,
   136→    scan_count: int | None,
   137→) -> bool:
   138→    """Record that the scan stage completed for the current refresh cycle."""
   139→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   140→    try:
   141→        normalized_scan_count = int(scan_count or 0)
   142→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   143→        normalized_scan_count = 0
   144→    if refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY) == normalized_scan_count:
   145→        return False
   146→    refresh_state[_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY] = normalized_scan_count
   147→    return True
   148→
   149→
   150→def clear_postflight_scan_completion(
   151→    plan: PlanModel,
   152→    *,
   153→    issue_ids: Iterable[str] | None = None,
   154→) -> bool:
   155→    """Require a fresh scan after queue-changing work on real issues."""
   156→    if issue_ids is not None and not any(
   157→        _is_real_queue_issue(issue_id) for issue_id in issue_ids
   158→    ):
   159→        return False
   160→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   161→    if _POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY not in refresh_state:
   162→        return False
   163→    refresh_state[_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY] = LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   164→    refresh_state.pop(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY, None)
   165→    return True
   166→
   167→
   168→__all__ = [
   169→    "clear_postflight_scan_completion",
   170→    "current_lifecycle_phase",
   171→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE",
   172→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW",
   173→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN",
   174→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE",
   175→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW",
   176→    "mark_postflight_scan_completed",

   182→]

1→"""Skip/unskip mutations for plan operations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations.lifecycle import clear_focus_if_cluster_empty
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations.queue import _remove_id_from_lists
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SkipEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
    11→from desloppify.engine._plan.skip_policy import skip_kind_needs_state_reopen
    12→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    13→
    14→
    15→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    16→class SkipOptions:
    17→    """Optional fields used when skipping issue IDs."""
    18→
    19→    kind: str = "temporary"
    20→    reason: str | None = None
    21→    note: str | None = None
    22→    attestation: str | None = None
    23→    review_after: int | None = None
    24→    scan_count: int = 0
    25→
    26→
    27→def _skip_options(
    28→    *,
    29→    options: SkipOptions | None,
    30→    legacy_kwargs: dict[str, object],
    31→) -> SkipOptions:
    32→    """Resolve skip options from a config object or legacy kwargs."""
    33→    allowed_keys = {
    34→        "kind",
    35→        "reason",
    36→        "note",
    37→        "attestation",
    38→        "review_after",
    39→        "scan_count",
    40→    }
    41→    unknown = sorted(set(legacy_kwargs) - allowed_keys)
    42→    if unknown:
    43→        joined = ", ".join(unknown)
    44→        raise TypeError(f"Unexpected keyword argument(s): {joined}")
    45→    if options is not None and legacy_kwargs:
    46→        raise TypeError("Pass either options=... or legacy keyword args, not both")
    47→    if options is not None:
    48→        return options
    49→    kind = legacy_kwargs.get("kind")
    50→    reason = legacy_kwargs.get("reason")
    51→    note = legacy_kwargs.get("note")
    52→    attestation = legacy_kwargs.get("attestation")
    53→    review_after = legacy_kwargs.get("review_after")
    54→    scan_count = legacy_kwargs.get("scan_count")
    55→    return SkipOptions(
    56→        kind=str(kind) if kind is not None else "temporary",
    57→        reason=None if reason is None else str(reason),
    58→        note=None if note is None else str(note),
    59→        attestation=None if attestation is None else str(attestation),
    60→        review_after=int(review_after) if isinstance(review_after, int) else None,
    61→        scan_count=int(scan_count) if isinstance(scan_count, int) else 0,
    62→    )
    63→
    64→
    65→def skip_items(
    66→    plan: PlanModel,
    67→    issue_ids: list[str],
    68→    *,
    69→    options: SkipOptions | None = None,
    70→    **legacy_kwargs,
    71→) -> int:
    72→    """Move issue IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
    73→    skip_options = _skip_options(
    74→        options=options,
    75→        legacy_kwargs=legacy_kwargs,
    76→    )
    77→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    78→    now = utc_now()
    79→    count = 0
    80→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
    81→    skip_set = set(issue_ids)
    82→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, skip_set)
    83→    for fid in issue_ids:
    84→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
    85→        skipped[fid] = {
    86→            "issue_id": fid,
    87→            "kind": skip_options.kind,
    88→            "reason": skip_options.reason,
    89→            "note": skip_options.note,
    90→            "attestation": skip_options.attestation,
    91→            "created_at": now,
    92→            "review_after": skip_options.review_after,
    93→            "skipped_at_scan": skip_options.scan_count,
    94→        }
    95→        count += 1
    96→    clear_focus_if_cluster_empty(plan)
    97→    return count
    98→
    99→
   100→def _is_protected_skip(entry: SkipEntry) -> bool:
   101→    """A skip is protected if it was permanent/false_positive AND has a note.
   102→
   103→    Protected skips represent deliberate human judgment and should not be
   104→    silently undone by bulk unskip operations.
   105→    """
   106→    kind = str(entry.get("kind", ""))
   107→    has_note = bool(entry.get("note", ""))
   108→    return kind in ("permanent", "false_positive") and has_note
   109→
   110→
   111→def unskip_items(
   112→    plan: PlanModel,
   113→    issue_ids: list[str],
   114→    *,
   115→    include_protected: bool = False,
   116→) -> tuple[int, list[str], list[str]]:
   117→    """Bring issue IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   118→
   119→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen, protected_ids_kept)``
   120→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   121→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller, and the third
   122→    list contains protected IDs that were NOT unskipped (unless
   123→    ``include_protected=True``).
   124→    """
   125→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   126→    count = 0
   127→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   128→    protected_kept: list[str] = []
   129→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   130→    for fid in issue_ids:
   131→        entry = skipped.get(fid)
   132→        if entry is None:
   133→            continue
   134→        if not include_protected and _is_protected_skip(entry):
   135→            protected_kept.append(fid)
   136→            continue
   137→        skipped.pop(fid)
   138→        if skip_kind_needs_state_reopen(str(entry.get("kind", ""))):
   139→            need_reopen.append(fid)
   140→        if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   141→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   142→        count += 1
   143→    return count, need_reopen, protected_kept
   144→
   145→
   146→def resurface_stale_skips(
   147→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   148→) -> list[str]:
   149→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   150→
   151→    Returns list of resurfaced issue IDs.
   152→    """
   153→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   154→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   155→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   156→    for fid in list(skipped):
   157→        entry = skipped[fid]
   158→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   159→            continue
   160→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   161→        if review_after is None:
   162→            continue
   163→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   164→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   165→            skipped.pop(fid)
   166→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   167→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   168→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   169→    return resurfaced
   170→
   171→
   172→__all__ = ["resurface_stale_skips", "skip_items", "unskip_items"]
   173→

1→"""Shared helpers for queue-sync decisions.
     2→
     3→Every sync module (``sync_triage``, ``sync_dimensions``, ``sync_workflow``,
     4→``auto_cluster_sync``) needs to answer two questions:
     5→
     6→1. **Is there an objective backlog?**  (Should we defer optional items?)
     7→2. **Are we mid-cycle?**  (Has a queue cycle started and not yet completed?)
     8→
     9→This module provides a single definition of each so the logic stays
    10→consistent and changes propagate everywhere at once.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    17→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS as _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    20→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→def has_objective_backlog(
    26→    state_or_issues: StateModel | dict,
    27→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
    28→) -> bool:
    29→    """Return whether an objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues).
    30→
    31→    Prefers the pre-computed *policy* snapshot when available.
    32→    Falls back to scanning *state_or_issues* directly — accepts either a
    33→    full ``StateModel`` (looks up ``issues``) or a raw issues dict.
    34→    """
    35→    if policy is not None:
    36→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
    37→
    38→    # Accept either full state payload (`{"issues": ...}`) or raw issues dict.
    39→    issues = state_or_issues
    40→    if isinstance(state_or_issues, dict):
    41→        maybe_issues = state_or_issues.get("issues")
    42→        if isinstance(maybe_issues, dict):
    43→            issues = maybe_issues
    44→    return any(
    45→        f.get("status") == "open"
    46→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    47→        and not f.get("suppressed")
    48→        for f in issues.values()
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def is_mid_cycle(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    53→    """True when a queue cycle is in progress.
    54→
    55→    A cycle is active when ``plan_start_scores`` is set to real score
    56→    values.  The ``{"reset": True}`` sentinel (set by lifecycle reset)
    57→    does NOT count as mid-cycle — it means "seed real scores on next scan".
    58→    """
    59→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    60→    return bool(scores) and not (isinstance(scores, dict) and scores.get("reset"))
    61→
    62→
    63→__all__ = ["has_objective_backlog", "is_mid_cycle"]
    64→
```

Good. Now I have a complete picture. Let me produce the implementation plan.

---

## Implementation Plan

### Change 1: Unplanned objective items should NOT block postflight

**Problem**: In `snapshot.py`, `objective_items` includes ALL open non-skipped objective items (planned and unplanned). `_phase_for_snapshot()` checks `if objective_items:` which means unplanned items prevent phase transitions to SCAN/REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT/etc.

**Solution**: Split `objective_items` into `planned_objective_items` (used for phase determination) and keep the full list for visibility/counts.

**Definition of "planned"**: An item is planned if its ID appears in `tracked_ids`. Looking at `_tracked_plan_ids()` (lines 75-86 of snapshot.py), it returns IDs from `queue_order`, `skipped`, `overrides`, and `cluster.issue_ids`/`cluster.action_steps[].issue_refs`. Items in `skipped` are already excluded by `_is_objective_item` (via `skipped_ids` filter). So a "planned objective item" is one that passes `_is_objective_item` AND whose ID is in `tracked_ids`.

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/snapshot.py`**

1. After computing `objective_items` (line 238-241), add:
```python
planned_objective_items = [
    item for item in objective_items
    if item.get("id", "") in tracked_ids
]
```

2. Pass `planned_objective_items` (not `objective_items`) to `_phase_for_snapshot()` at line 257:
```python
phase = _phase_for_snapshot(
    ...
    objective_items=planned_objective_items,
    ...
)
```

3. Also pass `planned_objective_items` to `_execution_items_for_phase()` at line 264. When the phase is EXECUTE, the execution items should be only the planned ones. The unplanned items go into backlog.

4. Keep `all_objective_items=tuple(objective_items)` in the QueueSnapshot (line 290) so the full set remains visible for display and counting.

5. Update `objective_in_scope_count` (line 298) -- this should remain `len(objective_items)` since it counts all in-scope items for display purposes.

6. The `has_unplanned_objective_blockers` flag (line 284-286) already correctly identifies unplanned items. No change needed.

**Impact on preflight.py**: The preflight gate at line 130 uses `ctx.snapshot.objective_in_scope_count` for the blocker count. This still includes unplanned items, which is correct for the review rerun gate -- you don't want to rerun reviews while any objective issues exist. No change needed here.

**Impact on `refresh_lifecycle.py`**: `resolve_lifecycle_phase` takes `has_objective_backlog` as a boolean parameter. Callers compute this from `sync/context.py:has_objective_backlog()` which checks ALL open objective issues in state (not just planned). This is a separate code path from the snapshot phase waterfall, but the semantics should be consistent. However, `resolve_lifecycle_phase` is the *persisted* phase; `_phase_for_snapshot` is the *runtime* phase. Since `_phase_for_snapshot` already re-resolves from items as a safety net, the persisted phase is less critical. The sync callers that pass `has_objective_backlog` should ideally be updated too, but that is a larger change and the persisted phase is a fallback. Leave it for now.

### Change 2: Add "postpone" option to deferred disposition

**New operation**: `postpone_items()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations/skip.py`

```python
def postpone_items(
    plan: PlanModel,
    issue_ids: list[str],
) -> int:
    """Remove issue IDs from plan.skipped without adding to queue_order.

    The issues remain open in state but are no longer tracked by the plan.
    Returns count of items postponed.
    """
    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    count = 0
    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
    for fid in issue_ids:
        if fid in skipped:
            skipped.pop(fid)
            # Do NOT add to queue_order -- that's the key difference from unskip
            count += 1
    return count
```

**Export chain**:
- Add `postpone_items` to `__all__` in `skip.py`
- Add import and re-export in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/plan_ops.py`

**CLI command**: Add `cmd_plan_postpone` to `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_skip.py`. This is simpler than skip/unskip -- no attestation needed, no state changes needed (the issue stays open in state). Pattern:

```python
def cmd_plan_postpone(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Postpone deferred items to backlog — remove from plan tracking."""
    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    state = runtime.state
    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
        return

    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    state_file = runtime.state_path
    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
    if not issue_ids:
        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
        return

    count = postpone_items(plan, issue_ids)
    append_log_entry(
        plan,
        "postpone",
        issue_ids=issue_ids,
        actor="user",
    )
    clear_postflight_scan_completion(plan, issue_ids=issue_ids)
    save_plan(plan, plan_file)
    print(colorize(f"  Postponed {count} item(s) to backlog.", "green"))
```

**Register CLI subcommand**: Add to the plan subparser registration (in the CLI wiring -- likely in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override/__init__.py` or wherever `unskip` is registered). Also export from `override/__init__.py`.

**Update deferred disposition builder** in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/synthetic_workflow.py`:

In `build_deferred_disposition_item()`, add a third `decision_options` entry and update `planning_tools`:

```python
postpone_cmd = 'desloppify plan postpone "*"'
subset_postpone_cmd = "desloppify plan postpone <cluster-or-id>"
```

Add to `planning_tools`:
```python
{
    "label": "Postpone a subset to backlog",
    "command": subset_postpone_cmd,
},
```

Add to `decision_options`:
```python
{
    "label": "Postpone deferred work to backlog",
    "command": postpone_cmd,
},
```

### Change 3: Terminology cleanup

In `build_deferred_disposition_item()` (synthetic_workflow.py), update the summary text and decision option labels:

- Summary: Keep "Deferred backlog decision required:" prefix. Update the suffix from "must be reactivated or marked wontfix" to "must be reactivated, postponed, or marked wontfix."
- Decision options labels:
  - "Reactivate deferred work" (keep as-is)
  - "Postpone deferred work to backlog" (new)
  - "Convert deferred work to wontfix" (keep as-is)

### Test Updates

**`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/review/test_work_queue_synthetic_workflow_direct.py`**:
- Update `test_build_deferred_disposition_item_with_temporary_skips`: `decision_options` now has 3 entries, `planning_tools` now has 5 entries. Update the summary assertion to include "postponed".

**New test for `postpone_items`** in the appropriate test file (likely alongside skip/unskip tests):
- Test that postpone removes from `skipped` but does NOT add to `queue_order`
- Test that postponing an ID not in skipped is a no-op

**New test for snapshot phase with unplanned items**:
- Test in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/review/test_work_queue_plan_order_and_triage.py` (or a new focused test):
  - Build state with objective issues, some tracked by plan and some not
  - When all planned items are resolved but unplanned items remain, snapshot.phase should NOT be PHASE_EXECUTE
  - `has_unplanned_objective_blockers` should be True
  - `objective_in_scope_count` should include all items

### Sequencing

1. **Change 1 first** (snapshot.py) -- it's self-contained, only touches one file, and has no API additions
2. **Change 2** (postpone_items operation + CLI + disposition builder) -- builds on understanding from Change 1
3. **Change 3** (terminology) -- trivially merged with Change 2 since it touches the same function

### Potential Risks

- **preflight.py** uses `objective_in_scope_count` to block review reruns. After Change 1, unplanned items still count here, which is correct -- but worth a test to confirm the behavior is intentional.
- **`sync/context.py:has_objective_backlog()`** remains a separate code path that checks ALL open objective issues. This means the persisted lifecycle phase (via `sync_lifecycle_phase`) might still show "execute" when only unplanned items exist. This is tolerable because the runtime phase (`_phase_for_snapshot`) is the one that actually determines queue behavior. A follow-up could align the persisted phase logic, but it is not required for correctness.
- **`postpone_items` and the validate_plan invariant**: `validate_plan()` checks that no ID appears in both `queue_order` and `skipped`. Postpone only removes from `skipped` without adding to `queue_order`, so this invariant is preserved.

### Critical Files for Implementation
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/snapshot.py` - Core phase logic: split objective_items into planned/unplanned for phase determination
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/synthetic_workflow.py` - Add postpone option to deferred disposition builder and terminology updates
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations/skip.py` - Add new `postpone_items()` operation alongside existing skip/unskip
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_skip.py` - Add `cmd_plan_postpone` CLI handler
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/plan_ops.py` - Re-export `postpone_items` through the public facade
